- kind: parameter
  text: Quoted prices must be structured according to Schedule-A (percentage rate over BMRCL estimates), Schedule-B
    (KPWD schedule of rates at par), and Schedule-C (lump sum credit to BMRCL for salvageable items).
  evidence_ids:
  - Jfaf820f2f48dbba8
  - S221c78cbe87f11d4
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation
  from_id: ''
  to_id: ''
  id: C423a5929e125826b
  citations:
  - id: Jfaf820f2f48dbba8
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secB.md
    okf_sha256: e939aa5d764ea69ebc90821e3739717055c6fc9a79f67e3ed96823ded78b04f9
    heading: Clause 10.0 — Tender Price
    source_document: Vol1DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: 74441e621d2e627d9132506a894883aff3b5fe3fdfa9600f5be3c7ec394d5c5c
    pdf_pages:
    - 14
    - 15
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol1/section_b_itt/itt_clauses.json
    json_sha256: 5cbf8e397b40de4d6a1dfcd47405f4c93e0487e11869bb7807e45ebef78c56bf
    json_pointer: /clauses/9
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"10.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"text\": \"\
      10.0     TENDER PRICE\\n\\n         10.1          (i)  The provisions  of the Tender Documents, as specified\
      \ under Clause 5.1  of the\\n                          Instructions to Tenderers (ITT), govern the Quoted\
      \ Tender Prices.\\n\\n                      Tenderers shall download the Price Bid file (.xls format) from\
      \ the eProcurement Portal\\n                    and fill in only the coloured (unprotected) cells by entering\
      \ their name and financial offer.\\n                      For Schedule-A, tenderer shall select the applicable\
      \ option “At Par / Excess / Less” and\\n                        enter percentage rate in figures, for Schedule-B\
      \ - ‘Items under schedule of rates published\\n                     by KPWD’, tenderer is required to select\
      \ percentage option as ‘At Par’ (as the items\\n                      operated under this schedule shall be\
      \ paid as per the rates of KPWD SoR) and for\\n                     Schedule-C tenderer shall offer Credit\
      \ to BMRCL for Salvageable Items, in ‘Lump sum’\\n                     amount. The Tenderer’s quoted price\
      \ shall be (Total Amount of Schedule  - A and\\n                     Schedule – B, calculated based on Tenderer’s\
      \ quote) – (Tenderer’s quoted ‘Lump sum\\n                      amount’ for Schedule-C) in Price Bid - Summary\
      \ of BoQ.\\n\\n                                  (ii)  After entering the required details, the Tenderer shall\
      \ validate the Price Bid file (.xls), save\\n                                             it without changing\
      \ the original file name, and upload the validated file under the “Financial\\n                      Cover”\
      \ on the eProcurement Portal.\\n\\n         10.2          (i)  The Tendered or Quoted Price includes all applicable\
      \ taxes, duties, cess, royalties, and\\n                          statutory levies as per the laws in force\
      \ as on last day of the month previous to the month\\n                           of invitation of this tender,\
      \ except Goods and Services Tax (GST). BMRCL will reimburse\\n                GST to the Contractor based\
      \ on the output GST levied on works contract services\\n                       corresponding to the taxable\
      \ value of the works executed and duly remitted by the\\n                         Contractor. BMRCL will make\
      \ this reimbursement upon receipt of a valid GST-compliant\\n                         tax invoice submitted\
      \ along with the corresponding Interim Payment Certificates (IPC)\\n                       and/ or Final Payment\
      \ Certificate (FPC).\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 15,\n      \"text\": \"(ii) The Quoted Price (exclusive\
      \ of GST) remains firm and binding for the entire duration of\\n                 the Contract, including any\
      \ extensions, and does not vary due to input cost fluctuations\\n                  or changes in tax laws\
      \ or tax rates.\\n\\n  10.3     The Tenderer shall keep the contents of his Tender and the amount quoted by\
      \ him\\n               confidential.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"10.0     TENDER PRICE\",\n  \"\
      review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\"\n}"

- kind: parameter
  text: The Quoted Price includes all applicable taxes, duties, cess, royalties, and statutory levies in force as
    of the last day of the month prior to tender invitation, except for GST, which BMRCL reimburses separately against
    compliant tax invoices.
  evidence_ids:

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"10.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 14,\n      \"text\": \"\
      10.0     TENDER PRICE\\n\\n         10.1          (i)  The provisions  of the Tender Documents, as specified\
      \ under Clause 5.1  of the\\n                          Instructions to Tenderers (ITT), govern the Quoted\
      \ Tender Prices.\\n\\n                      Tenderers shall download the Price Bid file (.xls format) from\
      \ the eProcurement Portal\\n                    and fill in only the coloured (unprotected) cells by entering\
      \ their name and financial offer.\\n                      For Schedule-A, tenderer shall select the applicable\
      \ option “At Par / Excess / Less” and\\n                        enter percentage rate in figures, for Schedule-B\
      \ - ‘Items under schedule of rates published\\n                     by KPWD’, tenderer is required to select\
      \ percentage option as ‘At Par’ (as the items\\n                      operated under this schedule shall be\
      \ paid as per the rates of KPWD SoR) and for\\n                     Schedule-C tenderer shall offer Credit\
      \ to BMRCL for Salvageable Items, in ‘Lump sum’\\n                     amount. The Tenderer’s quoted price\
      \ shall be (Total Amount of Schedule  - A and\\n                     Schedule – B, calculated based on Tenderer’s\
      \ quote) – (Tenderer’s quoted ‘Lump sum\\n                      amount’ for Schedule-C) in Price Bid - Summary\
      \ of BoQ.\\n\\n                                  (ii)  After entering the required details, the Tenderer shall\
      \ validate the Price Bid file (.xls), save\\n                                             it without changing\
      \ the original file name, and upload the validated file under the “Financial\\n                      Cover”\
      \ on the eProcurement Portal.\\n\\n         10.2          (i)  The Tendered or Quoted Price includes all applicable\
      \ taxes, duties, cess, royalties, and\\n                          statutory levies as per the laws in force\
      \ as on last day of the month previous to the month\\n                           of invitation of this tender,\
      \ except Goods and Services Tax (GST). BMRCL will reimburse\\n                GST to the Contractor based\
      \ on the output GST levied on works contract services\\n                       corresponding to the taxable\
      \ value of the works executed and duly remitted by the\\n                         Contractor. BMRCL will make\
      \ this reimbursement upon receipt of a valid GST-compliant\\n                         tax invoice submitted\
      \ along with the corresponding Interim Payment Certificates (IPC)\\n                       and/ or Final Payment\
      \ Certificate (FPC).\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 15,\n      \"text\": \"(ii) The Quoted Price (exclusive\
      \ of GST) remains firm and binding for the entire duration of\\n                 the Contract, including any\
      \ extensions, and does not vary due to input cost fluctuations\\n                  or changes in tax laws\
      \ or tax rates.\\n\\n  10.3     The Tenderer shall keep the contents of his Tender and the amount quoted by\
      \ him\\n               confidential.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"10.0     TENDER PRICE\",\n  \"\
      review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\"\n}"

- kind: parameter
  text: Tender Security (EMD) may be furnished as an irrevocable and unconditional Bank Guarantee (physical or e-BG),
    an Insurance Surety Bond, a Demand Draft, a Banker's Cheque, or via NEFT/RTGS payment to BMRCL's designated
    Bank of India account.
  evidence_ids:
  - J96e5227223f2186d
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation
  from_id: ''
  to_id: ''
  id: C7e85ab0ce636a76d
  citations:
  - id: J96e5227223f2186d
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secB.md
    okf_sha256: e939aa5d764ea69ebc90821e3739717055c6fc9a79f67e3ed96823ded78b04f9
    heading: Clause 13.0 — Tender Security (EMD)
    source_document: Vol1DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: 74441e621d2e627d9132506a894883aff3b5fe3fdfa9600f5be3c7ec394d5c5c
    pdf_pages:
    - 15
    - 16
    - 17
    - 18
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol1/section_b_itt/itt_clauses.json
    json_sha256: 5cbf8e397b40de4d6a1dfcd47405f4c93e0487e11869bb7807e45ebef78c56bf
    json_pointer: /clauses/12
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"13.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 15,\n      \"text\": \"\
      13.0     TENDER SECURITY (EMD)\\n\\n\\n  13.1      The Tenderer shall submit a Tender Security (Earnest Money\
      \ Deposit – EMD) for the amount\\n               specified in the Notice Inviting Tender (NIT). The Tender\
      \ Security shall be furnished in the\\n             form of an irrevocable and unconditional Bank Guarantee\
      \ (Physical or e-BG; Preferably e-\\n           BG) or an Insurance Surety Bond or Demand Draft or Banker’s\
      \ Cheque or online payment\\n            by NEFT/RTGS, in accordance with the provisions set out below.\\\
      n\\n                       i.     Bank Guarantee (Physical or e-BG):\\n\\n           The Bank Guarantee (Physical\
      \ or e-BG) shall be issued by an Indian Scheduled Bank or a\\n            Scheduled Foreign Bank (as defined\
      \ in Section 2(e) of the Reserve Bank of India Act, 1934),\\n              excluding Co-operative Banks. In\
      \ the case of a Foreign Bank having no branch in India, the\\n         BG (Physical or e-BG) shall be accompanied\
      \ by a counter-guarantee from a Scheduled\\n          Bank in India.\\n\\n           The Bank Guarantee (Physical\
      \ or e-BG) shall be payable in India in favour of Bangalore\\n            Metro Rail Corporation Limited (BMRCL)\
      \ and shall conform to the prescribed format provided\\n                 in Attachment–12 of the Letter of\
      \ Technical Bid.\\n\\n           The Bank Guarantee (Physical or e-BG) shall remain valid for a period of\
      \ sixty (60) days\\n           beyond the Tender Validity Period, including any extensions under ITT Sub-Clause\
      \ 12.2\\n            and shall have been issued prior to Tender submission deadline (in case of e-BG,  it\
      \ shall\\n              also be fully accessible on the NeSL portal prior to Tender submission deadline).\\\
      n\\n           The Bank that is issuing the BG shall be on the “Structured Financing Messaging System\\n \
      \           (SFMS)” platform. Separate advice of the BG shall be sent by the issuing Bank to the\\n      \
      \       Employer’s Bank through SFMS to ensure a secure BG. For “SFMS” the following details are\\n      \
      \         to be considered:\\n\\n                 Name: BANGALORE METRO RAIL CORPORATION LIMITED\\n      \
      \         Name of the Bank: BANK OF INDIA\\n                     Branch: CANTONMENT BRANCH, BENGALURU.\\n\
      \                    Account No. 840420110000760\\n                  RTGS/IFSC: BKID0008404\\n           Copy\
      \ of Bank Guarantee along with a copy of MT760COV (for Bank Guarantee message) /\\n          MT767COV (for\
      \ Bank Guarantee amendment message) report sent by the issuing Bank shall\\n            be submitted by the\
      \ Tenderer as a part of their Bidding documents on e-Procurement Portal.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 16,\n      \"text\": \"Banks shall use the following details for issuing e-BG on the NeSL Portal:\\n\\n\
      \                   PAN of BMRCL (Beneficiary): AAACB4881D\\n                            E-mail ID of Beneficiary:\
      \ yogish_nayak@bmrc.co.in\\n                         Tender Reference No.: (Tender number as mentioned in\
      \ NIT)\\n\\n                                   ii.     Insurance Surety Bond:\\n\\n                The Insurance\
      \ Surety Bond shall be issued by an Insurance Company authorised by the\\n                   Insurance Regulatory\
      \ and Development Authority of India (IRDAI) and shall be payable in\\n                      India in favour\
      \ of Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Limited (BMRCL).\\n\\n                                  It shall conform\
      \ to the prescribed format provided in Attachment–12A of the Letter of\\n                    Technical Bid\
      \ and shall remain valid for a period of sixty (60) days beyond the Tender\\n                     Validity\
      \ Period, including any extensions under ITT Sub-Clause 12.2.\\n\\n                The Insurance Surety Bond\
      \ shall be issued prior to the Tender submission deadline.\\n\\n                                   iii.  \
      \  Demand Draft or Banker’s Cheque:\\n\\n               Demand Draft/ Banker’s Cheque drawn prior to the Tender\
      \ submission deadline in favour of\\n                   Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Limited payable at\
      \ Bengaluru issued from a Scheduled/\\n                     Nationalised Bank in India.\\n\\n            \
      \              iv.    NEFT/RTGS:\\n\\n               NEFT/RTGS payment shall be made prior to the Tender submission\
      \ deadline, to the BMRCL\\n                   account given below:\\n\\n                      Name: BANGALORE\
      \ METRO RAIL CORPORATION LIMITED\\n                    Name of the Bank: BANK OF INDIA\\n                \
      \           Branch: CANTONMENT BRANCH, BENGALURU.\\n                          Account No. 840420110000760\\\
      n                        RTGS/IFSC: BKID0008404\\n\\n         13.1.1      In Case of Tender Security (EMD)\
      \ in the form of Physical Bank Guarantee or Insurance\\n                    Surety Bond or Demand Draft or\
      \ Banker’s Cheque, the Tenderer shall submit the original\\n                  Tender Security (EMD) to BMRCL\
      \ within the due date & time stipulated in Notice Inviting\\n                  Tender (NIT) (or as amended,\
      \ if applicable).\\n\\n         13.2       In case Joint Venture (JV) participation is permitted in this tender,\
      \ Tender Security (EMD) can\\n                 be furnished by the intended JV or any member of the JV.\\\
      n         13.3     As part of the tender submission, the Tenderer shall upload a copy of the Tender Security\\\
      n                (EMD) on e-Procurement Portal.\\n\\n                                           i.    For\
      \ the purpose of tender evaluation and all subsequent contractual proceedings, the\\n\\n                 \
      \            authentic and legally binding version of the Tender Security shall be:\\n\\n                \
      \               (a) The original Physical Bank Guarantee received by BMRCL; or\\n\\n                     \
      \          (b) The version electronically received by BMRCL through direct intimation from the\\n        \
      \               NeSL portal (in the case of an e-BG); or\\n\\n                                (c) The original\
      \ physical Insurance Surety Bond received by BMRCL; or\\n\\n                               (d) The original\
      \ Demand Draft / Banker’s Cheque received by BMRCL (subject to\\n                                   realisation\
      \ of amount in BMRCL account); or\\n\\n                               (e) The amount received  in the prescribed\
      \ BMRCL account vide NEFT/RTGS\\n                          payment made by the tenderer before the deadline\
      \ for submission of tender.\\n\\n                                            ii.    In the event of any discrepancy\
      \ between the uploaded copy and the authoritative\\n\\n                              version, the following\
      \ shall prevail:\\n\\n                               (a) For a Physical Bank Guarantee, the original physical\
      \ document received by\\n                       BMRCL.\\n\\n                               (b) For an e-BG,\
      \ the version confirmed by the NeSL portal.\\n\\n                                (c) For an Insurance Surety\
      \ Bond, the original physical document received by\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 17,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"BMRCL.\\n\\n                       (d) For a Demand Draft / Banker’s Cheque, the original physical document\
      \ received\\n                     by BMRCL.\\n\\n                       (e) For NEFT/RTGS payment made by\
      \ the tenderer, amount received in prescribed\\n                BMRCL account before the deadline for submission\
      \ of tender.\\n\\n   13.4     The Employer shall summarily reject the Tender in any of the following cases:\\\
      n\\n                           (i)   If the required Tender Security (EMD) is not effectively provided:\\\
      n\\n                       (a) For a Physical Bank Guarantee:  If the  original physical document as per\\\
      n                       requirement is not received by BMRCL within the due date & time stipulated in\\n \
      \                       Notice Inviting Tender (NIT) (or as amended, if applicable).\\n\\n               \
      \        (b) For an e-Bank Guarantee (e-BG):  If  it  is not accessible to BMRCL as per\\n               \
      \        requirement on the NeSL portal within the deadline for submission of tender\\n                  \
      \        stipulated in Notice Inviting Tender (NIT) (or as amended, if applicable).\\n\\n                \
      \        (c) For an Insurance Surety Bond:  If the  original physical document as per\\n                 \
      \      requirement is not received by BMRCL within the due date & time stipulated in\\n                  \
      \      Notice Inviting Tender (NIT) (or as amended, if applicable).\\n\\n                       (d) For a\
      \ Demand Draft / Banker’s Cheque: If the original physical document as per\\n                       requirement\
      \ is not received by BMRCL within the due date & time stipulated in\\n                        Notice Inviting\
      \ Tender (NIT) (or as amended, if applicable) and if the submitted\\n                  Demand Draft / Banker’s\
      \ Cheque is not realized in BMRCL account.\\n\\n                       (e) For a NEFT/RTGS: Required amount\
      \ is not received in the prescribed BMRCL\\n                       account before the deadline for submission\
      \ of tender.\\n\\n                             (ii)  If the Bank Guarantee (Physical or e-BG) or Insurance\
      \ Surety Bond or Demand Draft\\n\\n                      or Banker’s Cheque is issued after the tender submission\
      \ deadline.\\n\\n                              (iii)  If the Bank Guarantee (Physical or e-BG) is issued by\
      \ any bank other than an Indian\\n\\n                   Scheduled Bank or a Scheduled Foreign Bank as defined\
      \ under Section 2(e) of the\\n\\n                  Reserve Bank of India Act, 1934, read with the Second Schedule\
      \ thereto.\\n\\n                       (iv) If the Bank Guarantee (Physical or e-BG) is issued by a Co-operative\
      \ Bank.\\n\\n                     (v)  If the Insurance Surety Bond is issued by an insurance company not\
      \ authorized by\\n\\n                     the Insurance Regulatory and Development Authority of India (IRDAI).\\\
      n\\n                       (vi) If the furnished amount of the Tender Security (EMD) is less than the amount\\\
      n\\n                      specified in the Notice Inviting Tender (NIT).\\n\\n                         (vii)\
      \ If the validity period of the Bank Guarantee (Physical or e-BG) or Insurance Surety\\n\\n              \
      \   Bond is shorter than the Tender validity period required under Clause 12.0 of the\\n\\n              \
      \         Instructions to Tenderers (ITT).\\n\\n  13.4.1        If the validity of the Tender Security (Bank\
      \ Guarantee (Physical or e-BG) / Insurance Surety\\n            Bond) submitted by the Tenderer meets the\
      \ prescribed Tender Validity Period but does not\\n            conform to the required Tender Security Validity\
      \ Period, the Employer shall notify the\\n            Tenderer in writing through the eProcurement Portal\
      \ or by e-mail/post, directing submission\\n               of a compliant Bank Guarantee (Physical or e-BG)\
      \  / Insurance Surety Bond within fifteen (15)\\n             working days from the date of such notification.\\\
      n\\n              Failure of the Tenderer to furnish a compliant Tender Security within the stipulated period\
      \ shall\\n               result in the Tender being rejected and declared non-responsive.\\n\\n  13.4.2  \
      \  Where the Employer, in its sole discretion, extends the deadline for submission of Bids in\\n         \
      \   accordance  with ITT Clause  15.5,  after commencement  of  Bid submission on  the\\n            eProcurement\
      \ Portal, any Bid already submitted shall remain valid, subject to compliance\\n              with the revised\
      \ Tender Security Validity Period. In the event that the Tender Security (Bank\\n            Guarantee (Physical\
      \ or e-BG) / Insurance Surety Bond) submitted with such Bid does not\\n            conform to the revised\
      \ validity requirements,  if necessary, the Employer shall notify the\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 18,\n      \"text\": \"concerned Tenderer in writing, through the eProcurement Portal or by e-mail/post,\
      \ and\\n                     require submission of a compliant Bank Guarantee (Physical or e-BG) / Insurance\
      \ Surety\\n                Bond within fifteen (15) working days from the date of issuance of such notice.\\\
      n\\n                     Failure of the Tenderer to furnish a compliant Tender Security within the stipulated\
      \ period\\n                       shall result in the tender being rejected and declared non-responsive.\\\
      n\\n         13.5      Tender Securities (EMD) of the non-responsive or unsuccessful bidders will be returned\
      \ in\\n                  accordance with sub-clause 22.1.7 or 25.3 of Instruction to Tenderers (ITT) respectively\
      \ as\\n                    the case may be.\\n\\n         13.6     The Tender Security (EMD) of the Successful\
      \ Tenderer shall be returned upon the Tenderer\\n                    executing the Contract Agreement after\
      \ furnishing the required Performance guarantee as\\n                   per the Contract.\\n\\n         13.7\
      \     The Tender Security (EMD) shall be forfeited;\\n\\n                       (a)       if a Tenderer withdraws\
      \ his tender during the period of tender validity;\\n\\n                       (b)       if the tenderer does\
      \ not accept the correction of his tendered price in terms of Clause\\n                          22.2.5 of\
      \ Instructions to Tenderers (ITT);\\n\\n                        (c)       if a tenderer misrepresents or omits\
      \ the facts to influence the procurement process.\\n\\n                       (d)    in the case of a successful\
      \ tenderer if he fails to;\\n\\n                                         (i)    Furnish the necessary Performance\
      \ guarantee.\\n\\n                                            (ii)    Enter into Contract within the time\
      \ limit specified.\\n\\n                       (e)    In case Joint Venture (JV) participation is permitted\
      \ in this tender and if any, JV member/s\\n                          withdraws from the JV after submission\
      \ of the tender.\\n\\n         13.8     No interest will be payable by the Employer on the Tender Security\
      \ amount.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"13.0     TENDER SECURITY (EMD)\",\n  \"review_status\": \"\
      pending_manual_review\"\n}"

- kind: parameter
  text: Additional Performance Security is required for abnormally low bids where the quoted price for Schedule
    A and Schedule B combined is lower by more than 10% of the total estimated cost.
  evidence_ids:

    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"22.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 21,\n      \"text\": \"\
      22.0     EVALUATION OF TENDER\\n\\n  22.1      Evaluation of Technical bids\\n\\n  22.1.1    The Employer\
      \ will carry out the technical evaluation of Tender submittals/ documents of all\\n              online bids\
      \ accepted by the Employer on the eProcurement Portal to determine that the\\n            Tenderer has a full\
      \ comprehension of the work of the contract. Where a Tenderer’s Technical\\n              submittal is found\
      \ to contain any material deviation or reservation with the requirement of\\n             work, the tender\
      \ may be rejected.\\n\\n  22.1.2     Evaluation of bids involves verification of payment of requisite Tender\
      \ Fees.\\n\\n  22.1.3    The evaluation of the Technical Bid includes verification of the Tender Security\
      \ to ensure it is\\n            adequate and fully compliant with Clause 13.0 of the Instructions to Tenderers\
      \ (ITT).\\n\\n  22.1.4     Further Technical bids will be evaluated for Eligibility cum Qualification Criteria\
      \ (EQC). If the\\n            Tenderer fails to meet the requirements of EQC, then evaluation of other technical\
      \ parameters\\n                  will not be done, and financial bids of those Tenderers will not be opened.\\\
      n\\n  22.1.5     Tenderer’s Financial bid will be opened only if Technical bid meet the requirements of EQC\\\
      n           and is found to be substantially responsive to the requirements stipulated in the Tender\\n  \
      \          document. The date and time of opening of financial bid shall be informed online on the\\n    \
      \        eProcurement Portal to the Tenderers who are found to be substantially responsive.\\n\\n  22.1.6\
      \    The offers of Tenderers which have not been found to be substantially responsive to the\\n          \
      \   requirements of the Tender document and which have been determined as being not qualified\\n         \
      \       in the evaluation of technical bid, the financial bid(s) of such Tenderer(s) shall not be opened\\\
      n           on eProcurement Portal.\\n\\n  22.1.7    Tender Securities (EMDs) of nonresponsive bidders (whose\
      \ financial bids are not opened)\\n               shall be returned  after  finalization of technical evaluation\
      \ and updating the same on\\n            eProcurement Portal.\\n\\n  22.2      Evaluation of Financial bid\\\
      n\\n  22.2.1    The Financial Bids of all Technically qualified and substantially responsive Tenderers shall\\\
      n           be opened online on the eProcurement Portal by the Employer on the due date and time of\\n   \
      \         opening notified under Clause 22.1.5 of ITT. The Financial bids of all such Tenderers shall\\n \
      \          be downloaded by the Employer from the eProcurement Portal for office records and further\\n  \
      \             financial evaluation.\\n\\n  22.2.2     Offers, deviations, and other factors that are more\
      \ than the requirements of the Tender\\n           documents or will otherwise result in the accrual of unsolicited\
      \ benefits to the Employer shall\\n             not be considered in the Tender evaluation.\\n\\n  22.2.3\
      \     Evaluation of the Financial bid will be based on the Price Bid summary of BOQ in the Pricing\\n    \
      \       Document, i.e. the Tenderer’s quoted price which shall be (Total Amount of Schedule - A and\"\n  \
      \  },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 22,\n      \"text\": \"Schedule – B, calculated based on Tenderer’s quote)\
      \ – (Tenderer’s quoted ‘Lump sum\\n                  amount’ for Schedule-C). Any alteration in Price Bid\
      \ will not be given any cognizance. The\\n                 amount quoted by Tenderers must be found to be\
      \ reasonable and acceptable.\\n\\n         22.2.4    The evaluation of Financial Bids by the employer will\
      \ take into account, in addition to the\\n                  Tender amounts, the following factors.\\n\\n \
      \                       (a)   Arithmetical errors corrected by the Employer as per Clause 22.2.5 of Instructions\
      \ to\\n                        Tenderers (ITT).\\n\\n                        (b)  Such other factors of an\
      \ administrative nature as the Employer may consider having a\\n                              potentially\
      \ significant impact on contract execution, price and payments, including the\\n                         \
      \     effect of items or unit rates that are unbalanced or unrealistically priced.\\n\\n         22.2.5  \
      \   Correction of Errors\\n\\n                      a)   Bidders are required to select option ‘At Par/ Excess\
      \ / Less’ and quote percentage rate\\n                                in figures for Schedule – A, select\
      \ option ‘At Par’ for Schedule - B  ‘Items under\\n                        schedule of rates published by\
      \ KPWD’ and shall quote ‘Lump sum’ amount for\\n                       Schedule - C.\\n\\n               \
      \       b)    In case the bidder selects the percentage option of ‘At Par’ and also quotes Percentage\\n \
      \                           rate in figures for Schedule – A, the selected Percentage option ‘At Par’ will\
      \ only govern\\n                      and percentage in figures will be ignored for evaluation of the bids.\\\
      n\\n                       c)    In case of discrepancy as per 22.2.5(b), the tenderer shall be bound to accept\
      \ the\\n                           evaluation based on ‘At Par’ rate. In case the tenderer does not accept,\
      \ his bid will be\\n                             rejected.\\n\\n         22.2.6    The effect of the price\
      \ adjustment provisions (if applicable for this tender) stipulated in the\\n                  General Conditions\
      \ of Contract (GCC), applicable during the execution period of the Contract,\\n                       shall\
      \ not be taken into account for the purpose of bid evaluation.\\n\\n         22.2.7     Conditional quotes\
      \ for any or all schedules of Price Bid may lead to rejection of Tender.\\n\\n         22.2.8      In case\
      \ of more than one L1 bidder, the tender may be awarded to a tenderer having a higher\\n                 \
      \   Bid Capacity. In case the Bid capacity is also the same, the tenderer having done higher value\\n    \
      \                  of similar work in the last three previous financial years and the current financial year\
      \ up to\\n                        last day of the month previous to the month of invitation of this tender,\
      \ may be selected for\\n                    the award.\\n\\n         22.3           If the lowest evaluated\
      \ Quoted Price for Schedule A & Schedule B of BoQ (combined total\\n                  amount)  is seriously\
      \ unbalanced, front-loaded, or abnormally low in the opinion of the\\n                   Employer, the Employer\
      \ may require the Tenderer to produce a detailed price analysis for\\n                 any or all schedules\
      \ of the Price Bid-BoQ to demonstrate the internal consistency of those\\n                     prices with\
      \ the work execution methods proposed. If after evaluating the price analyses, the\\n                  Employer\
      \ determines that the tenderer has substantially failed to demonstrate its capability\\n                 \
      \     to deliver the contract at the offered price, the employer may reject the Tender/bid.\\n\\n        \
      \              In case of bids with abnormally low quote for Schedule A & Schedule B of BoQ (combined\\n \
      \                        total amount), the Employer may require “Additional ‘Performance Security” over and\
      \ above\\n                    the ‘Performance Security’ outlined in Clause 27.1 of ITT and may be obtained\
      \ at the expense\\n                      of the successful Tenderer to a level sufficient to protect the Employer\
      \ against financial loss\\n                        in the event of default of the successful Tenderer under\
      \ the contract. If such an additional\\n                  Performance Security is required by the Employer,\
      \ the successful Tenderer shall furnish the\\n               same failing which his Tender shall be liable\
      \ to be rejected.\\n\\n                      “Additional Performance Security” is calculated as follows:\\\
      n\\n                                      (i)   If the Bid Price quoted for Schedule A & Schedule B of BoQ\
      \ (combined total amount)\\n                        by the Bidder is lower by more than 10% but up to 20%\
      \ of the total estimated cost of\\n                         Schedule A & Schedule B, then the additional Performance\
      \ Security shall be\\n                             calculated @ 10% of the difference in the (a) Combined\
      \ total Estimated cost of\\n                         Schedule A & Schedule B – 10% of the combined total estimated\
      \ cost of Schedule A\\n                   & Schedule B and (b) the Bid Price quoted by the Bidder for Schedule\
      \ A & Schedule\\n                   B (combined total amount).\\n\\n                                     \
      \   (ii)   If the Bid Price quoted for Schedule A & Schedule B of BoQ (combined total amount)\\n         \
      \               by the Bidder is lower by more than 20% of the combined total estimated cost of\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 23,\n      \"text\": \"Schedule A & Schedule B, then the additional Performance\
      \ Security shall be\\n                      calculated @ 15% of the difference in the (a) Combined total Estimated\
      \ cost of\\n                   Schedule A & Schedule B – 10% of the combined total amount estimated cost of\\\
      n                   Schedule A & Schedule B and (b) the bid price quoted by the Bidder for Schedule A\\n \
      \              & Schedule B.\\n\\n  22.4       Evaluation for Award of Multiple Contracts:\\n\\n  22.4.1 \
      \    Tenderers can participate in any One or Two or Three or All the four Tenders, issued under\\n       \
      \     Tender Notifications mentioned under clause 2.3 of ITT. However, Maximum  of Two\\n            Contracts\
      \ only will be awarded to a tenderer.\\n\\n                      If a Tenderer intends to participate in more\
      \ than one Tender and seeking for the award of\\n          Two Contracts, the Tenderer must demonstrate having\
      \ experience and resources to meet the\\n            aggregate of the prescribed qualifying criteria of individual\
      \ Tenders. Tenderer shall submit\\n              required documents, certificates and other credentials along\
      \ with his bid so as to qualify for\\n             the award of Two Contracts. Pursuant to Clause 22.1 & 22.2\
      \ of ITT, evaluation for award of\\n              Multiple Contracts shall be done as under:\\n\\n       \
      \         a.  In the first stage, the eligibility cum qualification of the Tenderer shall be evaluated\\n\
      \                  separately as per ITT Clause 22.1 for each individual Tender for which the Tenderer has\\\
      n                  submitted his Bid.\\n\\n                b.  In the second stage, the Tenderer whose bids\
      \ have been determined substantially\\n                  responsive and meeting the  eligibility cum qualification\
      \ requirements of individual\\n                  tenders in accordance with ITT Clause 22.1, the technical\
      \ evaluation of such Tenderers\\n              who have submitted Annexure-1A, will be carried out for the\
      \ aggregated requirements\\n                (combined requirements) of the following qualification criteria\
      \ of the respective tenders\\n                      for which he has submitted his bids and found substantially\
      \ responsive:\\n\\n                             (i) Similar Nature work requirements\\n\\n               \
      \                (ii) Liquidity\\n\\n                                (iii) Annual Turnover\\n\\n         \
      \                (iv) Bid Capacity\\n\\n  22.4.2         If a Tenderer is submitting one or more tenders mentioned\
      \ in ITB Clause 2.3 and seeking\\n           award of only One Contract, then he is not required to submit\
      \ Attachment-1A to Letter of\\n             Technical Bid and such bidder will be considered for evaluation\
      \ as per Clause 22.4.1(a) and\\n                  will not be consider for evaluation under 22.4.1(b).\\n\\\
      n                     If a Tenderer is participating in two or more tenders mentioned in ITB Clause 2.3 and\\\
      n            seeking award of Two Contracts, then he must submit Attachment-1A to Letter of\\n           \
      \ Technical Bid along with their bids without fail, otherwise such bidder will not be\\n            considered\
      \ for evaluation as per Clause 22.4.1(b).\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"22.0     EVALUATION OF TENDER\"\
      ,\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\"\n}"

    under GCC Sub-Clause 4.2 within the period specified in the Contract Data, using a bank guarantee or insurance
    surety bond.

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"27.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 24,\n      \"text\": \"\
      27.0    PERFORMANCE SECURITY\\n\\n         27.1     The successful Tenderer shall furnish to the Employer\
      \ a Performance Security and Additional\\n                  Performance      Security          (if      applicable)\
      \     under     Sub-Clause      4.2\\n                      of General Conditions of Contract within the period\
      \ as stated in the Contract Data of General\\n                    Conditions of Contract.\\n\\n         27.2\
      \     The successful tenderer shall furnish either (i) a Bank Guarantee (physical or e-BG) or (ii) an\\n \
      \                  Insurance Surety Bond towards the Performance Security, in the prescribed format provided\\\
      n                  under the Contract Forms (Form–2/2A).\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 25,\n     \
      \ \"text\": \"For issuance of e-Bank Guarantees, duly incorporate the beneficiary details specified under\\\
      n            Clause 13.0 of the Instructions to Tenderers (ITT).\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"27.0\
      \    PERFORMANCE SECURITY\",\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\"\n}"

  text: The liquidity requirement for Tender Package P1 is set at not less than ₹ 1.19 Crores, demonstrated through
    liquid assets and/or credit facilities.
  evidence_ids:

  - id: J704434795075ef24

    okf_sha256: 5ae5ea7a30496ffbb3384dfb998e3fdd89ef3222744b6b639e3d564508b11172
    heading: Clause 3.0 — Qualification Criteria
    source_document: Vol1DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: 74441e621d2e627d9132506a894883aff3b5fe3fdfa9600f5be3c7ec394d5c5c
    pdf_pages:

    json_path: parsed_json/vol1/section_c_eqc/eqc_clauses.json
    json_sha256: d8207622663bc2dd0cf2a7c5e3ed660f5c3881838e376d1bbb26fdae47ed8abf
    json_pointer: /clauses/2
    json_record_id: null

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"3.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 32,\n      \"text\": \"\
      3.0 \\nQUALIFICATION CRITERIA \\n \\nPursuant to clause 2.0, tenderers shall meet the qualification criteria\
      \ stipulated in the succeeding paragraphs in order to qualify. \\n3.1 WORK EXPERIENCE & FINANCIAL STANDING:\
      \ \\n3.1. A. WORK EXPERIENCE \\nSl. No \\nCriteria Requirement \\nDocument Submission \\nRequirement \\n3.1.A.(i)\
      \ \\nTenderer as a Prime Contractor (Single entity or Joint Venture member) during last 07(seven) \\nyears\
      \ ending as on last day of the month previous to the month of invitation of this tender, \\nshould have completed/\
      \ **substantially completed One work of *Similar nature, where the \\nvalue of the work is equal to or exceed\
      \ ₹ 3.79 Crores.*** \\nAttachment-5 & Attachment-\\n6 to Letter of Technical Bid \\nOR \\n3.1.A. (ii) \\nTenderer\
      \ as a Prime Contractor (Single entity or Joint Venture member) during last 07(seven) \\nyears ending as on\
      \ last day of the month previous to the month of invitation of this tender, \\nshould have completed/ **substantially\
      \ completed Two works of *Similar nature, where the \\nvalue of each work is equal to or exceed ₹ 2.37 Crores.\
      \ *** \\nAttachment-5 & Attachment-\\n6 to Letter of Technical Bid \\nOR \\n3.1.A. (iii) Tenderer as a Prime\
      \ Contractor (Single entity or Joint Venture member) during last 07(seven) \\nyears ending as on last day\
      \ of the month previous to the month of invitation of this tender, \\nshould have completed/ **substantially\
      \ completed Three works of *Similar nature, where the \\nvalue of each work is equal to or exceed ₹ 1.90 Crores.***\
      \ \\nAttachment-5 & Attachment-\\n6 to Letter of Technical Bid \\nOR \\n3.1.A. (iv) Tenderer as a Prime Contractor\
      \ (Single entity or Joint Venture member) during last 07(seven) \\nyears ending as on last day of the month\
      \ previous to the month of invitation of this tender, \\nAttachment-5 & Attachment-\\n6 to Letter of Technical\
      \ Bid\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 33,\n      \"text\": \"should have completed/ **substantially\
      \ completed in any number of works of *Similar nature, \\nwhere the combined value of works is equal to or\
      \ exceed ₹ 4.74 Crores.*** \\nNotes: \\n1) \\n* Similar nature work for this contract shall be, ‘Demolition/Dismantling\
      \ of Buildings/Structures’. \\n2) \\n** Similar nature work(s) where Financial value of physically executed\
      \ work is 90%  or more of the original awarded contract value or \\nrevised contract value (if any), duly\
      \ certified by the client, will be treated as substantially completed Similar nature work(s) and will be \\\
      nconsidered for qualification of work experience criteria. In case original contract value has been revised,\
      \ the revised contract value shall \\nbe considered for this purpose.  \\n3) \\nThe Tenderer shall furnish\
      \ the Client’s Completion Certificate in support of the Work Experience criteria as specified under Clause\
      \ 3.1A. \\nThe certificate shall clearly indicate the Nature and Scope of Similar Nature Work, Awarded Contract\
      \ Value (excluding GST), Actual \\nCompletion Value (excluding GST), and the Actual Date of Completion. Where\
      \ applicable, the certificate shall also specify the Revised \\nContract Value (excluding GST), along with\
      \ details of any variation orders and extensions of time granted. \\n4) \\nIn cases where the Client’s Completion\
      \ Certificate indicates values inclusive of GST and/or other applicable taxes, the Tenderer shall \\nsubmit\
      \ the actual completion cost after deducting the applicable GST dully certified by Chartered Accountant. For\
      \ portions of Similar \\nNature Works executed in the pre-GST period, the Tenderer shall make a deduction\
      \ towards taxes at the rate of 12% to arrive at the \\ncompletion cost exclusive of taxes. \\n5) \\nIf a Tenderer\
      \ has completed /substantially completed the Similar Nature Work(s) as a member of a Joint Venture (JV), the\
      \ value of \\n‘Similar Nature Work(s)’ will be recognized only in proportion to the Tenderer’s stipulated\
      \ financial share in that JV. A copy of the JV \\nAgreement must be submitted with the bid. \\n6) \\nFor the\
      \ purpose of evaluation, value of works in foreign currencies shall be converted to equivalent INR as per\
      \ the following procedure: \\nValue of completed/ substantially completed Similar Nature Work(s) in foreign\
      \ currency shall be converted to Equivalent INR by adopting \\nExchange rate between the foreign currency\
      \ and INR as prevailing on the date of completion / date given in the substantial completion \\ncertificate\
      \ as applicable, as per the website of Reserve Bank of India (RBI). \\n7) \\n***The value of Completed Similar\
      \ Nature Work(s) executed during the previous financial years shall be updated at a rate of 5% per \\nyear,\
      \ compounded annually, for the Indian Rupees portion (including foreign currencies, converted to Equivalent\
      \ INR), to bring them to \\nthe price level of the financial year of this Tender Invitation.\"\n    },\n \
      \   {\n      \"pdf_page\": 34,\n      \"text\": \"The value of ongoing work(s) that are substantially completed\
      \ shall not be updated to the price level of the financial year of this Tender \\nInvitation. \\n8) \\nIn\
      \ case the Similar Nature Work(s) are executed for client other than Government Department, PSU, or other\
      \ Government Body, Contract \\nAgreement, Bill wise details of payment received and Proof of TDS (Form 26AS)\
      \ deducted by clients for all payments received shall be \\nsubmitted duly certified by Chartered Accountant.\
      \ \\n9) \\nWork experience certificates of the tenderers with qualification as a sub-contractor will not be\
      \ considered. \\n3.1. B.  FINANCIAL STANDING: The Tenderer should meet the following financial qualification\
      \ criteria: \\nSl. No \\nCriteria Requirement \\nDocument Submission \\nrequirement \\n3.1.B (i) \\nLiquidity:\
      \ Tenderer shall demonstrate that it has access to, or possess liquid assets and /or \\navailability of credit\
      \ facilities of not less than ₹ 1.19 Crores (Credit lines/ letter of credit/ certificates \\nfrom banks for\
      \ meeting the fund requirement etc.). \\nAttachment-13 to Letter of \\nTechnical Bid \\n3.1.B (ii) \\nAnnual\
      \ Turnover: Tenderer in its name should have in the last five financial years (2021-2022 \\nto 2025-2026)\
      \ achieved in at least two financial years a minimum financial turnover (in all \\nclasses of civil engineering\
      \ construction works only) ≥ ₹ 9.48 Crores. **** \\nAttachment-8 to Letter of \\nTechnical Bid \\n      Notes:\
      \ \\n1) \\nA copy of the Banker’s Certificate issued from a Nationalised / Scheduled Bank in India (for liquidity)\
      \ needs to be uploaded in the \\nTechnical Proposal in respect of EQC Clause 3.1 B (i). \\n2) \\nAnnual financial\
      \ statements (Audited Balance sheets including all related notes and income statements) for last five financial\
      \ years \\n(2021-2022 to 2025-2026) shall be submitted by the Tenderer. The financial information of the Tenderer\
      \ must be certified either by the \\nIndependent Financial Auditor (statutory Auditor) of the company appointed\
      \ under the companies’ Act or by a Chartered Accountant with \\nhis stamp and signature with membership number\
      \ and firm registration number. \\n3) \\nIn case any discrepancy in data is found between the Audited Balance\
      \ sheet and the financial information submitted, the data as available \\nin the Audited Balance sheet will\
      \ be considered.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 35,\n      \"text\": \"4) \\nIn case Audited Balance\
      \ sheet of the last financial year ending with 2025-2026 is not made available by the Tenderer, he has to\
      \ submit \\nan affidavit certifying that 'The Balance Sheet has actually not been audited so far'. In such\
      \ a case the financial data of previous '4' \\naudited financial years will be taken into consideration for\
      \ evaluation. If audited Balance sheet of any year other than the last financial \\nyear ending with 2025-2026\
      \ is not submitted even when requested for as a clarification under clause 20 of ITT, then the Tender will\
      \ be \\nconsidered as non-responsive. \\n5) \\nAll documents shall reflect the stand-alone financial status\
      \ of the Tenderer and not the Tenderer’s parent companies, subsidiaries, or \\naffiliates. \\n6) \\nFor the\
      \ purpose of evaluation, amounts in foreign currencies shall be converted to equivalent INR as per the following\
      \ procedure: \\nIn cases where the financial data is denominated in a currency other than Indian Rupees, it\
      \ shall be converted into the equivalent value \\nin Indian Rupees by adopting the exchange rate between the\
      \ foreign currency and INR prevailing on the last day of the respective \\nfinancial year, as published on\
      \ the Reserve Bank of India (RBI) website, for the purpose of bid evaluation. \\n7) \\nTo align a January–December\
      \ financial year with the standard April–March fiscal year, use the following conversion: \\nA calendar year\
      \ (e.g., January–December 2023) corresponds to the subsequent fiscal year (e.g., FY 2023–24, covering April\
      \ 2023 to \\nMarch 2024). Apply this rule consistently to all such conversions. \\n8) \\n****Financial turnover\
      \ in INR/converted equivalent INR of previous financial years shall be given a weightage of 5% per year \\\
      n(compounded annually) to bring them to the price level of the Financial Year of this Tender Invitation. \\\
      n9) \\nAll the documents or certifications, which are provided by Chartered Accountant after 1st July, 2019,\
      \ must contain UDIN thereon and \\nthe particulars of certifications must be same as mentioned on document/certification\
      \ and submitted to ICAI on its website which can be \\nverified online on https://udin.icai.org/search-udin.\
      \ \\n3.2 BID CAPACITY: \\nRequirement \\nDocument Submission \\nRequirement \\nAvailable Bid capacity should\
      \ be ≥ ₹ 4.74 Crores \\nFormula: \\nAvailable Bid Capacity = (1.5 × A × N) – B  \\nAttachment-7, Attachment-\\\
      n8 to Letter of Technical Bid\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"text\": \"Requirement \\\
      nDocument Submission \\nRequirement \\nWhere: \\nA = Maximum value of total Civil Engineering works executed\
      \ (annual turnover from Civil Engineering works) \\nin any one year during the last five financial years.\
      \ The turnover for previous years shall be updated by 5% \\nper year (compounded annually) to bring them to\
      \ the price level of the Financial Year of this Tender Invitation.  \\nN = Number of years prescribed for\
      \ completion of this tendered work as mentioned in NIT.  \\nB = Total value (updated to the price level of\
      \ the Financial Year of this Tender Invitation) of existing \\ncommitments for ongoing Civil Engineering works\
      \ during the completion period mentioned in NIT, from the \\nlast day of the month previous to the month of\
      \ invitation of this tender. \\nNotes: \\n1) Works in Hand information provided in ‘Attachment-7 to Letter\
      \ of Technical Bid’ must be certified either by the Independent Financial \\nAuditor (Statutory Auditor) of\
      \ the company appointed under the companies’ Act or by a Chartered Accountant with his stamp and signature\
      \ \\nwith Membership Number and Firm’s Registration Number. \\n2) All the documents or certifications, which\
      \ are provided by Chartered Accountant after 1st July 2019, must contain UDIN thereon and the \\nparticulars\
      \ of certifications must be same as mentioned on document/certification and submitted to ICAI on its website,\
      \ which can be \\nverified online on https://udin.icai.org/search-udin.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"review_status\"\
      : \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"title\": \"Qualification Criteria\"\n}"

  - id: J704434795075ef24
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secC.md
    okf_sha256: 5ae5ea7a30496ffbb3384dfb998e3fdd89ef3222744b6b639e3d564508b11172
    heading: Clause 3.0 — Qualification Criteria
    source_document: Vol1DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: 74441e621d2e627d9132506a894883aff3b5fe3fdfa9600f5be3c7ec394d5c5c
    pdf_pages:
    - 32
    - 33
    - 34
    - 35
    - 36
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol1/section_c_eqc/eqc_clauses.json
    json_sha256: d8207622663bc2dd0cf2a7c5e3ed660f5c3881838e376d1bbb26fdae47ed8abf
    json_pointer: /clauses/2
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"3.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 32,\n      \"text\": \"\
      3.0 \\nQUALIFICATION CRITERIA \\n \\nPursuant to clause 2.0, tenderers shall meet the qualification criteria\
      \ stipulated in the succeeding paragraphs in order to qualify. \\n3.1 WORK EXPERIENCE & FINANCIAL STANDING:\
      \ \\n3.1. A. WORK EXPERIENCE \\nSl. No \\nCriteria Requirement \\nDocument Submission \\nRequirement \\n3.1.A.(i)\
      \ \\nTenderer as a Prime Contractor (Single entity or Joint Venture member) during last 07(seven) \\nyears\
      \ ending as on last day of the month previous to the month of invitation of this tender, \\nshould have completed/\
      \ **substantially completed One work of *Similar nature, where the \\nvalue of the work is equal to or exceed\
      \ ₹ 3.79 Crores.*** \\nAttachment-5 & Attachment-\\n6 to Letter of Technical Bid \\nOR \\n3.1.A. (ii) \\nTenderer\
      \ as a Prime Contractor (Single entity or Joint Venture member) during last 07(seven) \\nyears ending as on\
      \ last day of the month previous to the month of invitation of this tender, \\nshould have completed/ **substantially\
      \ completed Two works of *Similar nature, where the \\nvalue of each work is equal to or exceed ₹ 2.37 Crores.\
      \ *** \\nAttachment-5 & Attachment-\\n6 to Letter of Technical Bid \\nOR \\n3.1.A. (iii) Tenderer as a Prime\
      \ Contractor (Single entity or Joint Venture member) during last 07(seven) \\nyears ending as on last day\
      \ of the month previous to the month of invitation of this tender, \\nshould have completed/ **substantially\
      \ completed Three works of *Similar nature, where the \\nvalue of each work is equal to or exceed ₹ 1.90 Crores.***\
      \ \\nAttachment-5 & Attachment-\\n6 to Letter of Technical Bid \\nOR \\n3.1.A. (iv) Tenderer as a Prime Contractor\
      \ (Single entity or Joint Venture member) during last 07(seven) \\nyears ending as on last day of the month\
      \ previous to the month of invitation of this tender, \\nAttachment-5 & Attachment-\\n6 to Letter of Technical\
      \ Bid\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 33,\n      \"text\": \"should have completed/ **substantially\
      \ completed in any number of works of *Similar nature, \\nwhere the combined value of works is equal to or\
      \ exceed ₹ 4.74 Crores.*** \\nNotes: \\n1) \\n* Similar nature work for this contract shall be, ‘Demolition/Dismantling\
      \ of Buildings/Structures’. \\n2) \\n** Similar nature work(s) where Financial value of physically executed\
      \ work is 90%  or more of the original awarded contract value or \\nrevised contract value (if any), duly\
      \ certified by the client, will be treated as substantially completed Similar nature work(s) and will be \\\
      nconsidered for qualification of work experience criteria. In case original contract value has been revised,\
      \ the revised contract value shall \\nbe considered for this purpose.  \\n3) \\nThe Tenderer shall furnish\
      \ the Client’s Completion Certificate in support of the Work Experience criteria as specified under Clause\
      \ 3.1A. \\nThe certificate shall clearly indicate the Nature and Scope of Similar Nature Work, Awarded Contract\
      \ Value (excluding GST), Actual \\nCompletion Value (excluding GST), and the Actual Date of Completion. Where\
      \ applicable, the certificate shall also specify the Revised \\nContract Value (excluding GST), along with\
      \ details of any variation orders and extensions of time granted. \\n4) \\nIn cases where the Client’s Completion\
      \ Certificate indicates values inclusive of GST and/or other applicable taxes, the Tenderer shall \\nsubmit\
      \ the actual completion cost after deducting the applicable GST dully certified by Chartered Accountant. For\
      \ portions of Similar \\nNature Works executed in the pre-GST period, the Tenderer shall make a deduction\
      \ towards taxes at the rate of 12% to arrive at the \\ncompletion cost exclusive of taxes. \\n5) \\nIf a Tenderer\
      \ has completed /substantially completed the Similar Nature Work(s) as a member of a Joint Venture (JV), the\
      \ value of \\n‘Similar Nature Work(s)’ will be recognized only in proportion to the Tenderer’s stipulated\
      \ financial share in that JV. A copy of the JV \\nAgreement must be submitted with the bid. \\n6) \\nFor the\
      \ purpose of evaluation, value of works in foreign currencies shall be converted to equivalent INR as per\
      \ the following procedure: \\nValue of completed/ substantially completed Similar Nature Work(s) in foreign\
      \ currency shall be converted to Equivalent INR by adopting \\nExchange rate between the foreign currency\
      \ and INR as prevailing on the date of completion / date given in the substantial completion \\ncertificate\
      \ as applicable, as per the website of Reserve Bank of India (RBI). \\n7) \\n***The value of Completed Similar\
      \ Nature Work(s) executed during the previous financial years shall be updated at a rate of 5% per \\nyear,\
      \ compounded annually, for the Indian Rupees portion (including foreign currencies, converted to Equivalent\
      \ INR), to bring them to \\nthe price level of the financial year of this Tender Invitation.\"\n    },\n \
      \   {\n      \"pdf_page\": 34,\n      \"text\": \"The value of ongoing work(s) that are substantially completed\
      \ shall not be updated to the price level of the financial year of this Tender \\nInvitation. \\n8) \\nIn\
      \ case the Similar Nature Work(s) are executed for client other than Government Department, PSU, or other\
      \ Government Body, Contract \\nAgreement, Bill wise details of payment received and Proof of TDS (Form 26AS)\
      \ deducted by clients for all payments received shall be \\nsubmitted duly certified by Chartered Accountant.\
      \ \\n9) \\nWork experience certificates of the tenderers with qualification as a sub-contractor will not be\
      \ considered. \\n3.1. B.  FINANCIAL STANDING: The Tenderer should meet the following financial qualification\
      \ criteria: \\nSl. No \\nCriteria Requirement \\nDocument Submission \\nrequirement \\n3.1.B (i) \\nLiquidity:\
      \ Tenderer shall demonstrate that it has access to, or possess liquid assets and /or \\navailability of credit\
      \ facilities of not less than ₹ 1.19 Crores (Credit lines/ letter of credit/ certificates \\nfrom banks for\
      \ meeting the fund requirement etc.). \\nAttachment-13 to Letter of \\nTechnical Bid \\n3.1.B (ii) \\nAnnual\
      \ Turnover: Tenderer in its name should have in the last five financial years (2021-2022 \\nto 2025-2026)\
      \ achieved in at least two financial years a minimum financial turnover (in all \\nclasses of civil engineering\
      \ construction works only) ≥ ₹ 9.48 Crores. **** \\nAttachment-8 to Letter of \\nTechnical Bid \\n      Notes:\
      \ \\n1) \\nA copy of the Banker’s Certificate issued from a Nationalised / Scheduled Bank in India (for liquidity)\
      \ needs to be uploaded in the \\nTechnical Proposal in respect of EQC Clause 3.1 B (i). \\n2) \\nAnnual financial\
      \ statements (Audited Balance sheets including all related notes and income statements) for last five financial\
      \ years \\n(2021-2022 to 2025-2026) shall be submitted by the Tenderer. The financial information of the Tenderer\
      \ must be certified either by the \\nIndependent Financial Auditor (statutory Auditor) of the company appointed\
      \ under the companies’ Act or by a Chartered Accountant with \\nhis stamp and signature with membership number\
      \ and firm registration number. \\n3) \\nIn case any discrepancy in data is found between the Audited Balance\
      \ sheet and the financial information submitted, the data as available \\nin the Audited Balance sheet will\
      \ be considered.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 35,\n      \"text\": \"4) \\nIn case Audited Balance\
      \ sheet of the last financial year ending with 2025-2026 is not made available by the Tenderer, he has to\
      \ submit \\nan affidavit certifying that 'The Balance Sheet has actually not been audited so far'. In such\
      \ a case the financial data of previous '4' \\naudited financial years will be taken into consideration for\
      \ evaluation. If audited Balance sheet of any year other than the last financial \\nyear ending with 2025-2026\
      \ is not submitted even when requested for as a clarification under clause 20 of ITT, then the Tender will\
      \ be \\nconsidered as non-responsive. \\n5) \\nAll documents shall reflect the stand-alone financial status\
      \ of the Tenderer and not the Tenderer’s parent companies, subsidiaries, or \\naffiliates. \\n6) \\nFor the\
      \ purpose of evaluation, amounts in foreign currencies shall be converted to equivalent INR as per the following\
      \ procedure: \\nIn cases where the financial data is denominated in a currency other than Indian Rupees, it\
      \ shall be converted into the equivalent value \\nin Indian Rupees by adopting the exchange rate between the\
      \ foreign currency and INR prevailing on the last day of the respective \\nfinancial year, as published on\
      \ the Reserve Bank of India (RBI) website, for the purpose of bid evaluation. \\n7) \\nTo align a January–December\
      \ financial year with the standard April–March fiscal year, use the following conversion: \\nA calendar year\
      \ (e.g., January–December 2023) corresponds to the subsequent fiscal year (e.g., FY 2023–24, covering April\
      \ 2023 to \\nMarch 2024). Apply this rule consistently to all such conversions. \\n8) \\n****Financial turnover\
      \ in INR/converted equivalent INR of previous financial years shall be given a weightage of 5% per year \\\
      n(compounded annually) to bring them to the price level of the Financial Year of this Tender Invitation. \\\
      n9) \\nAll the documents or certifications, which are provided by Chartered Accountant after 1st July, 2019,\
      \ must contain UDIN thereon and \\nthe particulars of certifications must be same as mentioned on document/certification\
      \ and submitted to ICAI on its website which can be \\nverified online on https://udin.icai.org/search-udin.\
      \ \\n3.2 BID CAPACITY: \\nRequirement \\nDocument Submission \\nRequirement \\nAvailable Bid capacity should\
      \ be ≥ ₹ 4.74 Crores \\nFormula: \\nAvailable Bid Capacity = (1.5 × A × N) – B  \\nAttachment-7, Attachment-\\\
      n8 to Letter of Technical Bid\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"text\": \"Requirement \\\
      nDocument Submission \\nRequirement \\nWhere: \\nA = Maximum value of total Civil Engineering works executed\
      \ (annual turnover from Civil Engineering works) \\nin any one year during the last five financial years.\
      \ The turnover for previous years shall be updated by 5% \\nper year (compounded annually) to bring them to\
      \ the price level of the Financial Year of this Tender Invitation.  \\nN = Number of years prescribed for\
      \ completion of this tendered work as mentioned in NIT.  \\nB = Total value (updated to the price level of\
      \ the Financial Year of this Tender Invitation) of existing \\ncommitments for ongoing Civil Engineering works\
      \ during the completion period mentioned in NIT, from the \\nlast day of the month previous to the month of\
      \ invitation of this tender. \\nNotes: \\n1) Works in Hand information provided in ‘Attachment-7 to Letter\
      \ of Technical Bid’ must be certified either by the Independent Financial \\nAuditor (Statutory Auditor) of\
      \ the company appointed under the companies’ Act or by a Chartered Accountant with his stamp and signature\
      \ \\nwith Membership Number and Firm’s Registration Number. \\n2) All the documents or certifications, which\
      \ are provided by Chartered Accountant after 1st July 2019, must contain UDIN thereon and the \\nparticulars\
      \ of certifications must be same as mentioned on document/certification and submitted to ICAI on its website,\
      \ which can be \\nverified online on https://udin.icai.org/search-udin.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"review_status\"\
      : \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"title\": \"Qualification Criteria\"\n}"

      \ as applicable, as per the website of Reserve Bank of India (RBI). \\n7) \\n***The value of Completed Similar\
      \ Nature Work(s) executed during the previous financial years shall be updated at a rate of 5% per \\nyear,\
      \ compounded annually, for the Indian Rupees portion (including foreign currencies, converted to Equivalent\
      \ INR), to bring them to \\nthe price level of the financial year of this Tender Invitation.\"\n    },\n \
      \   {\n      \"pdf_page\": 34,\n      \"text\": \"The value of ongoing work(s) that are substantially completed\
      \ shall not be updated to the price level of the financial year of this Tender \\nInvitation. \\n8) \\nIn\
      \ case the Similar Nature Work(s) are executed for client other than Government Department, PSU, or other\
      \ Government Body, Contract \\nAgreement, Bill wise details of payment received and Proof of TDS (Form 26AS)\
      \ deducted by clients for all payments received shall be \\nsubmitted duly certified by Chartered Accountant.\
      \ \\n9) \\nWork experience certificates of the tenderers with qualification as a sub-contractor will not be\
      \ considered. \\n3.1. B.  FINANCIAL STANDING: The Tenderer should meet the following financial qualification\
      \ criteria: \\nSl. No \\nCriteria Requirement \\nDocument Submission \\nrequirement \\n3.1.B (i) \\nLiquidity:\

      \ facilities of not less than ₹ 1.19 Crores (Credit lines/ letter of credit/ certificates \\nfrom banks for\
      \ meeting the fund requirement etc.). \\nAttachment-13 to Letter of \\nTechnical Bid \\n3.1.B (ii) \\nAnnual\
      \ Turnover: Tenderer in its name should have in the last five financial years (2021-2022 \\nto 2025-2026)\
      \ achieved in at least two financial years a minimum financial turnover (in all \\nclasses of civil engineering\
      \ construction works only) ≥ ₹ 9.48 Crores. **** \\nAttachment-8 to Letter of \\nTechnical Bid \\n      Notes:\
      \ \\n1) \\nA copy of the Banker’s Certificate issued from a Nationalised / Scheduled Bank in India (for liquidity)\
      \ needs to be uploaded in the \\nTechnical Proposal in respect of EQC Clause 3.1 B (i). \\n2) \\nAnnual financial\
      \ statements (Audited Balance sheets including all related notes and income statements) for last five financial\
      \ years \\n(2021-2022 to 2025-2026) shall be submitted by the Tenderer. The financial information of the Tenderer\
      \ must be certified either by the \\nIndependent Financial Auditor (statutory Auditor) of the company appointed\
      \ under the companies’ Act or by a Chartered Accountant with \\nhis stamp and signature with membership number\
      \ and firm registration number. \\n3) \\nIn case any discrepancy in data is found between the Audited Balance\
      \ sheet and the financial information submitted, the data as available \\nin the Audited Balance sheet will\
      \ be considered.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 35,\n      \"text\": \"4) \\nIn case Audited Balance\
      \ sheet of the last financial year ending with 2025-2026 is not made available by the Tenderer, he has to\
      \ submit \\nan affidavit certifying that 'The Balance Sheet has actually not been audited so far'. In such\
      \ a case the financial data of previous '4' \\naudited financial years will be taken into consideration for\
      \ evaluation. If audited Balance sheet of any year other than the last financial \\nyear ending with 2025-2026\
      \ is not submitted even when requested for as a clarification under clause 20 of ITT, then the Tender will\
      \ be \\nconsidered as non-responsive. \\n5) \\nAll documents shall reflect the stand-alone financial status\
      \ of the Tenderer and not the Tenderer’s parent companies, subsidiaries, or \\naffiliates. \\n6) \\nFor the\
      \ purpose of evaluation, amounts in foreign currencies shall be converted to equivalent INR as per the following\
      \ procedure: \\nIn cases where the financial data is denominated in a currency other than Indian Rupees, it\
      \ shall be converted into the equivalent value \\nin Indian Rupees by adopting the exchange rate between the\
      \ foreign currency and INR prevailing on the last day of the respective \\nfinancial year, as published on\
      \ the Reserve Bank of India (RBI) website, for the purpose of bid evaluation. \\n7) \\nTo align a January–December\
      \ financial year with the standard April–March fiscal year, use the following conversion: \\nA calendar year\
      \ (e.g., January–December 2023) corresponds to the subsequent fiscal year (e.g., FY 2023–24, covering April\
      \ 2023 to \\nMarch 2024). Apply this rule consistently to all such conversions. \\n8) \\n****Financial turnover\
      \ in INR/converted equivalent INR of previous financial years shall be given a weightage of 5% per year \\\
      n(compounded annually) to bring them to the price level of the Financial Year of this Tender Invitation. \\\
      n9) \\nAll the documents or certifications, which are provided by Chartered Accountant after 1st July, 2019,\
      \ must contain UDIN thereon and \\nthe particulars of certifications must be same as mentioned on document/certification\
      \ and submitted to ICAI on its website which can be \\nverified online on https://udin.icai.org/search-udin.\
      \ \\n3.2 BID CAPACITY: \\nRequirement \\nDocument Submission \\nRequirement \\nAvailable Bid capacity should\
      \ be ≥ ₹ 4.74 Crores \\nFormula: \\nAvailable Bid Capacity = (1.5 × A × N) – B  \\nAttachment-7, Attachment-\\\
      n8 to Letter of Technical Bid\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"text\": \"Requirement \\\
      nDocument Submission \\nRequirement \\nWhere: \\nA = Maximum value of total Civil Engineering works executed\
      \ (annual turnover from Civil Engineering works) \\nin any one year during the last five financial years.\
      \ The turnover for previous years shall be updated by 5% \\nper year (compounded annually) to bring them to\
      \ the price level of the Financial Year of this Tender Invitation.  \\nN = Number of years prescribed for\
      \ completion of this tendered work as mentioned in NIT.  \\nB = Total value (updated to the price level of\
      \ the Financial Year of this Tender Invitation) of existing \\ncommitments for ongoing Civil Engineering works\
      \ during the completion period mentioned in NIT, from the \\nlast day of the month previous to the month of\
      \ invitation of this tender. \\nNotes: \\n1) Works in Hand information provided in ‘Attachment-7 to Letter\
      \ of Technical Bid’ must be certified either by the Independent Financial \\nAuditor (Statutory Auditor) of\
      \ the company appointed under the companies’ Act or by a Chartered Accountant with his stamp and signature\
      \ \\nwith Membership Number and Firm’s Registration Number. \\n2) All the documents or certifications, which\
      \ are provided by Chartered Accountant after 1st July 2019, must contain UDIN thereon and the \\nparticulars\
      \ of certifications must be same as mentioned on document/certification and submitted to ICAI on its website,\
      \ which can be \\nverified online on https://udin.icai.org/search-udin.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"review_status\"\
      : \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"title\": \"Qualification Criteria\"\n}"
    page_basis: Parsed record provenance; not independently PDF-verified
