- kind: parameter
  text: Work experience monetary alternatives require either 1 work of ₹ 3.79 Crores, 2 works of ₹ 2.37 Crores each,
    3 works of ₹ 1.90 Crores each, or any number of works with a combined value of ₹ 4.74 Crores.
  evidence_ids:
  - J704434795075ef24
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation
  from_id: ''
  to_id: ''
  id: Cb07f0491248f02c0
  citations:
  - id: J704434795075ef24
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secC.md
    okf_sha256: 5ae5ea7a30496ffbb3384dfb998e3fdd89ef3222744b6b639e3d564508b11172
    heading: Clause 3.0 — Qualification Criteria
    source_document: Vol1DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: 74441e621d2e627d9132506a894883aff3b5fe3fdfa9600f5be3c7ec394d5c5c
    pdf_pages:
    - 32
    - 33
    - 34
    - 35
    - 36
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol1/section_c_eqc/eqc_clauses.json
    json_sha256: d8207622663bc2dd0cf2a7c5e3ed660f5c3881838e376d1bbb26fdae47ed8abf
    json_pointer: /clauses/2
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"3.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 32,\n      \"text\": \"\
      3.0 \\nQUALIFICATION CRITERIA \\n \\nPursuant to clause 2.0, tenderers shall meet the qualification criteria\
      \ stipulated in the succeeding paragraphs in order to qualify. \\n3.1 WORK EXPERIENCE & FINANCIAL STANDING:\
      \ \\n3.1. A. WORK EXPERIENCE \\nSl. No \\nCriteria Requirement \\nDocument Submission \\nRequirement \\n3.1.A.(i)\
      \ \\nTenderer as a Prime Contractor (Single entity or Joint Venture member) during last 07(seven) \\nyears\
      \ ending as on last day of the month previous to the month of invitation of this tender, \\nshould have completed/\
      \ **substantially completed One work of *Similar nature, where the \\nvalue of the work is equal to or exceed\
      \ ₹ 3.79 Crores.*** \\nAttachment-5 & Attachment-\\n6 to Letter of Technical Bid \\nOR \\n3.1.A. (ii) \\nTenderer\
      \ as a Prime Contractor (Single entity or Joint Venture member) during last 07(seven) \\nyears ending as on\
      \ last day of the month previous to the month of invitation of this tender, \\nshould have completed/ **substantially\
      \ completed Two works of *Similar nature, where the \\nvalue of each work is equal to or exceed ₹ 2.37 Crores.\
      \ *** \\nAttachment-5 & Attachment-\\n6 to Letter of Technical Bid \\nOR \\n3.1.A. (iii) Tenderer as a Prime\
      \ Contractor (Single entity or Joint Venture member) during last 07(seven) \\nyears ending as on last day\
      \ of the month previous to the month of invitation of this tender, \\nshould have completed/ **substantially\
      \ completed Three works of *Similar nature, where the \\nvalue of each work is equal to or exceed ₹ 1.90 Crores.***\
      \ \\nAttachment-5 & Attachment-\\n6 to Letter of Technical Bid \\nOR \\n3.1.A. (iv) Tenderer as a Prime Contractor\
      \ (Single entity or Joint Venture member) during last 07(seven) \\nyears ending as on last day of the month\
      \ previous to the month of invitation of this tender, \\nAttachment-5 & Attachment-\\n6 to Letter of Technical\
      \ Bid\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 33,\n      \"text\": \"should have completed/ **substantially\
      \ completed in any number of works of *Similar nature, \\nwhere the combined value of works is equal to or\
      \ exceed ₹ 4.74 Crores.*** \\nNotes: \\n1) \\n* Similar nature work for this contract shall be, ‘Demolition/Dismantling\
      \ of Buildings/Structures’. \\n2) \\n** Similar nature work(s) where Financial value of physically executed\
      \ work is 90%  or more of the original awarded contract value or \\nrevised contract value (if any), duly\
      \ certified by the client, will be treated as substantially completed Similar nature work(s) and will be \\\
      nconsidered for qualification of work experience criteria. In case original contract value has been revised,\
      \ the revised contract value shall \\nbe considered for this purpose.  \\n3) \\nThe Tenderer shall furnish\
      \ the Client’s Completion Certificate in support of the Work Experience criteria as specified under Clause\
      \ 3.1A. \\nThe certificate shall clearly indicate the Nature and Scope of Similar Nature Work, Awarded Contract\
      \ Value (excluding GST), Actual \\nCompletion Value (excluding GST), and the Actual Date of Completion. Where\
      \ applicable, the certificate shall also specify the Revised \\nContract Value (excluding GST), along with\
      \ details of any variation orders and extensions of time granted. \\n4) \\nIn cases where the Client’s Completion\
      \ Certificate indicates values inclusive of GST and/or other applicable taxes, the Tenderer shall \\nsubmit\
      \ the actual completion cost after deducting the applicable GST dully certified by Chartered Accountant. For\
      \ portions of Similar \\nNature Works executed in the pre-GST period, the Tenderer shall make a deduction\
      \ towards taxes at the rate of 12% to arrive at the \\ncompletion cost exclusive of taxes. \\n5) \\nIf a Tenderer\
      \ has completed /substantially completed the Similar Nature Work(s) as a member of a Joint Venture (JV), the\
      \ value of \\n‘Similar Nature Work(s)’ will be recognized only in proportion to the Tenderer’s stipulated\
      \ financial share in that JV. A copy of the JV \\nAgreement must be submitted with the bid. \\n6) \\nFor the\
      \ purpose of evaluation, value of works in foreign currencies shall be converted to equivalent INR as per\
      \ the following procedure: \\nValue of completed/ substantially completed Similar Nature Work(s) in foreign\
      \ currency shall be converted to Equivalent INR by adopting \\nExchange rate between the foreign currency\
      \ and INR as prevailing on the date of completion / date given in the substantial completion \\ncertificate\
      \ as applicable, as per the website of Reserve Bank of India (RBI). \\n7) \\n***The value of Completed Similar\
      \ Nature Work(s) executed during the previous financial years shall be updated at a rate of 5% per \\nyear,\
      \ compounded annually, for the Indian Rupees portion (including foreign currencies, converted to Equivalent\
      \ INR), to bring them to \\nthe price level of the financial year of this Tender Invitation.\"\n    },\n \
      \   {\n      \"pdf_page\": 34,\n      \"text\": \"The value of ongoing work(s) that are substantially completed\
      \ shall not be updated to the price level of the financial year of this Tender \\nInvitation. \\n8) \\nIn\
      \ case the Similar Nature Work(s) are executed for client other than Government Department, PSU, or other\
      \ Government Body, Contract \\nAgreement, Bill wise details of payment received and Proof of TDS (Form 26AS)\
      \ deducted by clients for all payments received shall be \\nsubmitted duly certified by Chartered Accountant.\
      \ \\n9) \\nWork experience certificates of the tenderers with qualification as a sub-contractor will not be\
      \ considered. \\n3.1. B.  FINANCIAL STANDING: The Tenderer should meet the following financial qualification\
      \ criteria: \\nSl. No \\nCriteria Requirement \\nDocument Submission \\nrequirement \\n3.1.B (i) \\nLiquidity:\
      \ Tenderer shall demonstrate that it has access to, or possess liquid assets and /or \\navailability of credit\
      \ facilities of not less than ₹ 1.19 Crores (Credit lines/ letter of credit/ certificates \\nfrom banks for\
      \ meeting the fund requirement etc.). \\nAttachment-13 to Letter of \\nTechnical Bid \\n3.1.B (ii) \\nAnnual\
      \ Turnover: Tenderer in its name should have in the last five financial years (2021-2022 \\nto 2025-2026)\
      \ achieved in at least two financial years a minimum financial turnover (in all \\nclasses of civil engineering\
      \ construction works only) ≥ ₹ 9.48 Crores. **** \\nAttachment-8 to Letter of \\nTechnical Bid \\n      Notes:\
      \ \\n1) \\nA copy of the Banker’s Certificate issued from a Nationalised / Scheduled Bank in India (for liquidity)\
      \ needs to be uploaded in the \\nTechnical Proposal in respect of EQC Clause 3.1 B (i). \\n2) \\nAnnual financial\
      \ statements (Audited Balance sheets including all related notes and income statements) for last five financial\
      \ years \\n(2021-2022 to 2025-2026) shall be submitted by the Tenderer. The financial information of the Tenderer\
      \ must be certified either by the \\nIndependent Financial Auditor (statutory Auditor) of the company appointed\
      \ under the companies’ Act or by a Chartered Accountant with \\nhis stamp and signature with membership number\
      \ and firm registration number. \\n3) \\nIn case any discrepancy in data is found between the Audited Balance\
      \ sheet and the financial information submitted, the data as available \\nin the Audited Balance sheet will\
      \ be considered.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 35,\n      \"text\": \"4) \\nIn case Audited Balance\
      \ sheet of the last financial year ending with 2025-2026 is not made available by the Tenderer, he has to\
      \ submit \\nan affidavit certifying that 'The Balance Sheet has actually not been audited so far'. In such\
      \ a case the financial data of previous '4' \\naudited financial years will be taken into consideration for\
      \ evaluation. If audited Balance sheet of any year other than the last financial \\nyear ending with 2025-2026\
      \ is not submitted even when requested for as a clarification under clause 20 of ITT, then the Tender will\
      \ be \\nconsidered as non-responsive. \\n5) \\nAll documents shall reflect the stand-alone financial status\
      \ of the Tenderer and not the Tenderer’s parent companies, subsidiaries, or \\naffiliates. \\n6) \\nFor the\
      \ purpose of evaluation, amounts in foreign currencies shall be converted to equivalent INR as per the following\
      \ procedure: \\nIn cases where the financial data is denominated in a currency other than Indian Rupees, it\
      \ shall be converted into the equivalent value \\nin Indian Rupees by adopting the exchange rate between the\
      \ foreign currency and INR prevailing on the last day of the respective \\nfinancial year, as published on\
      \ the Reserve Bank of India (RBI) website, for the purpose of bid evaluation. \\n7) \\nTo align a January–December\
      \ financial year with the standard April–March fiscal year, use the following conversion: \\nA calendar year\
      \ (e.g., January–December 2023) corresponds to the subsequent fiscal year (e.g., FY 2023–24, covering April\
      \ 2023 to \\nMarch 2024). Apply this rule consistently to all such conversions. \\n8) \\n****Financial turnover\
      \ in INR/converted equivalent INR of previous financial years shall be given a weightage of 5% per year \\\
      n(compounded annually) to bring them to the price level of the Financial Year of this Tender Invitation. \\\
      n9) \\nAll the documents or certifications, which are provided by Chartered Accountant after 1st July, 2019,\
      \ must contain UDIN thereon and \\nthe particulars of certifications must be same as mentioned on document/certification\
      \ and submitted to ICAI on its website which can be \\nverified online on https://udin.icai.org/search-udin.\
      \ \\n3.2 BID CAPACITY: \\nRequirement \\nDocument Submission \\nRequirement \\nAvailable Bid capacity should\
      \ be ≥ ₹ 4.74 Crores \\nFormula: \\nAvailable Bid Capacity = (1.5 × A × N) – B  \\nAttachment-7, Attachment-\\\
      n8 to Letter of Technical Bid\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 36,\n      \"text\": \"Requirement \\\
      nDocument Submission \\nRequirement \\nWhere: \\nA = Maximum value of total Civil Engineering works executed\
      \ (annual turnover from Civil Engineering works) \\nin any one year during the last five financial years.\
      \ The turnover for previous years shall be updated by 5% \\nper year (compounded annually) to bring them to\
      \ the price level of the Financial Year of this Tender Invitation.  \\nN = Number of years prescribed for\
      \ completion of this tendered work as mentioned in NIT.  \\nB = Total value (updated to the price level of\
      \ the Financial Year of this Tender Invitation) of existing \\ncommitments for ongoing Civil Engineering works\
      \ during the completion period mentioned in NIT, from the \\nlast day of the month previous to the month of\
      \ invitation of this tender. \\nNotes: \\n1) Works in Hand information provided in ‘Attachment-7 to Letter\
      \ of Technical Bid’ must be certified either by the Independent Financial \\nAuditor (Statutory Auditor) of\
      \ the company appointed under the companies’ Act or by a Chartered Accountant with his stamp and signature\
      \ \\nwith Membership Number and Firm’s Registration Number. \\n2) All the documents or certifications, which\
      \ are provided by Chartered Accountant after 1st July 2019, must contain UDIN thereon and the \\nparticulars\
      \ of certifications must be same as mentioned on document/certification and submitted to ICAI on its website,\
      \ which can be \\nverified online on https://udin.icai.org/search-udin.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"review_status\"\
      : \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"title\": \"Qualification Criteria\"\n}"

    crushers, hydraulic mobile cranes, high/long reach demolition equipment, hydraulic rock breakers with loaders,
    and 100kVA or higher capacity DG sets.

    json_pointer: /forms/17
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"attachment_id\": \"12\",\n  \"form_id\": \"attachment_12\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n    \
      \  \"pdf_page\": 54,\n      \"text\": \"ATTACHMENT -12\\n\\n\\n      FORM OF BANK GUARANTEE FOR TENDER SECURITY/\
      \ EARNEST MONEY DEPOSIT\\n\\n                       (To be stamped in accordance with the Stamp Act of the\
      \ Country of Issuing Bank)\\n\\n     To,\\n    Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Limited (BMRCL)\\n      3rd\
      \ Floor BMTC Complex,\\n     K.H. Road, Shanthinagar\\n     Bengaluru- 560027.\\n     Karnataka, INDIA.\\\
      n\\n1.  WHEREAS ______________ [Name and Address of the Tenderer] (hereinafter referred to as the “Bidder”\
      \ or\\n     “Tenderer”) has  undertaken  for  submission  of  Tender  in  pursuance  of  Tender  Notification\
      \  No.\\n    _____________ for _________________________ [Name of the Works] (hereinafter referred to as the\
      \ “Bid”\\n     or “Tender”).\\n\\n2.  AND WHEREAS it has been stipulated in the Tender issued by Bangalore\
      \ Metro Rail Corporation Limited\\n    (BMRCL) (hereinafter referred to as the “Employer” or “BMRCL”) that\
      \ the Tenderer shall furnish to\\n    Employer an unconditional and irrevocable Bank Guarantee from an Indian\
      \ Scheduled Bank (excluding\\n     Cooperative Bank) or from a Scheduled Foreign Bank as defined in Section\
      \ 2(e) of RBI Act 1934 read with\\n    second schedule, for the sum specified herein below as Tender Security(EMD)\
      \ for compliance of the\\n     obligations in accordance with the Tender conditions. In pursuance to this,\
      \ M/s_________ has requested\\n      for issuance of the Bank Guarantee for the sum specified herein below\
      \ as Tender security (EMD).\\n\\n\\n3.  AND WHEREAS we _____________________________ [Name and full address\
      \ of the Bank including Email\\n     address] having registered office at ____________________________ (hereinafter\
      \ referred to as the\\n     “Bank”), which expression shall unless repugnant to the context or the meaning\
      \ thereof, include  its\\n     successors, administrators, executors, assignees, do hereby agree to give the\
      \ Employer such a Bank\\n    Guarantee drawn and payable at Bengaluru ________ branch on behalf of the tenderer.\\\
      n\\n\\n4.  NOW therefore, the Bank hereby affirm that  it is the guarantor and responsible to ‘Employer’ and\
      \ their\\n     successors,  on  behalf   of  the  ‘Tenderer’  up  to  a   total  sum   of  INR/Foreign  Currency/ies\\\
      n    _________________________________ [the amount of Guarantee to be specified in figure and words in the\\\
      n     respective currency/ies as  specified  in  the Tender document]. Further, the Bank hereby unconditionally,\\\
      n     irrevocably and without demur undertake to immediately pay, the Employer upon the Employer’s  first\\\
      n     written demand and without cavil, or arguments any sum or sums within the limits of the sum specified\
      \ in\\n      this Bank Guarantee as aforesaid without the Employer being required to prove or to show grounds\
      \ or\\n    reasons for the demand, by depositing/crediting to the Current Account of the Employer, the sums\\\
      n     specified herein.\\n\\n\\n5.   This Bank Guarantee is encashable at our designated Branch as mentioned\
      \ below:\\n\\n    Name of the Bank, Branch and contact details Address Telephone no: & Fax No: E-mail Address\
      \ Branch Manager/s\\n    name & mobile No: Bank Zonal Office Address, Telephone No, Fax Number, E-mail Address:\
      \ __________________\\n                                   OR\\n     This e- Bank Guarantee shall be encashable\
      \ at the issuing branch of the Bank through NeSL portal.\\n6.  The Bank hereby confirms that the Bank is on\
      \ the SFMS (Structural Finance Messaging System) platform\\n    and shall invariably send an advice of the\
      \ Bank Guarantee to the designated Bank of the Employer as\\n     given below:\\n\\n         Sl. No.  Particulars\
      \              Details\\n                i      Name of Beneficiary     Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Limited\\\
      n                ii      Name of Bank         Bank of India\\n                iii       Branch           \
      \    Cantonment Branch, Bengaluru.\\n          iv       Address of Branch       49, Jyothi Mahal,\\n     \
      \                                           St. Mark’s Road, Bangalore - 560001\\n       v       Account No.\
      \           840420110000760\\n          vi      IFSC Code:           BKID0008404\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"\
      pdf_page\": 55,\n      \"text\": \"7.   This Bank Guarantee shall be encashed at the demand of the Employer\
      \ on or before the due date.\\n\\n8.  The Employer shall have full rights to encash this bank guarantee at\
      \ any time during the validity period and\\n     the ‘Tenderer’ shall have no right or claim whatsoever in\
      \ the matter of encashment of the Bank guarantee\\n    amount by the ‘Employer’. The Bank will be fully responsible\
      \ in terms of the guarantee and obligation, to\\n   make immediate payment to ‘Employer’, without the consent\
      \ of the Tenderer and without referring the\\n     matter to the ‘Tenderer’.\\n\\n9.  The Bank will deliver\
      \ the money required by the ‘Employer’ immediately on demand without delay without\\n     reference to the\
      \ ‘Tenderer’ and without the necessity of a previous notice or of judicial or administrative\\n    procedures\
      \ and without it being necessary to prove to the Bank the liability or damages resulting from any\\n     defects\
      \ or shortcomings or debts of the ‘Tenderer’.\\n\\n10.  Courts at Bengaluru, Karnataka, India shall have exclusive\
      \ jurisdiction for adjudicating the disputes arising\\n     out of the encashment of the Guarantee, and, the\
      \ said Bank undertakes not to revoke this Guarantee\\n     during its currency, except with the previous consent\
      \ of the ‘Employer’ in writing and agree that any change\\n      in the constitution of the Bank shall not\
      \ discharge its liability hereunder.\\n\\n11.  This Bank Guarantee will not be discharged due to the change\
      \ in the constitution of the ‘Tenderer’ or\\n    change in the constitution of the bank.\\n\\n12.  This Deed\
      \ of guarantee is signed and executed by Shri/Smt ______________ and Shri/Smt ______ who\\n     is/are authorized\
      \ by the Bank for issuing the guarantee on  its behalf and their ID No/s. and Email\\n    address/es being________\
      \ & ________ and ________ & ________ respectively.\\n\\n13. The Bank’s liability under this Guarantee shall\
      \ not exceed the amount of ______ [Indian Rupees/Foreign\\n     Currency/ies to be specified in words and\
      \ figures].\\n\\n14.  This guarantee shall be valid till _________ [Date to be mentioned].\\n\\n15. The pendency\
      \ of any dispute or legal proceedings shall not affect this guarantee in any manner.\\n\\n16.   It is hereby\
      \ agreed that the liability of the Bank under this guarantee shall cease on whichever of the\\n     following\
      \ events first occurs.\\n        a) Payment by the Bank of the guaranteed sum in full to the ‘Employer’,\\\
      n                                                 or\\n        b) Receipt of written communication from the\
      \ ‘Employer’ to the Bank along with the   original Bank\\n        Guarantee discharging the Bank of  its \
      \ liability covered by this Bank Guarantee and in case of\\n          electronic Bank Guarantee (e-BG), after\
      \ release/discharge of e-BG by the Employer through NeSL\\n           portal.\\n\\n                      \
      \      SIGNATURE AND SEAL OF THE GUARANTOR\\n\\n                               DATE: ___________________\\\
      n                         NAME OF THE BANK: ______________________________\\n                            ADDRESS:\
      \ _______________________________________\\nNote:\\n1.  The stamp paper of appropriate value shall be purchased\
      \ in the name of the bank, who issues the “Bank\\n    Guarantee”.\\n2.  The above format shall be used for\
      \ e-Bank Guarantee also.\\n3.   For issuance of e-BG, the UIN (PAN No.) of BMRCL is AAACB4881D.\\n4.  Tender\
      \ No. as mentioned in relevant para of ITT pertaining to Tender Security shall be the reference to be\\n \
      \   mentioned in the e-BG.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"ATTACHMENT -12\",\n  \"kind\": \"attachment\"\
      ,\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"source_marking\": \"not_marked_deleted\"\n}"

      - id: Jf726bdabdb3daf46
        okf_path: OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secD.md
        okf_sha256: fb8297a9b0fb5a39db7af743aed04bb65c2a461730b8d1572f10654738e1a198
        heading: Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)
        source_document: Vol1DLNP1.pdf
        source_sha256: 74441e621d2e627d9132506a894883aff3b5fe3fdfa9600f5be3c7ec394d5c5c
        pdf_pages:
        - 54
        - 55
        sheet_cells: ''
        json_path: parsed_json/vol1/section_d_technical_bid/technical_bid_forms.json
        json_sha256: 6da3816d8291f7364cf6e12f62bf2c0b37ebebda9067f0516d4678f5e1f8f8d9
        json_pointer: /forms/17
        json_record_id: null
        source_review_status: pending_manual_review
        quote: "{\n  \"attachment_id\": \"12\",\n  \"form_id\": \"attachment_12\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n\
          \      \"pdf_page\": 54,\n      \"text\": \"ATTACHMENT -12\\n\\n\\n      FORM OF BANK GUARANTEE FOR TENDER\
          \ SECURITY/ EARNEST MONEY DEPOSIT\\n\\n                       (To be stamped in accordance with the Stamp\
          \ Act of the Country of Issuing Bank)\\n\\n     To,\\n    Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Limited (BMRCL)\\\
          n      3rd Floor BMTC Complex,\\n     K.H. Road, Shanthinagar\\n     Bengaluru- 560027.\\n     Karnataka,\
          \ INDIA.\\n\\n1.  WHEREAS ______________ [Name and Address of the Tenderer] (hereinafter referred to as\
          \ the “Bidder” or\\n     “Tenderer”) has  undertaken  for  submission  of  Tender  in  pursuance  of \
          \ Tender  Notification  No.\\n    _____________ for _________________________ [Name of the Works] (hereinafter\
          \ referred to as the “Bid”\\n     or “Tender”).\\n\\n2.  AND WHEREAS it has been stipulated in the Tender\
          \ issued by Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Limited\\n    (BMRCL) (hereinafter referred to as the “Employer”\
          \ or “BMRCL”) that the Tenderer shall furnish to\\n    Employer an unconditional and irrevocable Bank\
          \ Guarantee from an Indian Scheduled Bank (excluding\\n     Cooperative Bank) or from a Scheduled Foreign\
          \ Bank as defined in Section 2(e) of RBI Act 1934 read with\\n    second schedule, for the sum specified\
          \ herein below as Tender Security(EMD) for compliance of the\\n     obligations in accordance with the\
          \ Tender conditions. In pursuance to this, M/s_________ has requested\\n      for issuance of the Bank\
          \ Guarantee for the sum specified herein below as Tender security (EMD).\\n\\n\\n3.  AND WHEREAS we _____________________________\
          \ [Name and full address of the Bank including Email\\n     address] having registered office at ____________________________\
          \ (hereinafter referred to as the\\n     “Bank”), which expression shall unless repugnant to the context\
          \ or the meaning thereof, include  its\\n     successors, administrators, executors, assignees, do hereby\
          \ agree to give the Employer such a Bank\\n    Guarantee drawn and payable at Bengaluru ________ branch\
          \ on behalf of the tenderer.\\n\\n\\n4.  NOW therefore, the Bank hereby affirm that  it is the guarantor\
          \ and responsible to ‘Employer’ and their\\n     successors,  on  behalf   of  the  ‘Tenderer’  up  to\
          \  a   total  sum   of  INR/Foreign  Currency/ies\\n    _________________________________ [the amount\
          \ of Guarantee to be specified in figure and words in the\\n     respective currency/ies as  specified\
          \  in  the Tender document]. Further, the Bank hereby unconditionally,\\n     irrevocably and without\
          \ demur undertake to immediately pay, the Employer upon the Employer’s  first\\n     written demand and\
          \ without cavil, or arguments any sum or sums within the limits of the sum specified in\\n      this Bank\
          \ Guarantee as aforesaid without the Employer being required to prove or to show grounds or\\n    reasons\
          \ for the demand, by depositing/crediting to the Current Account of the Employer, the sums\\n     specified\
          \ herein.\\n\\n\\n5.   This Bank Guarantee is encashable at our designated Branch as mentioned below:\\\
          n\\n    Name of the Bank, Branch and contact details Address Telephone no: & Fax No: E-mail Address Branch\
          \ Manager/s\\n    name & mobile No: Bank Zonal Office Address, Telephone No, Fax Number, E-mail Address:\
          \ __________________\\n                                   OR\\n     This e- Bank Guarantee shall be encashable\
          \ at the issuing branch of the Bank through NeSL portal.\\n6.  The Bank hereby confirms that the Bank\
          \ is on the SFMS (Structural Finance Messaging System) platform\\n    and shall invariably send an advice\
          \ of the Bank Guarantee to the designated Bank of the Employer as\\n     given below:\\n\\n         Sl.\
          \ No.  Particulars              Details\\n                i      Name of Beneficiary     Bangalore Metro\
          \ Rail Corporation Limited\\n                ii      Name of Bank         Bank of India\\n           \
          \     iii       Branch               Cantonment Branch, Bengaluru.\\n          iv       Address of Branch\
          \       49, Jyothi Mahal,\\n                                                St. Mark’s Road, Bangalore\
          \ - 560001\\n       v       Account No.           840420110000760\\n          vi      IFSC Code:     \
          \      BKID0008404\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 55,\n      \"text\": \"7.   This Bank Guarantee\
          \ shall be encashed at the demand of the Employer on or before the due date.\\n\\n8.  The Employer shall\
          \ have full rights to encash this bank guarantee at any time during the validity period and\\n     the\
          \ ‘Tenderer’ shall have no right or claim whatsoever in the matter of encashment of the Bank guarantee\\\
          n    amount by the ‘Employer’. The Bank will be fully responsible in terms of the guarantee and obligation,\
          \ to\\n   make immediate payment to ‘Employer’, without the consent of the Tenderer and without referring\
          \ the\\n     matter to the ‘Tenderer’.\\n\\n9.  The Bank will deliver the money required by the ‘Employer’\
          \ immediately on demand without delay without\\n     reference to the ‘Tenderer’ and without the necessity\
          \ of a previous notice or of judicial or administrative\\n    procedures and without it being necessary\
          \ to prove to the Bank the liability or damages resulting from any\\n     defects or shortcomings or debts\
          \ of the ‘Tenderer’.\\n\\n10.  Courts at Bengaluru, Karnataka, India shall have exclusive jurisdiction\
          \ for adjudicating the disputes arising\\n     out of the encashment of the Guarantee, and, the said Bank\
          \ undertakes not to revoke this Guarantee\\n     during its currency, except with the previous consent\
          \ of the ‘Employer’ in writing and agree that any change\\n      in the constitution of the Bank shall\
          \ not discharge its liability hereunder.\\n\\n11.  This Bank Guarantee will not be discharged due to the\
          \ change in the constitution of the ‘Tenderer’ or\\n    change in the constitution of the bank.\\n\\n12.\
          \  This Deed of guarantee is signed and executed by Shri/Smt ______________ and Shri/Smt ______ who\\\
          n     is/are authorized by the Bank for issuing the guarantee on  its behalf and their ID No/s. and Email\\\
          n    address/es being________ & ________ and ________ & ________ respectively.\\n\\n13. The Bank’s liability\
          \ under this Guarantee shall not exceed the amount of ______ [Indian Rupees/Foreign\\n     Currency/ies\
          \ to be specified in words and figures].\\n\\n14.  This guarantee shall be valid till _________ [Date\
          \ to be mentioned].\\n\\n15. The pendency of any dispute or legal proceedings shall not affect this guarantee\
          \ in any manner.\\n\\n16.   It is hereby agreed that the liability of the Bank under this guarantee shall\
          \ cease on whichever of the\\n     following events first occurs.\\n        a) Payment by the Bank of\
          \ the guaranteed sum in full to the ‘Employer’,\\n                                                 or\\\
          n        b) Receipt of written communication from the ‘Employer’ to the Bank along with the   original\
          \ Bank\\n        Guarantee discharging the Bank of  its  liability covered by this Bank Guarantee and\
          \ in case of\\n          electronic Bank Guarantee (e-BG), after release/discharge of e-BG by the Employer\
          \ through NeSL\\n           portal.\\n\\n                            SIGNATURE AND SEAL OF THE GUARANTOR\\\
          n\\n                               DATE: ___________________\\n                         NAME OF THE BANK:\
          \ ______________________________\\n                            ADDRESS: _______________________________________\\\
          nNote:\\n1.  The stamp paper of appropriate value shall be purchased in the name of the bank, who issues\
          \ the “Bank\\n    Guarantee”.\\n2.  The above format shall be used for e-Bank Guarantee also.\\n3.   For\
          \ issuance of e-BG, the UIN (PAN No.) of BMRCL is AAACB4881D.\\n4.  Tender No. as mentioned in relevant\
          \ para of ITT pertaining to Tender Security shall be the reference to be\\n    mentioned in the e-BG.\"\
          \n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"ATTACHMENT -12\",\n  \"kind\": \"attachment\",\n  \"review_status\"\
          : \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"source_marking\": \"not_marked_deleted\"\n}"

  text: Attachment 8 note 4 provides that if the audited balance sheet for the financial year ending 2025-26 is
    unavailable, the bidder must submit an affidavit certifying that the balance sheet is not yet audited, allowing
    evaluation based on the preceding four audited financial years.

    quote: "{\n  \"pdf_page\": 34,\n  \"text\": \"The value of ongoing work(s) that are substantially completed\
      \ shall not be updated to the price level of the financial year of this Tender \\nInvitation. \\n8) \\nIn\
      \ case the Similar Nature Work(s) are executed for client other than Government Department, PSU, or other\
      \ Government Body, Contract \\nAgreement, Bill wise details of payment received and Proof of TDS (Form 26AS)\
      \ deducted by clients for all payments received shall be \\nsubmitted duly certified by Chartered Accountant.\
      \ \\n9) \\nWork experience certificates of the tenderers with qualification as a sub-contractor will not be\
      \ considered. \\n3.1. B.  FINANCIAL STANDING: The Tenderer should meet the following financial qualification\
      \ criteria: \\nSl. No \\nCriteria Requirement \\nDocument Submission \\nrequirement \\n3.1.B (i) \\nLiquidity:\
      \ Tenderer shall demonstrate that it has access to, or possess liquid assets and /or \\navailability of credit\
      \ facilities of not less than ₹ 1.19 Crores (Credit lines/ letter of credit/ certificates \\nfrom banks for\
      \ meeting the fund requirement etc.). \\nAttachment-13 to Letter of \\nTechnical Bid \\n3.1.B (ii) \\nAnnual\
      \ Turnover: Tenderer in its name should have in the last five financial years (2021-2022 \\nto 2025-2026)\
      \ achieved in at least two financial years a minimum financial turnover (in all \\nclasses of civil engineering\
      \ construction works only) ≥ ₹ 9.48 Crores. **** \\nAttachment-8 to Letter of \\nTechnical Bid \\n      Notes:\
      \ \\n1) \\nA copy of the Banker’s Certificate issued from a Nationalised / Scheduled Bank in India (for liquidity)\
      \ needs to be uploaded in the \\nTechnical Proposal in respect of EQC Clause 3.1 B (i). \\n2) \\nAnnual financial\
      \ statements (Audited Balance sheets including all related notes and income statements) for last five financial\
      \ years \\n(2021-2022 to 2025-2026) shall be submitted by the Tenderer. The financial information of the Tenderer\
      \ must be certified either by the \\nIndependent Financial Auditor (statutory Auditor) of the company appointed\
      \ under the companies’ Act or by a Chartered Accountant with \\nhis stamp and signature with membership number\
      \ and firm registration number. \\n3) \\nIn case any discrepancy in data is found between the Audited Balance\
      \ sheet and the financial information submitted, the data as available \\nin the Audited Balance sheet will\
      \ be considered.\"\n}"

- kind: parameter
  text: 'Liquidated damages for delay are structured around two milestones: Rs. 5,000/- per day for Milestone-1
    (50% area handed over in 6 months) and Rs. 10,000/- per day for Milestone-2 (100% area handed over in 12 months),
    with an upper limit of 5% of the Contract Price.'

  - id: J7957e3c3de8aec5b

    heading: Clause 15 — Insurance
    source_document: Vol1DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: 74441e621d2e627d9132506a894883aff3b5fe3fdfa9600f5be3c7ec394d5c5c
    pdf_pages:
    - 134
    - 135
    - 136
    - 137
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/vol1/section_e_gcc/gcc_clauses.json
    json_sha256: ebcddb3df6158a35353d5cbabda41eaabf5b49ea6a2e275040396356122c49d5
    json_pointer: /clauses/14
    json_record_id: null
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"15\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 134,\n      \"text\": \"\
      15.         Insurance.\\n\\n\\n    Professional    15.1       The Contractor  shall effect and maintain  Professional\
      \  Indemnity  Insurance,\\n    Indemnity                     preferably in the name of BMRCL, for the amount\
      \ in Indian Rupees stipulated in\\n    Insurance                     ‘Contract data’ In respect of any design\
      \ of the Works to be carried out by or on\\n                                     behalf of the Contractor.\
      \  This insurance, which shall ensure the Contractor’s\\n                                                \
      \  liability by reason of professional negligence and errors in the design of the Works,\\n              \
      \                          shall be valid from the date of commencement of Works, until  5 years  after the\\\
      n                                  date  of issue  of Performance  Certificate.\\n                       \
      \                  Alternatively, the Contractor shall renew the insurance before the expiry of the\\n   \
      \                                Yearly Insurance in such a way that the entire validity period is covered.\\\
      n                             The Engineer will not issue Final Payment Certificate until the Contractor has\\\
      n                                produced evidence that coverage of the Professional Indemnity Insurance has\\\
      n                              been provided for the aforesaid period.\\n\\n     Injury to third    15.2 \
      \      The contractor shall be liable for and shall indemnify the Employer (principal)\\n     party      \
      \                    against any liability, loss claim or proceedings whatsoever arising under any\\n    personnel\
      \ and                 statute or at common law in respect of personal injury to or the death of any person\\\
      n     Property of                 whosoever arising out of or in the course of or caused by the carrying out\
      \ the\\n     third party/ies                 contract works, unless due to any act or neglect of the Employer\
      \ (principal).\\n\\n                             The contractor shall be liable for and shall indemnify the\
      \ Employer(Principal)\\n                                    against any expenses, liability, loss, claim or\
      \ proceedings in respect of any injury\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 135,\n      \"text\": \"or damage\
      \ whatsoever to any property real or personal in so far as such injury or\\n                        damage\
      \ arises out of or in the course of or by reason of the carrying out of the\\n                           \
      \     contract works, and provided always that the same is due to any negligence,\\n                     \
      \        omission or default of the contractor, his servants or agents of any sub-contractor,\\n         \
      \                         his servant or agent.\\n\\nInsurance        15.3         Without prejudice to his\
      \  liability to indemnify the Employer (Principal), the\\nAgainst  Injury                contractor shall\
      \ take all types of insurance policies such as Contractors All Risks\\nto Persons and             and Workmen’s\
      \ Compensation (Employers Liability) Policy at his cost in the\\nProperty.                  JOINT NAMES OF\
      \ THE EMPLOYER (PRINCIPAL) AND CONTRACTOR AND\\n                     SUB CONTRACTOR, IF ANY and maintain such\
      \ policies throughout the period\\n                                   of contract works.\\n\\n           \
      \               Such insurance as are necessary to cover the liability of the contractor or as the\\n    \
      \                        case may be of such Sub-Contractor in respect of personal injuries or death of\\\
      n                         workmen engaged by the contractor or sub-contractor, arising out of or in course\\\
      n                                   of or caused by carrying out of work.\\n\\n                         The\
      \ contractor at his cost shall maintain in the joint names of the Employer\\n                            \
      \         (principal) and contractor such insurances as may be required in respect of any\\n             \
      \                expenses, liability, loss, claim or proceedings which the Employer (principal) may\\n   \
      \                               incur or sustain by reason of injury or damage to property real or personal\
      \ arising\\n                                out of or in the course or by reason of the carrying out the work,\
      \ and caused\\n                               otherwise than by the negligence, omission or default of the\
      \ contractor, his\\n                               servants or agents or any sub-contractor, his servants\
      \ or agents.\\n\\n                         Any such insurance policy or policies or receipts effected shall\
      \ have to be\\n                              submitted in original  with the Employer (principal).\\n\\nInsurance\
      \ of     15.4       The contractor at his cost shall in the JOINT NAMES OF THE EMPLOYER\\nthe contract   \
      \             (PRINCIPAL) AND CONTRACTOR:\\nworks under a\\nContractors All                              \
      \     i.    Insure the plant, material and works forming part of the contract works\\nRisks              \
      \                    against loss or damage by all types of risks as envisaged under the\\nInsurance     \
      \                       standard Contractors All Risks policy.\\nPolicy.                                 \
      \                    ii.    obtain the insurance policy for the full replacement cost at site of the\\n  \
      \                                       contract works entrusted to the contractor, sub-contractor.\\n   \
      \                                                               iii.    Include in the insurance policy the\
      \ cost of demolition works if any.\\n                                               iv.   Ensure that the\
      \ policy shall remain in force till the entire contract works\\n                                         \
      \          is completed and the Taking Over Certificate issued.\\n\\n                         The Contractors\
      \ All Risks policy which is an All Risks policy with only named\\n                                exclusions\
      \ normally extends cover for loss or damage caused by fire, storm,\\n                               tempest,\
      \ lightning, flood, aircraft or anything dropped there from, aerial objects,\\n                          \
      \                riot and civil commotion, defective materials, defective workmanship, etc. and\\n       \
      \                             shall be extended to cover the following:\\n\\n                            \
      \            a.  earthquake risks\\n                                        b.  terrorism\\n             \
      \                             c.  Third Party Liability including Cross Liability to the extent of at least\
      \ 10%\\n                                            of the contract works.\\n                            \
      \            d.  Debris removal (of insured property) to the extent of at least Rs. 50 lakhs\\n          \
      \                              e.  Owners (Principal`s) surrounding property to the extent of 10% of the\\\
      n                                           policy limits.\\n                                            \
      \              f. Defect Liability for a period of 12 months from the date mentioned in the\\n           \
      \                             taking over certificate for the whole of the works.\\n\\n Contractor’s    15.5\
      \          All the Contractor's Plant, Equipment & Materials shall be brought and kept at the\\n plant & \
      \                          Facilities Site at the sole cost, risk and expense of the Contractor. The Employer\\\
      n machinery.                     shall not be liable for any loss or damage thereto until the completion of\
      \ work or\\n                            removal of materials, plant & machinery, whichever is later. The Contractor,\
      \ at its\\n                               sole cost, shall maintain adequate, appropriate and prudent insurance\
      \ with\\n                              respect  thereto.   Any  insurance  policy  carried  by  the  Contractor,\
      \  any\\n                              Subcontractor or any third party on the Contractor's Equipment shall\
      \ provide for a\\n                              waiver of the underwriter's right to subrogation against the\
      \ Employer, the Lenders,\\n                                        affiliates, employees, insurers and underwriters.\
      \ The Contractor shall obtain\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 136,\n      \"text\": \"adequate insurance\
      \ to cover all equipment, tools and the Constructional Plants\\n                                   rented\
      \ or leased from third parties.\\n\\n                             The Contractor shall obtain insurance for\
      \ all plant & equipment and materials\\n                                 whether at Site as aforementioned\
      \ or whether in transit or in storage awaiting\\n                                 shipment  to the  Site,\
      \ including erection/re-erection and testing at  site and\\n                                subsequent operation\
      \ and until their removal from site.\\n\\n                             The policy shall be on floater basis\
      \ which provides usage of the plant & machinery\\n                                        at different project\
      \ sites, including Earthquake, Terrorism, underground works,\\n                                          \
      \ third party liability including cross liability for not less than10% of the total value of\\n          \
      \                            plant & machinery. The policy should also cover clearance and removal of debris\\\
      n                                          for not less than 10% of the claim amount, any other relevant add\
      \ on cover. Plant\\n                              and Machinery brought to the site in semi or completely\
      \ knocked down condition\\n                                              is to be insured under the storage\
      \ cum erection All Risks policy. Once assembled\\n                              and put to use and which are\
      \ in readiness for operation are to be insured under\\n                                    the contractor’s\
      \ plant & machinery insurance policy. The policy shall be obtained\\n                                    \
      \       in the joint name of the Principal i.e. BMRCL and the contractor.\\n\\n                          \
      \   The contractor before commencing the contract shall file with BMRCL the list of\\n                   \
      \                 the Plant & machinery that would be in use at the project site, their make, model,\\n  \
      \                                year of manufacture, current cost of replacement and the insurance shall\
      \ be\\n                                     availed based on the list so submitted.\\n    Workmen       15.6\
      \       Workman’s compensation policy with the following endorsement no.140 to protect\\n     Compensation\
      \             Employer (Principal) interest.\\n     (Employers                            “It is hereby understood\
      \ and agreed that in the event of any workman employed\\n       Liability)                  by the within\
      \ named insured or any dependent of such workman bringing or\\n      Insurance.                making a claim\
      \ under Section 12 of the Workmen’s Compensation Act 1923, and\\n                                subsequent\
      \ amendments of the said Act against …………for personal injury or\\n                                  disease\
      \ sustained whilst at work on any contract covered by the terms and\\n                                   \
      \  conditions of the within policy which the Insured may be carrying out for the\\n                      \
      \   said…………….the Company will indemnify the said……….against such claim\\n                               \
      \    and/or any costs, charges and expenses in respect thereof Provided always that\\n                   \
      \                 the Company shall not be liable hereunder unless the Company have the sole\\n          \
      \                       conduct and control of all claims covered by this endorsement.  Nothing in this\\\
      n                               endorsement  shall be construed as  affecting the Insured’s  title  to recover\\\
      n                            damages under any other Section of the said Act.”\\n                        \
      \     The policy shall be:\\n                                        a.  In the joint names of the Employer(Principal),\
      \ contractor and sub-contractor\\n                                        b.  for the declared wages and emoluments\
      \ as per standard terms of the policy.\\n                                          c.  cover all the workmen\
      \ of the contractor and sub-contractor\\n                                        d.  in force till the completion\
      \ of the work\\n                                        e.  include cover for medical expenses incurred on\
      \ account of an accident or\\n                                              injury sustained by the workmen\
      \ in the course of and during the employment\\n                                                          \
      \ f.   include cover for Liability as per Fatal Accidents Act and Common Law.\\n\\n    General          15.7\
      \      The Contractor shall, within the respective periods stated in the Contract Data\\n    requirements\
      \                 (calculated from the Commencement Date), submit to the Employer:\\n     for insurances\\\
      n                                         (a) evidence that the insurances described in this Clause have been\
      \ effected, with\\n                              an Insurance Company operating in India.\\n             \
      \                            (b) Submit the original of the policies with the Employer (Principal) for the\\\
      n                                  insurances described as above.\\n\\n                         When each\
      \ premium has been paid, the Contractor shall submit copy of receipts\\n                                 \
      \       to the Employer. The Contractor shall also, when providing such evidence, policies\\n            \
      \                  and receipts to the Employer, notify the Engineer of so doing.\\n\\n                  \
      \           The Contractor shall effect all insurances for which he is responsible with insurers\\n      \
      \                        and in terms approved by the employer. Each policy insuring against loss or\\n  \
      \                         damage shall provide for payments required to rectify such loss or damage.\\n  \
      \                            Payments received from insurers shall be used for the rectification of such loss\
      \ or\\n                              damage.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 137,\n      \"text\": \"\
      The contractor (and, if appropriate, the employer) shall comply with the conditions\\n                   \
      \              stipulated in each of the insurance policies. The contractor shall make no material\\n    \
      \                               alteration to the terms of any insurance without the prior approval of the\
      \ employer.\\n                                                   If an insurer makes (or purports to make)\
      \ any such alteration, the contractor shall\\n                                    notify the employer immediately.\\\
      n\\n                                                   If the contractor fails to effect and keep in force\
      \ any of the insurances required\\n                            under the contract, or fails to provide satisfactory\
      \ evidence, policies and receipts\\n                                     in accordance with this sub-clause,\
      \ the employer may, without prejudice to any\\n                               other right or remedy, effect\
      \ insurance for the coverage relevant to such default,\\n                          and pay the premiums due.\
      \ In such cases the premium paid by the employer plus\\n                            nominal administrative\
      \ charge shall be recoverable from the contractor by the\\n                             employer, and may\
      \ be deducted by the employer from any amounts due, or to be\\n                       come due, to the contractor\
      \ or recover the same as debt due from the contractor.\\n                         The contractor shall not\
      \ dispute the amount of premium paid by the employer or\\n                               the overhead charges\
      \ thereon.\\n\\n                             Nothing in this clause limits the obligations, liabilities or\
      \ responsibilities of the\\n                                contractor or the employer, under the other terms\
      \ of the contract or otherwise. Any\\n                          amount not insured or not recovered from the\
      \ insurers shall be borne by the\\n                                contractor and/or the Employer accordingly,\
      \ unless otherwise specified in the\\n                              Special Conditions of Contract.\\n\\n\
      \                         The Contractor shall submit to the Engineer, the details of all claims made with\\\
      n                               the insurer and claims accepted by the insurer or any other details as required\
      \ by\\n                               the Engineer on monthly basis.\\n\\n                               \
      \       All insurances mentioned  shall be obtained with insurers approved by the\\n                     \
      \      Employer (Principal) and the contractor shall submit with the Employer (Principal)\\n             \
      \                  the policy or policies and the receipts in respect of premium paid.\\n\\n             \
      \              Should the contractor make default in insuring or continuing to insure different\\n       \
      \                     from stated aforesaid the employer(principal) may himself insure against any risk\\\
      n                                with respect of which the default shall have occurred and deduct the premium\
      \ from\\n                          any amounts due to or to become due to the contractor. The Deductions shall\
      \ be\\n                           as envisaged in of the General Requirement for insurance given above.\\\
      n\\n                             Provided always that if the contractor shall independently of his obligations\
      \ under\\n                                     this contract maintain a policy of insurance which covers (inter-alia)\
      \ the said work,\\n                                materials and goods against the aforesaid contingencies\
      \ to the full value thereof\\n                             then the maintenance by the contractor of such\
      \ policy shall  if the employer’s\\n                                   (principal)interest  is endorsed thereon,\
      \ be a discharge  of  the  contractor’s\\n                                 obligation to insure in the joint\
      \ names of the employer(Principal) and the contractor\\n                          and the production by the\
      \ contractor as and when may reasonably be required by\\n                               the Employer(Principal)\
      \ of a current certificate of insurance from the company or\\n                                  firm which\
      \ shall have issued the said policy shall be a discharge of the contractor’s\\n                          \
      \       obligation to submit with the Employer(Principal) a policy or policies and the\\n                \
      \                 receipts in respect of premium paid.\\n\\n                        Upon settlement of any\
      \ claim by the insurance companies under the insurances\\n                                aforesaid, the contractor\
      \ shall diligently with due diligence shall restore work\\n                          damaged, replace or repair\
      \ unfixed materials or goods which have been destroyed\\n                                or injured, remove\
      \ or dispose of any debris and proceed with the carrying out and\\n                             completion\
      \ of the work. All amounts received from such insurances shall be paid\\n                                \
      \  to the contractor by instalments under certificates of the Employer issued at the\\n                  \
      \             “Period of interim Certificate”. The contractor shall not be entitled to payment in\\n     \
      \                         respect of restoration of work damaged, replacement and repair of any unfixed\\\
      n                                materials or goods and removal and disposal of debris Employer(Principal)\
      \ other\\n                             than amounts received under said insurance.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\"\
      : \"15.         Insurance.\",\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n  \"title\": \"Insurance\"\
      \n}"

    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"23.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 45,\n      \"text\": \"\
      23.0     Construction machinery\\n\\n\\n          23.1     Construction machineries may include dumpers and\
      \ dump trucks,  lift trucks and\\n                     telescopic handlers piling rigs, vibro hammers, rail\
      \ welding equipments, mobile\\n                    elevating work platforms, cranes, tipper lorries, lorry\
      \ loaders, skip wagons, 360°\\n                    excavators, 180° backhoe loaders, crawler tractors, scrapers,\
      \ graders, loading\\n                    shovels,  trenchers,  side  booms,  pavers,  planers,  chippers,\
      \  road  rollers,\\n                    locomotives, tankers and bowsers, trailers, hydraulic and mechanical\
      \ breakers\\n                       etc.\\n\\n\\n          23.2     Safe worthiness certificate\\n\\n\\n \
      \         23.2.1   Every construction equipment shall be in sound mechanical working condition and\\n    \
      \                   certified by  either competent person under Factories Act  or manufacturers’\\n      \
      \             warranty in case of brand new equipments or authorized persons / firms approved\\n         \
      \        by Employer before induction to any site.\\n\\n\\n          23.2.2   Every such  certificate  shall\
      \ have  the  date  of  purchase, main  overhauling\\n                  undertaken in the past, any accident\
      \ to the equipment, visual examination details,\\n                          critical components safety check,\
      \ list of safety devises and its working condition,\\n                   manufacturer’s maintenance checklist,\
      \ past projects wherein the equipments were\\n                 used etc as its minimum content.\\n\\n\\n \
      \         23.3     Reverse Horns\\n\\n\\n          23.3.1    All Vehicles shall be fitted with audible reverse\
      \ alarms and maintained in\\n               good working condition. Reversing  shall be done only when there\
      \  is\\n                 adequate rear view visibility or under the directions of a banksman\\n\\n\\n    \
      \      23.4     General operating procedures\\n\\n\\n                                 i)     Drivers entering\
      \ site shall be instructed to follow the safe system of work adopted on\\n                               \
      \ site. These shall be verbal instructions or, preferably, written instructions showing\\n               \
      \           the relevant site rules, the site layout, delivery areas, speed limits, etc.\\n\\n           \
      \                         ii)   No passengers shall be carried, unless specific seating has been provided\
      \  in\\n                       accordance with the manufacturers’ recommendations.\\n\\n                 \
      \                    iii)   Working on gradients beyond any equipments capability shall not be allowed.\\\
      n\\n                          iv)   Prevention of dumper and dump truck accidents should be managed by providing\\\
      n                       wheel stops at a sufficient distance from the edges of excavations, spoil heaps, pits,\\\
      n                             etc.\\n\\n                      v)   The manufacturer’s recommended bucket size\
      \ must not be exceeded in excavators.\\n\\n                          vi)      If excavators operating on a\
      \ gradient which cannot be avoided, it must be ensured\\n                            that the working cycle\
      \ is slowed down, that the bucket is not extended too far in the\\n                          downhill direction,\
      \ and that travel  is undertaken with extreme caution. A large\\n                         excavator must never\
      \ be permitted to travel in a confined area, or around people,\\n                          without a banksman\
      \ to guide the driver, who should have the excavator attachment\\n                         close in to the\
      \ machine, with the bucket just clear of the ground. On wheeled\\n                         excavators, it\
      \ is essential that the tyres are in good condition and correctly inflated.\\n                           \
      \                If stabilizing devices are  fitted, they should be employed when the machine  is\\n     \
      \                     excavating.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 46,\n      \"text\": \"vii)  When\
      \ the front shovel of the 1800 backhoe loaders is being employed, the backhoe\\n               attachment\
      \ shall be in its “travel” position, with the safety locking device in place.\\n\\n               viii)  When\
      \ operating the backhoe in poor ground conditions, the stabilisers tend to sink\\n                  into the\
      \ surface of the ground, reducing stability. Therefore frequent checks shall be\\n           made for the\
      \ stability of the machine. The loading shovel should always be lowered\\n                 to the ground to\
      \ stabilise the machine when the backhoe is employed.\\n\\n             ix)   The netting operation of the\
      \ skip wagons should be carried out prior to lifting the\\n                skip to reduce the risks of working\
      \ on the rear platform\\n\\n           x)       If a tractor dozer is employed on clearing scrub or felling\
      \ trees, it shall be provided\\n                with adequate driver protection.\\n\\n             xi)   When\
      \ two or more scrapers are working on the same job, a minimum distance of at\\n                 least 25m\
      \ shall be kept between them.\\n\\n              xii)   Incase of hydraulic breakers, hydraulic rams and hoses\
      \ shall be in good working\\n                condition\\n\\n\\n23.5       All wood working machines shall\
      \ be fitted with suitable guards and devices such as top\\n          guard, riving knife, push stick, guards\
      \ for drive belts and chains, and emergency stop\\n          switch easily accessible by the operator.\\n\\\
      n\\n23.6     Penalty\\n\\n\\n23.6.1      If any of the above clauses are not adhered, penalty shall be imposed\
      \ as per\\n         relevant clause depending upon the gravity of the unsafe act and or condition.\"\n   \
      \ }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"          23.0     Construction machinery\",\n  \"retained_lines\": [\n \

      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 45,\n      \"source_line_number\": 77,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n\
      \      \"pdf_page\": 45,\n      \"source_line_number\": 78,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"\

      : 45,\n      \"source_line_number\": 80,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 46,\n \
      \     \"source_line_number\": 3,\n      \"text\": \"              vii)  When the front shovel of the 1800\
      \ backhoe loaders is being employed, the backhoe\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 46,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 4,\n      \"text\": \"               attachment shall be in its “travel” position, with the safety locking\
      \ device in place.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 46,\n      \"source_line_number\": 5,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 46,\n      \"source_line_number\": 6,\n      \"text\": \"     \
      \          viii)  When operating the backhoe in poor ground conditions, the stabilisers tend to sink\"\n \
      \   },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 46,\n      \"source_line_number\": 7,\n      \"text\": \"             \
      \     into the surface of the ground, reducing stability. Therefore frequent checks shall be\"\n    },\n \
      \   {\n      \"pdf_page\": 46,\n      \"source_line_number\": 8,\n      \"text\": \"           made for the\
      \ stability of the machine. The loading shovel should always be lowered\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 46,\n      \"source_line_number\": 9,\n      \"text\": \"                 to the ground to stabilise the\
      \ machine when the backhoe is employed.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 46,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 10,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 46,\n      \"source_line_number\": 11,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"             ix)   The netting operation of the skip wagons should be carried out prior\
      \ to lifting the\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 46,\n      \"source_line_number\": 12,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"                skip to reduce the risks of working on the rear platform\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 46,\n      \"source_line_number\": 13,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 46,\n \
      \     \"source_line_number\": 14,\n      \"text\": \"           x)       If a tractor dozer is employed on\
      \ clearing scrub or felling trees, it shall be provided\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 46,\n      \"\
      source_line_number\": 15,\n      \"text\": \"                with adequate driver protection.\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 46,\n      \"source_line_number\": 16,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n\
      \      \"pdf_page\": 46,\n      \"source_line_number\": 17,\n      \"text\": \"             xi)   When two\
      \ or more scrapers are working on the same job, a minimum distance of at\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 46,\n      \"source_line_number\": 18,\n      \"text\": \"                 least 25m shall be kept between\
      \ them.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 46,\n      \"source_line_number\": 19,\n      \"text\": \"\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 46,\n      \"source_line_number\": 20,\n      \"text\": \"          \
      \    xii)   Incase of hydraulic breakers, hydraulic rams and hoses shall be in good working\"\n    },\n  \
      \  {\n      \"pdf_page\": 46,\n      \"source_line_number\": 21,\n      \"text\": \"                condition\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 46,\n      \"source_line_number\": 22,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 46,\n      \"source_line_number\": 23,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n\
      \      \"pdf_page\": 46,\n      \"source_line_number\": 24,\n      \"text\": \"23.5       All wood working\
      \ machines shall be fitted with suitable guards and devices such as top\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 46,\n      \"source_line_number\": 25,\n      \"text\": \"          guard, riving knife, push stick, guards\

      \   The responsibilities and training of those involved; and\\n                          iv)  How to check\

      \ duration\\n                    period not exceeding 12 hours.\\n\\n\\n          35.5    A copy of each Permit\

      \ proximity to the actual works location to which it applies.\\n\\n\\n          36.0      Traffic Management\\\
      n\\n\\n          36.1    The basic objective of the following guidelines  is to lay down procedures to be\\\
      n                  adopted by contractor to ensure the safe and efficient movement of traffic and also to\\\
      n                  ensure the safety of workmen at construction sites.\\n\\n\\n          36.2       All construction\
      \ workers should be provided with high visibility jackets with reflective\\n                   tapes as most\
      \ of viaduct /tunnelling/station and depot works or either above or under\\n                      right-of-way.\
      \ The conspicuity of workmen at  all times shall be increased so as to\\n                     protect from\
      \ speeding vehicular traffic.\\n\\n\\n          36.3    The guiding principles to be adopted for safety in\
      \ construction zone are to\\n\\n\\n                                 i)    Warn the road user clearly and sufficiently\
      \ in advance.\\n\\n                                    ii)    Provide safe and clearly marked lanes for guiding\
      \ road users.\\n\\n                                     iii)    Provide safe and clearly marked buffer and\
      \ work zones\\n\\n                          iv)   Provide adequate measures that control driver behaviour\
      \ through construction\\n                        zones.\\n\\n\\n          36.4     Legal permission\\n\\n\\\
      n          36.4.1    In  all cases, the contractor shall employ proper precautions. Wherever operations\\\
      n                  undertaken are likely to interfere with public traffic, specific traffic management plans\\\
      n                      shall be drawn up and implemented by the contractor in consultation with the approval\\\
      n                      of local police authorities and/or the concerned metropolitan/civil authorities as\
      \ the\\n                 case may be.\\n\\n\\n          36.4.2   Such  traffic management plans  shall  include\
      \  provision  for  traffic  diversion and\\n                     selection of alternative routes for transport\
      \ of equipment.  If necessary, the contractor\\n                      shall carry out road widening before\
      \ commencement of works to accommodate the\\n                    extra load\\n\\n\\n          36.5    The\
      \ primary traffic control devices used in work zones shall include signs, delineators,\\n                \
      \    barricades, cones, pylons, pavement markings and flashing lights.\\n\\n\\n          36.6    The road\
      \ construction and maintenance signs which fall into the same three major\\n                    categories\
      \ as do other traffic signs, that are Regulatory Signs, Warning Signs and\\n                     Direction\
      \ (or guidelines) Signs shall only be used. The IRC: 67 (Code of Practice for\\n               Road Signs)\
      \ provide a list of traffic signs. The size, colours and placement of sign\\n                      shall confirm\
      \ to IRC: 67.\\n\\n\\n\\n         Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.              September 2026     Section\
      \ Page 53 of 114\",\n  \"text_blocks\": [\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        50.15998077392578,\n        15.768376350402832,\n\

    heading: Clause 42.0 — Medical Facilities
    source_document: Vol3DLNP1.pdf
    source_sha256: 1ee3fc8dfd12f659f34446b0a6db74e1e1cdd4d593ca91b2a48d59bf1ef97d52

    json_path: parsed_json/vol3/part_3_health_welfare/health_welfare_clauses.json
    json_sha256: dc172436c564fe7d5a449f7f786e6863b16e68ebd59020d555002ab6fc04758d
