    quote: "{\n  \"clause_id\": \"42.0\",\n  \"fragments\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 64,\n      \"text\": \"\
      42.0     Medical Facilities\\n\\n\\n42.1     Medical Examination\\n\\n\\n42.1.1   The contractor shall arrange\
      \ a medical examination of all his employees including his\\n          sub-contractor employees employed as\
      \ drivers, operators of  lifting appliances and\\n          transport equipment before employing, after illness\
      \ or injury,  if  it appears that the\\n           illness or injury might have affected his fitness and,\
      \ thereafter, once in every two years\\n        up to the age of 40 and once in a year, thereafter.\\n\\n\\\
      n                i)    The Contractor shall maintain the confidential records of medical examination or\\\
      n                the physician authorized by the Employer.\\n\\n                  ii)   No building or other\
      \ construction worker is charged for the medical examination\\n             and the cost of such examination\
      \ is borne by contractor employing such building\\n               worker.\\n\\n                  iii)   The\
      \ medical examination shall include: -\\n\\n\\n                a)     Full medical and occupational history.\\\
      n\\n                b)     Clinical examination with particular reference to\\n\\n                       \
      \             i)    General Physique;\\n\\n                                       ii)     Vision:- Total visual\
      \ performance using standard orthorator  like\\n                         Titmus  Vision  Tester  should be\
      \  estimated and  suitability  for\\n                         placement  ascertained  in accordance  with\
      \  the  prescribed  job\\n                           standards.\\n\\n                                    \
      \    iii)    Hearing:  - Persons with normal must be able to hear a forced\\n                          whisper\
      \ at twenty-four feet. Persons using hearing aids must be\\n                           able to hear a warning\
      \ shout under noisy working conditions.\\n\\n                             iv)    Breathing:- Peak flow rate\
      \ using standard peak flow meter and the\\n                        average peak flow rate determined out of\
      \ these readings of the test\\n                          performed.  The  results  recorded  at  pre-placement\
      \  medical\\n                          examination could be used as a standard for the same individual at\\\
      n                           the same altitude for reference during subsequent examination.\\n\\n         \
      \               v)   Upper Limbs: - Adequate arm function and grip\\n\\n                             vi) \
      \   Spine: - Adequately flexible for the job concerned.\\n\\n                               vii)   Lower Limbs:\
      \ - Adequate leg and foot concerned.\\n\\n                                 viii)   General: - Mental alertness\
      \ and stability with good eye, hand and\\n                              foot coordination.\\n\\n         \
      \        c)   Any other tests which the examining doctor considers necessary\\n\\n\\n42.1.2.     If the contractor\
      \ fails to get the medical examination conducted as mentioned above,\\n          the employer will have the\
      \ right to get the same conducted by through an agency with\\n           intimation to the contractor and\
      \ deduct the cost and overhead charges.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"text\": \"42.2\
      \     Occupational Health Centre\\n\\n          42.2.1   The contractor shall ensure at a construction site\
      \ an occupational health centre,\\n                 mobile or static is provided and maintained in good order.\
      \ Services and facilities\\n                as per the scale lay down in Schedule X of BOCWR. A construction\
      \ medical\\n                     officer appointed in an occupational health centre, possess the qualification\
      \ as\\n                     laid down in Schedule XI of BOCWR.\\n\\n\\n          42.3    Ambulance van and\
      \ room\\n\\n\\n          42.3.1   The contractor  shall ensure  at a construction  site of a building or other\\\
      n                  construction work that an ambulance van and room are provided at such\\n              \
      \    construction site or an arrangement is made with a nearby hospital for providing\\n                such\
      \ ambulance van for transportation of serious cases of accident or sickness\\n                   of workers\
      \  to  hospital promptly and such ambulance van and room are\\n                 maintained in good repair\
      \ and is equipped with standard facilities specified in\\n                Schedule IV and Schedule V of BOCWR.\\\
      n\\n\\n          42.4      First-aid boxes\\n\\n\\n          42.4.1   The contractor shall ensure at a construction\
      \ site one First-aid box for 100 workers\\n                   provided and maintained for providing First-aid\
      \ to the building workers. Every First-aid\\n                 box  is  distinctly marked  “First-aid” and\
      \  is equipped with the  articles specified  in\\n                 Schedule III of BOCWR.\\n\\n\\n       \
      \   42.5     HIV/ AIDS prevention and control\\n\\n\\n          42.5.1   The contractor shall adopt the Employer’s\
      \ Policy on “HIV  / AIDS Prevention and\\n                    Control for Workmen Engaged by Contractors”\
      \ and the copy of the policy is given in\\n                Appendix No.: 4.\\n\\n          42.5.2   The Employer\
      \ will engage a professional agency for implementing the guidelines laid\\n               down in the policy\
      \ and communicate to the contractor.\\n\\n          42.5.3   The Contractor shall extend necessary support\
      \ to the appointed agency by deputing\\n                    the workmen to attend the awareness creation programmes.\\\
      n\\n          42.5.4   The contractor shall also extend necessary organizational support to the appointed\\\
      n                 agency  for the  effective implementation  of the Employers’ workplace  policy on\\n   \
      \              HIV/AIDS for workmen of the Contractors.\\n\\n          42.5.5   As laid down in the policy\
      \ the contractor shall identify peer educators (1 for every 100\\n                   workers) and refer them\
      \ for professional training to the Employers’ appointed agency\\n                       for the purpose.\\\
      n\\n          42.5.6   The peer educators on completion of the training shall serve as the focal point\\n\
      \                    for any information, education and awareness campaign among the workmen\\n          \
      \       throughout the contract period.\\n\\n          42.5.7   The peer educators will be paid a monthly\
      \ honorarium as fixed by the Employer for\\n                   rendering his services in addition to his regular\
      \ duty.\\n\\n          42.5.8   The total number of peer educators (1 for 100 workers) shall always be maintained\
      \ by\\n                    the contractor.\\n\\n          42.5.9    In case if these peer educators leave\
      \ the contractor by creating vacancy, then\\n                  the contractor at his own expense train the\
      \ new replacement peer educator from\\n                  the Employers’ appointed agency for the purpose.\\\
      n          42.5.10    It is suggested to the contractor that due care should be taken to select the peer\\\
      n                   educators from among the group of workmen so that they remain with the contractor\\n \
      \                  throughout the contract period.\\n\\n          42.6     Prevention of mosquito breeding\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"text\": \"42.6.1   Measures shall be taken to prevent mosquito\
      \ breeding at site. The measures to be\\n         taken shall include:\\n\\n\\n                i)    Empty\
      \ cans, oil drums, packing and other receptacles, which may retain water\\n                  shall be deposited\
      \ at a central collection point and shall be removed from the\\n                   site regularly.\\n\\n \
      \                 ii)      Still waters shall be treated at least once every week with oil in order to prevent\\\
      n              mosquito breeding.\\n\\n                  iii)    Contractor’s equipment and other items on\
      \ the site, which may retain water,\\n                  shall be stored, covered or treated in such a manner\
      \ that water could not be\\n                 retained.\\n\\n             iv)   Water storage tanks shall be\
      \ provided.\\n\\n\\n42.6.2    Posters in both Hindi, English and local language which draw attention to the\
      \ dangers\\n           of permitting mosquito breeding, shall be displayed prominently on the site.\\n\\n\\\
      n42.6.3   The contractor at periodic interval shall arrange to prevent mosquito breeding\\n        by fumigation\
      \  / spraying of insecticides. Most  effective insecticides  shall\\n         include SOLFAC WP 10 or Baytex,\
      \ The Ideal Larvicide etc.\\n\\n\\n42.7     Alcohol and drugs\\n\\n\\n42.7.1   The contractor shall ensure\
      \ at  all times that no employee  is working under the\\n          influence of alcohol / drugs which are\
      \ punishable under Govt. regulations.\\n\\n\\n42.7.2   Smoking  at  public worksites by any employee  is also\
      \ prohibited as per Govt.\\n          regulations.\"\n    }\n  ],\n  \"heading_line\": \"42.0     Medical\
      \ Facilities\",\n  \"retained_lines\": [\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 64,\n      \"source_line_number\": 19,\n\
      \      \"text\": \"42.0     Medical Facilities\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 64,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 20,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 64,\n      \"source_line_number\": 21,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 64,\n      \"source_line_number\": 22,\n      \"\
      text\": \"42.1     Medical Examination\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 64,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 23,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 64,\n      \"source_line_number\": 24,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 64,\n      \"source_line_number\": 25,\n      \"\
      text\": \"42.1.1   The contractor shall arrange a medical examination of all his employees including his\"\

      text\": \"          42.5.1   The contractor shall adopt the Employer’s Policy on “HIV  / AIDS Prevention and\"\

      \          Control for Workmen Engaged by Contractors” and the copy of the policy is given in\"\n    },\n\

      \"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 40,\n      \"text\": \"        \

      : 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 46,\n      \"text\": \"          42.5.4   The contractor shall also extend\

      \  \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 58,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"          42.5.7   The peer educators will be paid a monthly honorarium as fixed by the Employer for\"\

      \ 100 workers) shall always be maintained by\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 62,\n      \"text\": \"                    the contractor.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n \

      source_line_number\": 64,\n      \"text\": \"          42.5.9    In case if these peer educators leave the\
      \ contractor by creating vacancy, then\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 65,\n      \"text\": \"                  the contractor at his own expense train the new replacement peer\

      : \"                  the Employers’ appointed agency for the purpose.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 67,\n      \"text\": \"          42.5.10    It is suggested to the contractor\
      \ that due care should be taken to select the peer\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 68,\n      \"text\": \"                   educators from among the group of workmen so that they remain\
      \ with the contractor\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 69,\n     \
      \ \"text\": \"                   throughout the contract period.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n\

      source_line_number\": 71,\n      \"text\": \"          42.6     Prevention of mosquito breeding\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 72,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n\
      \      \"pdf_page\": 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 73,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"\

      : 65,\n      \"source_line_number\": 75,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n \
      \     \"source_line_number\": 3,\n      \"text\": \"42.6.1   Measures shall be taken to prevent mosquito breeding\
      \ at site. The measures to be\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 4,\n\
      \      \"text\": \"         taken shall include:\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 5,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 6,\n   \
      \   \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 7,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"                i)    Empty cans, oil drums, packing and other receptacles, which may retain water\"\n\
      \    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 8,\n      \"text\": \"            \
      \      shall be deposited at a central collection point and shall be removed from the\"\n    },\n    {\n \
      \     \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 9,\n      \"text\": \"                   site regularly.\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 10,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 11,\n      \"text\": \"                  ii)\
      \      Still waters shall be treated at least once every week with oil in order to prevent\"\n    },\n   \
      \ {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 12,\n      \"text\": \"              mosquito\
      \ breeding.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 13,\n      \"text\":\
      \ \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 14,\n      \"text\": \"    \
      \              iii)    Contractor’s equipment and other items on the site, which may retain water,\"\n   \
      \ },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 15,\n      \"text\": \"              \
      \    shall be stored, covered or treated in such a manner that water could not be\"\n    },\n    {\n     \
      \ \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 16,\n      \"text\": \"                 retained.\"\n \
      \   },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 17,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n\
      \    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 18,\n      \"text\": \"             iv)   Water\
      \ storage tanks shall be provided.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 19,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 20,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 21,\n      \"\
      text\": \"42.6.2    Posters in both Hindi, English and local language which draw attention to the dangers\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 22,\n      \"text\": \"          \
      \ of permitting mosquito breeding, shall be displayed prominently on the site.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"\
      pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 23,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\"\
      : 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 24,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n \
      \     \"source_line_number\": 25,\n      \"text\": \"42.6.3   The contractor at periodic interval shall arrange\
      \ to prevent mosquito breeding\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 26,\n\
      \      \"text\": \"        by fumigation  / spraying of insecticides. Most  effective insecticides  shall\"\
      \n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 27,\n      \"text\": \"         include\
      \ SOLFAC WP 10 or Baytex, The Ideal Larvicide etc.\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 28,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 29,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 30,\n      \"\
      text\": \"42.7     Alcohol and drugs\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\"\
      : 31,\n      \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 32,\n \
      \     \"text\": \"\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 33,\n      \"\
      text\": \"42.7.1   The contractor shall ensure at  all times that no employee  is working under the\"\n  \
      \  },\n    {\n      \"pdf_page\": 66,\n      \"source_line_number\": 34,\n      \"text\": \"          influence\

    basis, with a conspicuously displayed price list.

      pending_manual_review\",\n  \"rotation\": 0,\n  \"text\": \"Safety Health & Environment (SHE) Manual\\n  \
      \                                                        256\\n          shall have the right to provide/maintain\
      \ through renowned external agencies\\n           like “Sulabh” at the cost of the contractor.\\n\\n\\n46.2\
      \     Canteen\\n\\n\\n46.2.1    In every workplace wherein not less than 250 workers are ordinarily employed\\\
      n         the contractor shall provide an adequate canteen conforming to Section 37 of\\n       BOCWA, Rule\
      \ 244 of BOCWR and as stipulated in Rule 247 of BOCWR the\\n        changes for food stuff shall be based\
      \ on ‘no profit no loss’ basis. The price list\\n          of all items shall be conspicuously displayed in\
      \ such canteen.\\n\\n\\n46.3     Serving of tea and snacks at the workplace\\n\\n\\n46.3.1   As per Rule 246\
      \ of BOCWR, at a building or other construction work where a\\n        workplace is situated at a distance\
      \ of more than 200 m from the canteen\\n         provided under Rule 244(1) of BOCWR, the contractor employing\
      \ building works\\n          shall make suitable arrangement for serving tea and light refreshment to such\\\
      n         building works at such place.\\n\\n46.4     Drinking water\\n\\n\\n46.4.1   As per Section 32 of\
      \ BOCWA the contractor shall make in every worksite,\\n          effective arrangements to provide sufficient\
      \ supply of wholesome drinking\\n         water with minimum quantity of 5 litres per workman per day. Quality\
      \ of the\\n         drinking water shall conform to the requirements of national standards on\\n         Public\
      \ Health.\\n\\n\\n46.4.2   While locating these drinking water facility due care shall be taken so that these\
      \ are\\n           easily accessible within a distance of 200m from the place of work for all workers\\n \
      \          at all location of work sites.\\n\\n\\n46.4.3     All such points shall be legible marked “Drinking\
      \ Water” in a language understood by a\\n          majority of the workmen employed in such place and such\
      \ point shall be situated\\n           within six metres of any washing places, urinals or latrines.\\n\\\
      n\\n46.5     Labour Accommodation\\n\\n\\n46.5.1   The contractor shall provide free of charges as near as\
      \ possible, temporary\\n          living accommodation to all workers conforming to provisions of Section\
      \ 34 of\\n       BOCWA. These accommodations shall have cooking place, bathing, washing\\n        and lavatory\
      \ facilities\\n\\n46.6     Creche\\n\\n46.6.1    In every workplace where in more than 50 female workers are\
      \ ordinarily employed,\\n          there shall be provided and maintained a suitable room for use of children\
      \ under age\\n           of 6 yrs, conforming to the provisions of Section 35 of BOCWA.\\n\\n\\n\\n\\n\\nBangalore\

      \  \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157                                        Financial\
      \ Document \\n \\n \\n \\nBangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                            September 2026\
      \ \\nSection Page 3 of 7 \\n \\nPricing Document-Preamble \\n1. The Pricing Document-Bill of Quantities (BOQ)\
      \ & Abstract to Bill of Quantities (BOQ) \\nshall be read in conjunction with the entire Tender and Contract\
      \ Document including \\nInstructions to Tenderers, General Conditions of Contract (including ‘Contract Data’),\
      \ \\nSpecial Conditions of Contract, Notice Inviting Tender, Technical Specifications, \\nEmployer’s Requirements,\
      \ Scope of work,  Schedule, Attachments, Appendix, Annexure, \\nCorrigendum and Addendums. \\n2. The quantities\
      \ given in the Price document - “Bill of Quantities” are estimated \\n(provisional). The basis of payment\
      \ will be the actual quantities of work ordered and \\ncarried out, as measured by the Contractor and verified\
      \ by the Engineer and valued at \\nthe rates in the accepted priced “Price Bid – Summary of Bill of Quantities”.\
      \ \\n3. Tenderer shall quote in the Price Bid (.xls format) sheet only in accordance with the ITT \\nclause\
      \ 10. Tenderer is advised to quote their percentage rate in two decimals for \\nSchedule-A. Tenderer has to\
      \ select option “At Par / Excess / Less” and enter percentage \\nrate in figures on estimated cost to Schedule-A,\
      \ for Schedule-B tenderer is required to \\nselect percentage option as ‘At Par’ only and for Schedule-C tenderer\
      \ shall offer Credit \\nto BMRCL on Salvage of Materials, in ‘Lump sum’ amount. \\n4. The Lump sum amount\
      \ indicated for Schedule C will be recovered from the running \\naccount bills proportionately. In case of\
      \ any variation in Quantities (increase/decrease), \\nthe value of credit to BMRCL will be increased/decreased\
      \ according to the value of work \\ndone from the ‘on account’ bills on pro-rata basis. \\n5. The Contractor\
      \ should note that the credit to BMRCL on salvage materials should be in \\nreference to the quoted price\
      \ and not on the estimate cost put to tender. \\n6. The ‘Tender Price’ or ‘Quoted Price’ includes all applicable\
      \ taxes, duties, cess, royalties, \\nand statutory levies as per the laws in force as on last day of the month\
      \ previous to the \\nmonth of invitation of this tender, except Goods and Services Tax (GST). \\n7. The method\
      \ of measurement of completed work for payment shall be in accordance with \\nthe requirements as stated in\
      \ the Technical Specifications and as given in the Price \\ndocument- Bill of Quantities or elsewhere in the\
      \ tender documents. \\n8. The quoted price is for completed and finished items of work and complete in all\
      \ respects \\ncomplying with the provisions of the Contract. It will be deemed to have included all \\nconstructional\
      \ plant, machinery tools, fuel, oil, consumables, electric power, water, \\ntransportation, disposal of debris\
      \ at BBMP/GBA identified C&D waste management \\nfacility (dismantled/demolished material), all leads & lifts,\
      \ all temporary works & false \\nworks, fencing, watering, lighting, maintenance, night working and inspection\
      \ facilities. \\n9. The quoted price will be deemed to have included all the safety measures at work sites/\
      \ \\ncasting yard, safety measures for workmen & road users, safety of the public and \\nprotection of adjoining\
      \ land & building while working. \\n10. The quoted price will be deemed to have included establishment & overhead\
      \ charges, \\ncontractor’s profit, supervision, other charges together with all general risks, liabilities\
      \ \\nand obligations set out or implied in the contract and including remedy of any defects \\nduring the\
      \ Defect Liability Period.  \\n11. The quoted price will be deemed to have included accommodations for staff\
      \ & workmen, \\nall insurance costs for labour & works, the payment of all wages, salaries, fees if any, \\\
      ncharges payable to various departments for obtaining necessary approvals if any. \\n305\\n\",\n  \"text_blocks\"\

    quote: "{\n  \"page_type\": \"text\",\n  \"pdf_page\": 7,\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\",\n\
      \  \"text\": \" BMRCL/Phase-3/JPN-MRS/Demolition/DLN-P1/2026/157                                        Financial\
      \ Document \\n \\n \\n \\nBangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.                            September 2026\
      \ \\nSection Page 4 of 7 \\n \\n12. The work is to be executed as per Bill of Quantity description, Technical\
      \ Specifications, \\nspecifications of MORTH, KPWD, CPWD, drawings, best engineering practices and/or \\nas\
      \ directed by the Engineer.  \\n13. Removal of hoardings, interior lighting and interior furniture etc., ACP\
      \ cladding and \\nstructural glazing etc., which are existing within the line of cut are incidental to work.\
      \  No \\nseparate payment will be considered for payment. \\n14. If it is necessary to maintain any power,\
      \ water or other utilities during demolition, such \\nlines shall be temporarily relocated, as necessary and\
      \ protected before commencement \\nof demolition work.   \\n15. All such cost involved for temporary relocation/maintenance\
      \ must be borne by contractor \\nand no payment will be made for temporary works. The contractor shall make\
      \ his own \\narrangements for power requirement for his equipment/machinery. If advised by the \\nConsultant/BMRCL,\
      \ the contractor has to give temporary supports for the building during \\ndemolition wherever necessary without\
      \ any extra financial implications to BMRCL. \\n16. If advised by the Consultant/BMRCL, the contractor has\
      \ to give permanent supports like \\nwall, columns (Steel, RCC, etc.), including plastering, painting, plumbing,\
      \ water supply \\nand sanitary works, electrical restoration works, etc. to the supported portion of the \\\
      nbuilding before or after total/partial demolition, wherever necessary to maintain LOC, for \\nwhich payment\
      \ will be made as per  accepted rate in BoQ schedule for items available in \\nCommon SR for the year 2023-24\
      \ (Schedule of Rates),  for the execution of such work.  \\n17. 50% area of Balcony and Canopy if exists will\
      \ be considered for calculating area for \\npayment as per the guide lines for valuation of properties. The\
      \ area of chejjas or such \\nsimilar coverings/shadows will not be considered for payment.  \\n18. The demolition\
      \ and dismantling shall be carried out up to ground and/or up to basement \\nlevel as the case may be including\
      \ underground water storage structures if any. Refilling \\nthe low-level areas up to ground level is within\
      \ the scope of contractor’s work.  \\n19. The items existing excluding trees within the proposed line of cut\
      \ have to be dismantled \\nand demolished and debris to be transported and disposed. \\n20. The total floor\
      \ area means the summation of total built area of all floors in the buildings \\nindicated in Schedule A.\
      \ \\n21. In case some buildings are deleted or added in BOQ, the rates per sqm for that \\ndeletion/addition\
      \ will be same as given above. For payment purpose, the %age quoted \\nand accepted in the Summary Cost will\
      \ be used for the above rate.  \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n306\\n\",\n  \"text_blocks\"\

      \  71.99996948242188,\n        802.54736328125,\n        525.8132934570312,\n        823.079833984375\n  \
      \    ],\n      \"block_number\": 3,\n      \"text\": \"Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.             \
      \               September 2026 \\nSection Page 4 of 7 \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n      \
      \  71.99996948242188,\n        74.76317596435547,\n        526.3867797851562,\n        112.47867584228516\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 4,\n      \"text\": \"12. The work is to be executed as per Bill of Quantity\
      \ description, Technical Specifications, \\nspecifications of MORTH, KPWD, CPWD, drawings, best engineering\
      \ practices and/or \\nas directed by the Engineer.  \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.999267578125,\n\
      \        121.80315399169922,\n        526.3859252929688,\n        159.39866638183594\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 5,\n      \"text\": \"13. Removal of hoardings, interior lighting and interior furniture etc., ACP cladding\
      \ and \\nstructural glazing etc., which are existing within the line of cut are incidental to work.  No \\\
      nseparate payment will be considered for payment. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99853515625,\n\
      \        168.72314453125,\n        526.3866577148438,\n        206.31858825683594\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 6,\n      \"text\": \"14. If it is necessary to maintain any power, water or other utilities during demolition,\
      \ such \\nlines shall be temporarily relocated, as necessary and protected before commencement \\nof demolition\
      \ work.   \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99884033203125,\n        215.64306640625,\n\
      \        526.3867797851562,\n        280.5985412597656\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 7,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"15. All such cost involved for temporary relocation/maintenance must be borne by contractor \\nand no\
      \ payment will be made for temporary works. The contractor shall make his own \\narrangements for power requirement\
      \ for his equipment/machinery. If advised by the \\nConsultant/BMRCL, the contractor has to give temporary\
      \ supports for the building during \\ndemolition wherever necessary without any extra financial implications\
      \ to BMRCL. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99887084960938,\n        289.92303466796875,\n\
      \        526.3902587890625,\n        368.5584716796875\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 8,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"16. If advised by the Consultant/BMRCL, the contractor has to give permanent supports like \\nwall, columns\
      \ (Steel, RCC, etc.), including plastering, painting, plumbing, water supply \\nand sanitary works, electrical\
      \ restoration works, etc. to the supported portion of the \\nbuilding before or after total/partial demolition,\
      \ wherever necessary to maintain LOC, for \\nwhich payment will be made as per  accepted rate in BoQ schedule\
      \ for items available in \\nCommon SR for the year 2023-24 (Schedule of Rates),  for the execution of such\
      \ work.  \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.99984741210938,\n        377.8829345703125,\n\
      \        526.3875732421875,\n        415.4784240722656\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 9,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"17. 50% area of Balcony and Canopy if exists will be considered for calculating area for \\npayment as\
      \ per the guide lines for valuation of properties. The area of chejjas or such \\nsimilar coverings/shadows\
      \ will not be considered for payment.  \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        71.9998779296875,\n\
      \        424.80291748046875,\n        526.3875732421875,\n        462.39837646484375\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 10,\n      \"text\": \"18. The demolition and dismantling shall be carried out up to ground and/or up to\
      \ basement \\nlevel as the case may be including underground water storage structures if any. Refilling \\\
      nthe low-level areas up to ground level is within the scope of contractor’s work.  \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n\
      \      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00090026855469,\n        471.7228698730469,\n        526.3887329101562,\n \
      \       495.75836181640625\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 11,\n      \"text\": \"19. The items existing\
      \ excluding trees within the proposed line of cut have to be dismantled \\nand demolished and debris to be\
      \ transported and disposed. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00192260742188,\n        505.08282470703125,\n\
      \        526.3892211914062,\n        528.9983520507812\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 12,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"20. The total floor area means the summation of total built area of all floors in the buildings \\nindicated\
      \ in Schedule A. \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00222778320312,\n        538.2028198242188,\n\
      \        526.3902587890625,\n        577.5983276367188\n      ],\n      \"block_number\": 13,\n      \"text\"\
      : \"21. In case some buildings are deleted or added in BOQ, the rates per sqm for that \\ndeletion/addition\
      \ will be same as given above. For payment purpose, the %age quoted \\nand accepted in the Summary Cost will\
      \ be used for the above rate.  \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\": [\n        72.00222778320312,\n     \
      \   594.0028076171875,\n        75.06951141357422,\n        768.158203125\n      ],\n      \"block_number\"\
      : 14,\n      \"text\": \" \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n \\n\"\n    },\n    {\n      \"bbox\"\
      : [\n        39.599998474121094,\n        24.479991912841797,\n        63.57600021362305,\n        41.38799285888672\n\
      \      ],\n      \"block_number\": 15,\n      \"text\": \"306\\n\"\n    }\n  ]\n}"

- kind: parameter
  text: Under Corrigendum No. 2 amending GCC Sub-Clause 12.3.1 (iii) c, minor value item quantities operated up
    to and including 200% of the agreement quantity are paid at 100% of the accepted rate.
  evidence_ids:
  - Jdbaceb05b8fa1555
  relation_type: none
  explicitness: not_a_relation
  from_id: ''
  to_id: ''
  id: C04a38791b9306b3f
  citations:
  - id: Jdbaceb05b8fa1555
    okf_path: OKF_bundle/addendum_and_corrigendum/A_and_C2.md
    okf_sha256: cdd5f8641206e173e0b62d494966695a4d54c893ce8b0cd781d5a9c3837ca161
    heading: Addendum and Corrigendum No. 2
    source_document: AandC2_DLN_P1_157.PDF
    source_sha256: db6b30a513be4294cda22c931729ee418297a59dce4459fee508c8061a10d77a
    pdf_pages:
    - 1
    sheet_cells: ''
    json_path: parsed_json/addendum_corrigendum/corrigenda_amendments.json
    json_sha256: 41af12b27292ebbdcf0de3bbd3c72bf1a52ac20ad7977041d1ed344c3240e712
    json_pointer: /amendments/5
    json_record_id: corrigendum_2/gcc_minor_value_item_variation
    source_review_status: pending_manual_review
    quote: "{\n  \"affected_reference\": \"12.3.1, (iii) c\",\n  \"affected_section\": \"GCC\",\n  \"booklet_page_reference\"\
      : \"121 & 122\",\n  \"corrigendum_number\": 2,\n  \"document_date\": \"2026-10-08\",\n  \"existing_selected_text\"\
      : [\n    \"(i) Quantities operated upto and in excess of 100% of the agreement quantity of the concerned minor\
      \ value item, shall be paid at the rate awarded for that item as per accepted rate;\",\n    \"(ii) Quantities\
      \ operated in excess of 100% but upto 200% of the agreement quantity of the concerned minor value item, shall\
      \ be paid at 98% of the rate awarded for that item as per accepted rate;\",\n    \"(iii) Variation in quantities\
      \ of individual minor value item beyond 200% shall be paid at 96% of the rate awarded for that item as per\
      \ accepted rate.\"\n  ],\n  \"field_provenance\": \"Prepared from visual inspection of the source scan; not\
      \ automatically inferred from OCR\",\n  \"ocr_evidence_relative_to_project\": \"output/corrigenda/ocr/run_20261030T051229372457Z/corrigendum_2/ocr.json\"\
      ,\n  \"operation\": \"replace_specified_minor_value_item_passage\",\n  \"pdf_pages\": [\n    1\n  ],\n  \"\
      printed_amendment_serial\": \"1\",\n  \"record_id\": \"corrigendum_2/gcc_minor_value_item_variation\",\n \
      \ \"replacement_rate_bands\": [\n    {\n      \"payment_percentage_of_accepted_rate\": 100,\n      \"quantity_percentage_upper_inclusive\"\
      : 200\n    },\n    {\n      \"payment_percentage_of_accepted_rate\": 98,\n      \"quantity_percentage_lower_exclusive\"\
      : 200,\n      \"quantity_percentage_upper_inclusive\": 300\n    },\n    {\n      \"payment_percentage_of_accepted_rate\"\
      : 96,\n      \"quantity_percentage_lower_exclusive\": 300\n    }\n  ],\n  \"replacement_selected_text\": [\n\
      \    \"(i) Quantities operated upto and including 200% of the agreement quantity of the concerned minor value\
      \ item, shall be paid at the rate awarded for that item as per accepted rate;\",\n    \"(ii) Quantities operated\
      \ in excess of 200% but upto 300% of the agreement quantity of the concerned minor value item, shall be paid\
      \ at 98% of the rate awarded for that item as per accepted rate;\",\n    \"(iii) Variation in quantities of\
      \ individual minor value item beyond 300% shall be paid at 96% of the rate awarded for that item as per accepted\
      \ rate.\"\n  ],\n  \"review_notes\": [\n    \"Preserve the unusual existing wording 'upto and in excess of\
      \ 100%'; do not silently repair it.\",\n    \"The replacement rate bands are derived structured fields.\"\
      ,\n    \"The surrounding unchanged passage remains in the OCR.\"\n  ],\n  \"review_status\": \"pending_manual_review\"\
      ,\n  \"source_document\": \"AandC2_DLN_P1_157.PDF\",\n  \"source_sha256\": \"db6b30a513be4294cda22c931729ee418297a59dce4459fee508c8061a10d77a\"\
      ,\n  \"transcription_scope\": \"Selected amendment wording and fields, not a complete verbatim table transcription\"\
      ,\n  \"unchanged_selected_text\": \"A minor value item for this purpose is defined as an item whose original\
      \ agreement value is less than 1 % of the total original contract value.\"\n}"

# Commercial Entity Index - Corrigendum No. 3 Deadlines

> Draft model-derived knowledge. Citation validation establishes supplied-source provenance, not interpretation correctness.

## Summary

- The estimated cost of work for Contract Package P1 is INR 4.74 Crores.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secA#Key Detail 1 — Estimated Cost of Work|Key Detail 1 — Estimated Cost of Work]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 7; quotation `Sbf2936923c511178`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secA#Key Detail 1 — Estimated Cost of Work|Key Detail 1 — Estimated Cost of Work]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 7; quotation `J5ecd0a6a976ec5b2`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_a_nit/nit_items.json#/key_details/0`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secA#Key Detail 1 — Estimated Cost of Work|Key Detail 1 — Estimated Cost of Work]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 7; quotation `J1e178b1d5564ca2c`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_a_nit/nit_table.json#/rows/0`.

- Canteen foodstuff pricing in workplaces with 250 or more workers must be managed on a no profit no loss basis, with a conspicuously displayed price list.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part3#Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part III: Occupational Health and Welfare|Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part III: Occupational Health and Welfare]]; `Vol3DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 72; quotation `J68c70ff4ff74226a`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol3/part_3_health_welfare/health_welfare_clauses.json#/source_page_context/8`.

- Corrigendum No. 1 updates the timeline for delivering original Tender Security instruments (Bank Guarantee, Insurance Surety Bond, Demand Draft, or Banker's Cheque) to BMRCL.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/addendum_and_corrigendum/A_and_C1#Change 4 — Submission of Original Tender Security Instruments|Change 4 — Submission of Original Tender Security Instruments]]; `DLN_P1_AandC1_157.PDF`; PDF page(s) 1; quotation `J694eb1f7c6c20642`. JSON: `parsed_json/addendum_corrigendum/corrigenda_amendments.json#/amendments/2`.

- Corrigendum No. 3 updates key commercial submission deadlines for original Tender Security instruments and document availability.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/addendum_and_corrigendum/A_and_C3#Change 1 — Tender Document Availability Period|Change 1 — Tender Document Availability Period]]; `AandC3_DLN_P1_157.PDF`; PDF page(s) 1; quotation `J8827536a17c54d1a`. JSON: `parsed_json/addendum_corrigendum/corrigenda_amendments.json#/amendments/16`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/addendum_and_corrigendum/A_and_C3#Change 4 — Submission of Original Tender Security Instruments|Change 4 — Submission of Original Tender Security Instruments]]; `AandC3_DLN_P1_157.PDF`; PDF page(s) 1; quotation `J9e0c4f73f95653c7`. JSON: `parsed_json/addendum_corrigendum/corrigenda_amendments.json#/amendments/15`.

## Key Parameters and Source Statements

- Tender Security (EMD) amount is INR 7.11 Lakhs.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secA#Key Detail 2 — Tender Security (EMD)|Key Detail 2 — Tender Security (EMD)]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 7; quotation `S4d44a7a957170a04`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secA#Key Detail 2 — Tender Security (EMD)|Key Detail 2 — Tender Security (EMD)]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 7; quotation `Jc66c61c80a9fa3ea`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_a_nit/nit_items.json#/key_details/1`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secA#Key Detail 2 — Tender Security (EMD)|Key Detail 2 — Tender Security (EMD)]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 7; quotation `J1edc5235a348b39d`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_a_nit/nit_table.json#/rows/1`.

- Tender Fee inclusive of GST is INR 11,800/- payable online.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secA#Key Detail 3 — Tender Fee|Key Detail 3 — Tender Fee]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 7; quotation `S066ae4b04cfbdeea`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secA#Key Detail 3 — Tender Fee|Key Detail 3 — Tender Fee]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 7; quotation `J67873c515fa1cae7`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_a_nit/nit_items.json#/key_details/2`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secA#Key Detail 3 — Tender Fee|Key Detail 3 — Tender Fee]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 7; quotation `J6b25db952d30aefd`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_a_nit/nit_table.json#/rows/2`.

- Quoted prices must be structured according to Schedule-A (percentage rate over BMRCL estimates), Schedule-B (KPWD schedule of rates at par), and Schedule-C (lump sum credit to BMRCL for salvageable items).
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secB#Clause 10.0 — Tender Price|Clause 10.0 — Tender Price]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 14, 15; quotation `Jfaf820f2f48dbba8`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_b_itt/itt_clauses.json#/clauses/9`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secB#Clause 10.0 — Tender Price|Clause 10.0 — Tender Price]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 14, 15; quotation `S221c78cbe87f11d4`.

- The Quoted Price includes all applicable taxes, duties, cess, royalties, and statutory levies in force as of the last day of the month prior to tender invitation, except for GST, which BMRCL reimburses separately against compliant tax invoices.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secB#Clause 10.0 — Tender Price|Clause 10.0 — Tender Price]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 14, 15; quotation `Jfaf820f2f48dbba8`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_b_itt/itt_clauses.json#/clauses/9`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secB#Clause 10.0 — Tender Price|Clause 10.0 — Tender Price]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 14, 15; quotation `S221c78cbe87f11d4`.

- The Quoted Price (exclusive of GST) remains firm and binding for the entire contract duration, including extensions, and is not subject to variation due to input cost fluctuations or tax law changes.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secB#Clause 10.0 — Tender Price|Clause 10.0 — Tender Price]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 14, 15; quotation `Jfaf820f2f48dbba8`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_b_itt/itt_clauses.json#/clauses/9`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secB#Clause 10.0 — Tender Price|Clause 10.0 — Tender Price]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 14, 15; quotation `S221c78cbe87f11d4`.

- Tender Security (EMD) may be furnished as an irrevocable and unconditional Bank Guarantee (physical or e-BG), an Insurance Surety Bond, a Demand Draft, a Banker's Cheque, or via NEFT/RTGS payment to BMRCL's designated Bank of India account.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secB#Clause 13.0 — Tender Security (EMD)|Clause 13.0 — Tender Security (EMD)]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 15, 16, 17, 18; quotation `J96e5227223f2186d`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_b_itt/itt_clauses.json#/clauses/12`.

- Additional Performance Security is required for abnormally low bids where the quoted price for Schedule A and Schedule B combined is lower by more than 10% of the total estimated cost.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secB#Clause 22.0 — Evaluation of Tender|Clause 22.0 — Evaluation of Tender]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 21, 22, 23; quotation `J82de4565268740e7`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_b_itt/itt_clauses.json#/clauses/21`.

- Successful tenderers must furnish performance security and additional performance security as applicable under GCC Sub-Clause 4.2 within the period specified in the Contract Data, using a bank guarantee or insurance surety bond.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secB#Clause 27.0 — Performance Security|Clause 27.0 — Performance Security]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 24, 25; quotation `J42b19ad1a0ba2e3e`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_b_itt/itt_clauses.json#/clauses/26`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secB#Clause 27.0 — Performance Security|Clause 27.0 — Performance Security]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 24, 25; quotation `S4f9e37b6dc95f6a2`.

- The liquidity requirement for Tender Package P1 is set at not less than ₹ 1.19 Crores, demonstrated through liquid assets and/or credit facilities.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secC#Clause 3.0 — Qualification Criteria|Clause 3.0 — Qualification Criteria]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 32, 33, 34, 35, 36; quotation `J704434795075ef24`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_c_eqc/eqc_clauses.json#/clauses/2`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secC#Eligibility cum Qualification Criteria|Eligibility cum Qualification Criteria]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 34; quotation `Ja40f707b2e28a76e`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_c_eqc/eqc_criteria.json#/criteria/4/requirement_fragments/0`.

- The annual turnover threshold is ₹ 9.48 Crores in civil engineering construction works achieved in at least two of the last five financial years (2021-2022 to 2025-2026).
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secC#Eligibility cum Qualification Criteria|Eligibility cum Qualification Criteria]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 34; quotation `J4e531b0c209bd3b8`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_c_eqc/eqc_criteria.json#/criteria/5/requirement_fragments/0`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secC#Clause 3.0 — Qualification Criteria|Clause 3.0 — Qualification Criteria]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 32, 33, 34, 35, 36; quotation `J704434795075ef24`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_c_eqc/eqc_clauses.json#/clauses/2`.

- The required available bid capacity must be at least ₹ 4.74 Crores.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secC#Clause 3.0 — Qualification Criteria|Clause 3.0 — Qualification Criteria]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 32, 33, 34, 35, 36; quotation `J704434795075ef24`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_c_eqc/eqc_clauses.json#/clauses/2`.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secC#Eligibility cum Qualification Criteria|Eligibility cum Qualification Criteria]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 35; quotation `J9474231ab903fc15`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_c_eqc/eqc_criteria.json#/criteria/6/requirement_fragments/0`.

- Tenderers must demonstrate liquidity of at least ₹ 1.19 Crores via credit lines, letters of credit, or bank certificates uploaded as Attachment-13.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secC#Eligibility cum Qualification Criteria|Eligibility cum Qualification Criteria]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 34; quotation `J2d79831d07f43931`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_c_eqc/eqc_tables.json#/context_fragments/2`.

- Annual turnover requirements mandate a minimum turnover of ₹ 9.48 Crores in at least two of the last five financial years (2021-2022 to 2025-2026) for civil engineering construction works.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secC#Eligibility cum Qualification Criteria|Eligibility cum Qualification Criteria]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 34; quotation `J2d79831d07f43931`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_c_eqc/eqc_tables.json#/context_fragments/2`.

- Available bid capacity must be equal to or exceed ₹ 4.74 Crores, calculated using the formula (1.5 x A x N) - B.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secC#Eligibility cum Qualification Criteria|Eligibility cum Qualification Criteria]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 35; quotation `J6d6e6db9092e1d12`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_c_eqc/eqc_tables.json#/context_fragments/3`.

- Work experience monetary alternatives require either 1 work of ₹ 3.79 Crores, 2 works of ₹ 2.37 Crores each, 3 works of ₹ 1.90 Crores each, or any number of works with a combined value of ₹ 4.74 Crores.

- Liquidated damages for delay are structured around two milestones: Rs. 5,000/- per day for Milestone-1 (50% area handed over in 6 months) and Rs. 10,000/- per day for Milestone-2 (100% area handed over in 12 months), with an upper limit of 5% of the Contract Price.
  - Evidence: [[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secE#General Conditions of Contract and Contract Data|General Conditions of Contract and Contract Data]]; `Vol1DLNP1.pdf`; PDF page(s) 142; quotation `J50cf7f54666ad6c6`. JSON: `parsed_json/vol1/section_e_gcc/contract_data_table.json#/items/8`.

### Jfaf820f2f48dbba8

[[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secB#Clause 10.0 — Tender Price|Clause 10.0 — Tender Price]]

> {
>   "clause_id": "10.0",
>   "fragments": [
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 14,
>       "text": "10.0     TENDER PRICE\n\n         10.1          (i)  The provisions  of the Tender Documents, as specified under Clause 5.1  of the\n                          Instructions to Tenderers (ITT), govern the Quoted Tender Prices.\n\n                      Tenderers shall download the Price Bid file (.xls format) from the eProcurement Portal\n                    and fill in only the coloured (unprotected) cells by entering their name and financial offer.\n                      For Schedule-A, tenderer shall select the applicable option “At Par / Excess / Less” and\n                        enter percentage rate in figures, for Schedule-B - ‘Items under schedule of rates published\n                     by KPWD’, tenderer is required to select percentage option as ‘At Par’ (as the items\n                      operated under this schedule shall be paid as per the rates of KPWD SoR) and for\n                     Schedule-C tenderer shall offer Credit to BMRCL for Salvageable Items, in ‘Lump sum’\n                     amount. The Tenderer’s quoted price shall be (Total Amount of Schedule  - A and\n                     Schedule – B, calculated based on Tenderer’s quote) – (Tenderer’s quoted ‘Lump sum\n                      amount’ for Schedule-C) in Price Bid - Summary of BoQ.\n\n                                  (ii)  After entering the required details, the Tenderer shall validate the Price Bid file (.xls), save\n                                             it without changing the original file name, and upload the validated file under the “Financial\n                      Cover” on the eProcurement Portal.\n\n         10.2          (i)  The Tendered or Quoted Price includes all applicable taxes, duties, cess, royalties, and\n                          statutory levies as per the laws in force as on last day of the month previous to the month\n                           of invitation of this tender, except Goods and Services Tax (GST). BMRCL will reimburse\n                GST to the Contractor based on the output GST levied on works contract services\n                       corresponding to the taxable value of the works executed and duly remitted by the\n                         Contractor. BMRCL will make this reimbursement upon receipt of a valid GST-compliant\n                         tax invoice submitted along with the corresponding Interim Payment Certificates (IPC)\n                       and/ or Final Payment Certificate (FPC)."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 15,
>       "text": "(ii) The Quoted Price (exclusive of GST) remains firm and binding for the entire duration of\n                 the Contract, including any extensions, and does not vary due to input cost fluctuations\n                  or changes in tax laws or tax rates.\n\n  10.3     The Tenderer shall keep the contents of his Tender and the amount quoted by him\n               confidential."
>     }
>   ],
>   "heading_line": "10.0     TENDER PRICE",
>   "review_status": "pending_manual_review"
> }

### J82de4565268740e7

[[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secB#Clause 22.0 — Evaluation of Tender|Clause 22.0 — Evaluation of Tender]]

> {
>   "clause_id": "22.0",
>   "fragments": [
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 21,
>       "text": "22.0     EVALUATION OF TENDER\n\n  22.1      Evaluation of Technical bids\n\n  22.1.1    The Employer will carry out the technical evaluation of Tender submittals/ documents of all\n              online bids accepted by the Employer on the eProcurement Portal to determine that the\n            Tenderer has a full comprehension of the work of the contract. Where a Tenderer’s Technical\n              submittal is found to contain any material deviation or reservation with the requirement of\n             work, the tender may be rejected.\n\n  22.1.2     Evaluation of bids involves verification of payment of requisite Tender Fees.\n\n  22.1.3    The evaluation of the Technical Bid includes verification of the Tender Security to ensure it is\n            adequate and fully compliant with Clause 13.0 of the Instructions to Tenderers (ITT).\n\n  22.1.4     Further Technical bids will be evaluated for Eligibility cum Qualification Criteria (EQC). If the\n            Tenderer fails to meet the requirements of EQC, then evaluation of other technical parameters\n                  will not be done, and financial bids of those Tenderers will not be opened.\n\n  22.1.5     Tenderer’s Financial bid will be opened only if Technical bid meet the requirements of EQC\n           and is found to be substantially responsive to the requirements stipulated in the Tender\n            document. The date and time of opening of financial bid shall be informed online on the\n            eProcurement Portal to the Tenderers who are found to be substantially responsive.\n\n  22.1.6    The offers of Tenderers which have not been found to be substantially responsive to the\n             requirements of the Tender document and which have been determined as being not qualified\n                in the evaluation of technical bid, the financial bid(s) of such Tenderer(s) shall not be opened\n           on eProcurement Portal.\n\n  22.1.7    Tender Securities (EMDs) of nonresponsive bidders (whose financial bids are not opened)\n               shall be returned  after  finalization of technical evaluation and updating the same on\n            eProcurement Portal.\n\n  22.2      Evaluation of Financial bid\n\n  22.2.1    The Financial Bids of all Technically qualified and substantially responsive Tenderers shall\n           be opened online on the eProcurement Portal by the Employer on the due date and time of\n            opening notified under Clause 22.1.5 of ITT. The Financial bids of all such Tenderers shall\n           be downloaded by the Employer from the eProcurement Portal for office records and further\n               financial evaluation.\n\n  22.2.2     Offers, deviations, and other factors that are more than the requirements of the Tender\n           documents or will otherwise result in the accrual of unsolicited benefits to the Employer shall\n             not be considered in the Tender evaluation.\n\n  22.2.3     Evaluation of the Financial bid will be based on the Price Bid summary of BOQ in the Pricing\n           Document, i.e. the Tenderer’s quoted price which shall be (Total Amount of Schedule - A and"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 22,
>       "text": "Schedule – B, calculated based on Tenderer’s quote) – (Tenderer’s quoted ‘Lump sum\n                  amount’ for Schedule-C). Any alteration in Price Bid will not be given any cognizance. The\n                 amount quoted by Tenderers must be found to be reasonable and acceptable.\n\n         22.2.4    The evaluation of Financial Bids by the employer will take into account, in addition to the\n                  Tender amounts, the following factors.\n\n                        (a)   Arithmetical errors corrected by the Employer as per Clause 22.2.5 of Instructions to\n                        Tenderers (ITT).\n\n                        (b)  Such other factors of an administrative nature as the Employer may consider having a\n                              potentially significant impact on contract execution, price and payments, including the\n                              effect of items or unit rates that are unbalanced or unrealistically priced.\n\n         22.2.5     Correction of Errors\n\n                      a)   Bidders are required to select option ‘At Par/ Excess / Less’ and quote percentage rate\n                                in figures for Schedule – A, select option ‘At Par’ for Schedule - B  ‘Items under\n                        schedule of rates published by KPWD’ and shall quote ‘Lump sum’ amount for\n                       Schedule - C.\n\n                      b)    In case the bidder selects the percentage option of ‘At Par’ and also quotes Percentage\n                            rate in figures for Schedule – A, the selected Percentage option ‘At Par’ will only govern\n                      and percentage in figures will be ignored for evaluation of the bids.\n\n                       c)    In case of discrepancy as per 22.2.5(b), the tenderer shall be bound to accept the\n                           evaluation based on ‘At Par’ rate. In case the tenderer does not accept, his bid will be\n                             rejected.\n\n         22.2.6    The effect of the price adjustment provisions (if applicable for this tender) stipulated in the\n                  General Conditions of Contract (GCC), applicable during the execution period of the Contract,\n                       shall not be taken into account for the purpose of bid evaluation.\n\n         22.2.7     Conditional quotes for any or all schedules of Price Bid may lead to rejection of Tender.\n\n         22.2.8      In case of more than one L1 bidder, the tender may be awarded to a tenderer having a higher\n                    Bid Capacity. In case the Bid capacity is also the same, the tenderer having done higher value\n                      of similar work in the last three previous financial years and the current financial year up to\n                        last day of the month previous to the month of invitation of this tender, may be selected for\n                    the award.\n\n         22.3           If the lowest evaluated Quoted Price for Schedule A & Schedule B of BoQ (combined total\n                  amount)  is seriously unbalanced, front-loaded, or abnormally low in the opinion of the\n                   Employer, the Employer may require the Tenderer to produce a detailed price analysis for\n                 any or all schedules of the Price Bid-BoQ to demonstrate the internal consistency of those\n                     prices with the work execution methods proposed. If after evaluating the price analyses, the\n                  Employer determines that the tenderer has substantially failed to demonstrate its capability\n                      to deliver the contract at the offered price, the employer may reject the Tender/bid.\n\n                      In case of bids with abnormally low quote for Schedule A & Schedule B of BoQ (combined\n                         total amount), the Employer may require “Additional ‘Performance Security” over and above\n                    the ‘Performance Security’ outlined in Clause 27.1 of ITT and may be obtained at the expense\n                      of the successful Tenderer to a level sufficient to protect the Employer against financial loss\n                        in the event of default of the successful Tenderer under the contract. If such an additional\n                  Performance Security is required by the Employer, the successful Tenderer shall furnish the\n               same failing which his Tender shall be liable to be rejected.\n\n                      “Additional Performance Security” is calculated as follows:\n\n                                      (i)   If the Bid Price quoted for Schedule A & Schedule B of BoQ (combined total amount)\n                        by the Bidder is lower by more than 10% but up to 20% of the total estimated cost of\n                         Schedule A & Schedule B, then the additional Performance Security shall be\n                             calculated @ 10% of the difference in the (a) Combined total Estimated cost of\n                         Schedule A & Schedule B – 10% of the combined total estimated cost of Schedule A\n                   & Schedule B and (b) the Bid Price quoted by the Bidder for Schedule A & Schedule\n                   B (combined total amount).\n\n                                        (ii)   If the Bid Price quoted for Schedule A & Schedule B of BoQ (combined total amount)\n                        by the Bidder is lower by more than 20% of the combined total estimated cost of"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 23,
>       "text": "Schedule A & Schedule B, then the additional Performance Security shall be\n                      calculated @ 15% of the difference in the (a) Combined total Estimated cost of\n                   Schedule A & Schedule B – 10% of the combined total amount estimated cost of\n                   Schedule A & Schedule B and (b) the bid price quoted by the Bidder for Schedule A\n               & Schedule B.\n\n  22.4       Evaluation for Award of Multiple Contracts:\n\n  22.4.1     Tenderers can participate in any One or Two or Three or All the four Tenders, issued under\n            Tender Notifications mentioned under clause 2.3 of ITT. However, Maximum  of Two\n            Contracts only will be awarded to a tenderer.\n\n                      If a Tenderer intends to participate in more than one Tender and seeking for the award of\n          Two Contracts, the Tenderer must demonstrate having experience and resources to meet the\n            aggregate of the prescribed qualifying criteria of individual Tenders. Tenderer shall submit\n              required documents, certificates and other credentials along with his bid so as to qualify for\n             the award of Two Contracts. Pursuant to Clause 22.1 & 22.2 of ITT, evaluation for award of\n              Multiple Contracts shall be done as under:\n\n                a.  In the first stage, the eligibility cum qualification of the Tenderer shall be evaluated\n                  separately as per ITT Clause 22.1 for each individual Tender for which the Tenderer has\n                  submitted his Bid.\n\n                b.  In the second stage, the Tenderer whose bids have been determined substantially\n                  responsive and meeting the  eligibility cum qualification requirements of individual\n                  tenders in accordance with ITT Clause 22.1, the technical evaluation of such Tenderers\n              who have submitted Annexure-1A, will be carried out for the aggregated requirements\n                (combined requirements) of the following qualification criteria of the respective tenders\n                      for which he has submitted his bids and found substantially responsive:\n\n                             (i) Similar Nature work requirements\n\n                               (ii) Liquidity\n\n                                (iii) Annual Turnover\n\n                         (iv) Bid Capacity\n\n  22.4.2         If a Tenderer is submitting one or more tenders mentioned in ITB Clause 2.3 and seeking\n           award of only One Contract, then he is not required to submit Attachment-1A to Letter of\n             Technical Bid and such bidder will be considered for evaluation as per Clause 22.4.1(a) and\n                  will not be consider for evaluation under 22.4.1(b).\n\n                     If a Tenderer is participating in two or more tenders mentioned in ITB Clause 2.3 and\n            seeking award of Two Contracts, then he must submit Attachment-1A to Letter of\n            Technical Bid along with their bids without fail, otherwise such bidder will not be\n            considered for evaluation as per Clause 22.4.1(b)."
>     }
>   ],
>   "heading_line": "22.0     EVALUATION OF TENDER",
>   "review_status": "pending_manual_review"
> }

### J42b19ad1a0ba2e3e

[[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secB#Clause 27.0 — Performance Security|Clause 27.0 — Performance Security]]

> {

>   "fragments": [
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 24,
>       "text": "27.0    PERFORMANCE SECURITY\n\n         27.1     The successful Tenderer shall furnish to the Employer a Performance Security and Additional\n                  Performance      Security          (if      applicable)     under     Sub-Clause      4.2\n                      of General Conditions of Contract within the period as stated in the Contract Data of General\n                    Conditions of Contract.\n\n         27.2     The successful tenderer shall furnish either (i) a Bank Guarantee (physical or e-BG) or (ii) an\n                   Insurance Surety Bond towards the Performance Security, in the prescribed format provided\n                  under the Contract Forms (Form–2/2A)."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 25,
>       "text": "For issuance of e-Bank Guarantees, duly incorporate the beneficiary details specified under\n            Clause 13.0 of the Instructions to Tenderers (ITT)."
>     }
>   ],
>   "heading_line": "27.0    PERFORMANCE SECURITY",

### J704434795075ef24

[[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secC#Clause 3.0 — Qualification Criteria|Clause 3.0 — Qualification Criteria]]

> {
>   "clause_id": "3.0",
>   "fragments": [
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 32,
>       "text": "3.0 \nQUALIFICATION CRITERIA \n \nPursuant to clause 2.0, tenderers shall meet the qualification criteria stipulated in the succeeding paragraphs in order to qualify. \n3.1 WORK EXPERIENCE & FINANCIAL STANDING: \n3.1. A. WORK EXPERIENCE \nSl. No \nCriteria Requirement \nDocument Submission \nRequirement \n3.1.A.(i) \nTenderer as a Prime Contractor (Single entity or Joint Venture member) during last 07(seven) \nyears ending as on last day of the month previous to the month of invitation of this tender, \nshould have completed/ **substantially completed One work of *Similar nature, where the \nvalue of the work is equal to or exceed ₹ 3.79 Crores.*** \nAttachment-5 & Attachment-\n6 to Letter of Technical Bid \nOR \n3.1.A. (ii) \nTenderer as a Prime Contractor (Single entity or Joint Venture member) during last 07(seven) \nyears ending as on last day of the month previous to the month of invitation of this tender, \nshould have completed/ **substantially completed Two works of *Similar nature, where the \nvalue of each work is equal to or exceed ₹ 2.37 Crores. *** \nAttachment-5 & Attachment-\n6 to Letter of Technical Bid \nOR \n3.1.A. (iii) Tenderer as a Prime Contractor (Single entity or Joint Venture member) during last 07(seven) \nyears ending as on last day of the month previous to the month of invitation of this tender, \nshould have completed/ **substantially completed Three works of *Similar nature, where the \nvalue of each work is equal to or exceed ₹ 1.90 Crores.*** \nAttachment-5 & Attachment-\n6 to Letter of Technical Bid \nOR \n3.1.A. (iv) Tenderer as a Prime Contractor (Single entity or Joint Venture member) during last 07(seven) \nyears ending as on last day of the month previous to the month of invitation of this tender, \nAttachment-5 & Attachment-\n6 to Letter of Technical Bid"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 33,
>       "text": "should have completed/ **substantially completed in any number of works of *Similar nature, \nwhere the combined value of works is equal to or exceed ₹ 4.74 Crores.*** \nNotes: \n1) \n* Similar nature work for this contract shall be, ‘Demolition/Dismantling of Buildings/Structures’. \n2) \n** Similar nature work(s) where Financial value of physically executed work is 90%  or more of the original awarded contract value or \nrevised contract value (if any), duly certified by the client, will be treated as substantially completed Similar nature work(s) and will be \nconsidered for qualification of work experience criteria. In case original contract value has been revised, the revised contract value shall \nbe considered for this purpose.  \n3) \nThe Tenderer shall furnish the Client’s Completion Certificate in support of the Work Experience criteria as specified under Clause 3.1A. \nThe certificate shall clearly indicate the Nature and Scope of Similar Nature Work, Awarded Contract Value (excluding GST), Actual \nCompletion Value (excluding GST), and the Actual Date of Completion. Where applicable, the certificate shall also specify the Revised \nContract Value (excluding GST), along with details of any variation orders and extensions of time granted. \n4) \nIn cases where the Client’s Completion Certificate indicates values inclusive of GST and/or other applicable taxes, the Tenderer shall \nsubmit the actual completion cost after deducting the applicable GST dully certified by Chartered Accountant. For portions of Similar \nNature Works executed in the pre-GST period, the Tenderer shall make a deduction towards taxes at the rate of 12% to arrive at the \ncompletion cost exclusive of taxes. \n5) \nIf a Tenderer has completed /substantially completed the Similar Nature Work(s) as a member of a Joint Venture (JV), the value of \n‘Similar Nature Work(s)’ will be recognized only in proportion to the Tenderer’s stipulated financial share in that JV. A copy of the JV \nAgreement must be submitted with the bid. \n6) \nFor the purpose of evaluation, value of works in foreign currencies shall be converted to equivalent INR as per the following procedure: \nValue of completed/ substantially completed Similar Nature Work(s) in foreign currency shall be converted to Equivalent INR by adopting \nExchange rate between the foreign currency and INR as prevailing on the date of completion / date given in the substantial completion \ncertificate as applicable, as per the website of Reserve Bank of India (RBI). \n7) \n***The value of Completed Similar Nature Work(s) executed during the previous financial years shall be updated at a rate of 5% per \nyear, compounded annually, for the Indian Rupees portion (including foreign currencies, converted to Equivalent INR), to bring them to \nthe price level of the financial year of this Tender Invitation."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 34,
>       "text": "The value of ongoing work(s) that are substantially completed shall not be updated to the price level of the financial year of this Tender \nInvitation. \n8) \nIn case the Similar Nature Work(s) are executed for client other than Government Department, PSU, or other Government Body, Contract \nAgreement, Bill wise details of payment received and Proof of TDS (Form 26AS) deducted by clients for all payments received shall be \nsubmitted duly certified by Chartered Accountant. \n9) \nWork experience certificates of the tenderers with qualification as a sub-contractor will not be considered. \n3.1. B.  FINANCIAL STANDING: The Tenderer should meet the following financial qualification criteria: \nSl. No \nCriteria Requirement \nDocument Submission \nrequirement \n3.1.B (i) \nLiquidity: Tenderer shall demonstrate that it has access to, or possess liquid assets and /or \navailability of credit facilities of not less than ₹ 1.19 Crores (Credit lines/ letter of credit/ certificates \nfrom banks for meeting the fund requirement etc.). \nAttachment-13 to Letter of \nTechnical Bid \n3.1.B (ii) \nAnnual Turnover: Tenderer in its name should have in the last five financial years (2021-2022 \nto 2025-2026) achieved in at least two financial years a minimum financial turnover (in all \nclasses of civil engineering construction works only) ≥ ₹ 9.48 Crores. **** \nAttachment-8 to Letter of \nTechnical Bid \n      Notes: \n1) \nA copy of the Banker’s Certificate issued from a Nationalised / Scheduled Bank in India (for liquidity) needs to be uploaded in the \nTechnical Proposal in respect of EQC Clause 3.1 B (i). \n2) \nAnnual financial statements (Audited Balance sheets including all related notes and income statements) for last five financial years \n(2021-2022 to 2025-2026) shall be submitted by the Tenderer. The financial information of the Tenderer must be certified either by the \nIndependent Financial Auditor (statutory Auditor) of the company appointed under the companies’ Act or by a Chartered Accountant with \nhis stamp and signature with membership number and firm registration number. \n3) \nIn case any discrepancy in data is found between the Audited Balance sheet and the financial information submitted, the data as available \nin the Audited Balance sheet will be considered."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 35,
>       "text": "4) \nIn case Audited Balance sheet of the last financial year ending with 2025-2026 is not made available by the Tenderer, he has to submit \nan affidavit certifying that 'The Balance Sheet has actually not been audited so far'. In such a case the financial data of previous '4' \naudited financial years will be taken into consideration for evaluation. If audited Balance sheet of any year other than the last financial \nyear ending with 2025-2026 is not submitted even when requested for as a clarification under clause 20 of ITT, then the Tender will be \nconsidered as non-responsive. \n5) \nAll documents shall reflect the stand-alone financial status of the Tenderer and not the Tenderer’s parent companies, subsidiaries, or \naffiliates. \n6) \nFor the purpose of evaluation, amounts in foreign currencies shall be converted to equivalent INR as per the following procedure: \nIn cases where the financial data is denominated in a currency other than Indian Rupees, it shall be converted into the equivalent value \nin Indian Rupees by adopting the exchange rate between the foreign currency and INR prevailing on the last day of the respective \nfinancial year, as published on the Reserve Bank of India (RBI) website, for the purpose of bid evaluation. \n7) \nTo align a January–December financial year with the standard April–March fiscal year, use the following conversion: \nA calendar year (e.g., January–December 2023) corresponds to the subsequent fiscal year (e.g., FY 2023–24, covering April 2023 to \nMarch 2024). Apply this rule consistently to all such conversions. \n8) \n****Financial turnover in INR/converted equivalent INR of previous financial years shall be given a weightage of 5% per year \n(compounded annually) to bring them to the price level of the Financial Year of this Tender Invitation. \n9) \nAll the documents or certifications, which are provided by Chartered Accountant after 1st July, 2019, must contain UDIN thereon and \nthe particulars of certifications must be same as mentioned on document/certification and submitted to ICAI on its website which can be \nverified online on https://udin.icai.org/search-udin. \n3.2 BID CAPACITY: \nRequirement \nDocument Submission \nRequirement \nAvailable Bid capacity should be ≥ ₹ 4.74 Crores \nFormula: \nAvailable Bid Capacity = (1.5 × A × N) – B  \nAttachment-7, Attachment-\n8 to Letter of Technical Bid"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 36,
>       "text": "Requirement \nDocument Submission \nRequirement \nWhere: \nA = Maximum value of total Civil Engineering works executed (annual turnover from Civil Engineering works) \nin any one year during the last five financial years. The turnover for previous years shall be updated by 5% \nper year (compounded annually) to bring them to the price level of the Financial Year of this Tender Invitation.  \nN = Number of years prescribed for completion of this tendered work as mentioned in NIT.  \nB = Total value (updated to the price level of the Financial Year of this Tender Invitation) of existing \ncommitments for ongoing Civil Engineering works during the completion period mentioned in NIT, from the \nlast day of the month previous to the month of invitation of this tender. \nNotes: \n1) Works in Hand information provided in ‘Attachment-7 to Letter of Technical Bid’ must be certified either by the Independent Financial \nAuditor (Statutory Auditor) of the company appointed under the companies’ Act or by a Chartered Accountant with his stamp and signature \nwith Membership Number and Firm’s Registration Number. \n2) All the documents or certifications, which are provided by Chartered Accountant after 1st July 2019, must contain UDIN thereon and the \nparticulars of certifications must be same as mentioned on document/certification and submitted to ICAI on its website, which can be \nverified online on https://udin.icai.org/search-udin."
>     }
>   ],
