### Jd8027164bb3d94d7

[[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol1/secD#Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)|Letter of Technical Bid (Including Attachments)]]

> {
>   "pdf_page": 52,
>   "text": "ATTACHMENT -10\n\n                                   (To be submitted by Tenderer)\n\n\n                      MINIMUM PLANTS & EQUIPMENTS FOR THE WORK\n\n                           (TO BE DEPLOYED AS AND WHEN REQUIRED)\n\n\n       Tenderers should have the following plant and machinery either owned or leased or hired by him.\n\n        Sl.\n              Type of equipment required for the work         Proposed to be deployed\n\n\n       1    Diamond  disc  Cutter/floor saw  machine  with               3 Nos\n               suitable capacity\n\n       2    Wall Sawing Machine with suitable capacity                    3 Nos\n\n       3     Electric Drill Motors/ Hammer drilling machine                  4 Nos\n\n\n       4     Floor Saw Machine with suitable capacity                      4 Nos\n\n       5    Chipping Machine                                        12 Nos\n\n       6    Crusher/Combi Crusher                                    2 Nos\n\n       7     Hydraulic Mobile crane                                     2 Nos\n\n       8    High/Long Reach Demolition equipment as per               1 Nos\n              the requirement\n\n       9     Hydraulic rock breaker with loader                            2 Nos\n\n      10  DG set 100kV or higher capacity                             2 Nos\n\n\n  NOTE:\n        1.  Plant and Equipment indicated above is minimum to be deployed at appropriate stage of the work.\n          However, depending on the requirement to complete the work in the stipulated completion period,\n           the tenderer should deploy additional machinery as circumstances warrant at no extra cost.\n        2.  Plant and equipment to be mobilized for the work shall be in good serviceable condition.\n\n\n\n\n\nSignature) ……………………\nName of signatory: ………………………………\nCapacity of signatory: ……………………………\nName and address of Tenderer:……………….."

>       "text": "ATTACHMENT -12\n\n\n      FORM OF BANK GUARANTEE FOR TENDER SECURITY/ EARNEST MONEY DEPOSIT\n\n                       (To be stamped in accordance with the Stamp Act of the Country of Issuing Bank)\n\n     To,\n    Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Limited (BMRCL)\n      3rd Floor BMTC Complex,\n     K.H. Road, Shanthinagar\n     Bengaluru- 560027.\n     Karnataka, INDIA.\n\n1.  WHEREAS ______________ [Name and Address of the Tenderer] (hereinafter referred to as the “Bidder” or\n     “Tenderer”) has  undertaken  for  submission  of  Tender  in  pursuance  of  Tender  Notification  No.\n    _____________ for _________________________ [Name of the Works] (hereinafter referred to as the “Bid”\n     or “Tender”).\n\n2.  AND WHEREAS it has been stipulated in the Tender issued by Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Limited\n    (BMRCL) (hereinafter referred to as the “Employer” or “BMRCL”) that the Tenderer shall furnish to\n    Employer an unconditional and irrevocable Bank Guarantee from an Indian Scheduled Bank (excluding\n     Cooperative Bank) or from a Scheduled Foreign Bank as defined in Section 2(e) of RBI Act 1934 read with\n    second schedule, for the sum specified herein below as Tender Security(EMD) for compliance of the\n     obligations in accordance with the Tender conditions. In pursuance to this, M/s_________ has requested\n      for issuance of the Bank Guarantee for the sum specified herein below as Tender security (EMD).\n\n\n3.  AND WHEREAS we _____________________________ [Name and full address of the Bank including Email\n     address] having registered office at ____________________________ (hereinafter referred to as the\n     “Bank”), which expression shall unless repugnant to the context or the meaning thereof, include  its\n     successors, administrators, executors, assignees, do hereby agree to give the Employer such a Bank\n    Guarantee drawn and payable at Bengaluru ________ branch on behalf of the tenderer.\n\n\n4.  NOW therefore, the Bank hereby affirm that  it is the guarantor and responsible to ‘Employer’ and their\n     successors,  on  behalf   of  the  ‘Tenderer’  up  to  a   total  sum   of  INR/Foreign  Currency/ies\n    _________________________________ [the amount of Guarantee to be specified in figure and words in the\n     respective currency/ies as  specified  in  the Tender document]. Further, the Bank hereby unconditionally,\n     irrevocably and without demur undertake to immediately pay, the Employer upon the Employer’s  first\n     written demand and without cavil, or arguments any sum or sums within the limits of the sum specified in\n      this Bank Guarantee as aforesaid without the Employer being required to prove or to show grounds or\n    reasons for the demand, by depositing/crediting to the Current Account of the Employer, the sums\n     specified herein.\n\n\n5.   This Bank Guarantee is encashable at our designated Branch as mentioned below:\n\n    Name of the Bank, Branch and contact details Address Telephone no: & Fax No: E-mail Address Branch Manager/s\n    name & mobile No: Bank Zonal Office Address, Telephone No, Fax Number, E-mail Address: __________________\n                                   OR\n     This e- Bank Guarantee shall be encashable at the issuing branch of the Bank through NeSL portal.\n6.  The Bank hereby confirms that the Bank is on the SFMS (Structural Finance Messaging System) platform\n    and shall invariably send an advice of the Bank Guarantee to the designated Bank of the Employer as\n     given below:\n\n         Sl. No.  Particulars              Details\n                i      Name of Beneficiary     Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Limited\n                ii      Name of Bank         Bank of India\n                iii       Branch               Cantonment Branch, Bengaluru.\n          iv       Address of Branch       49, Jyothi Mahal,\n                                                St. Mark’s Road, Bangalore - 560001\n       v       Account No.           840420110000760\n          vi      IFSC Code:           BKID0008404"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 55,
>       "text": "7.   This Bank Guarantee shall be encashed at the demand of the Employer on or before the due date.\n\n8.  The Employer shall have full rights to encash this bank guarantee at any time during the validity period and\n     the ‘Tenderer’ shall have no right or claim whatsoever in the matter of encashment of the Bank guarantee\n    amount by the ‘Employer’. The Bank will be fully responsible in terms of the guarantee and obligation, to\n   make immediate payment to ‘Employer’, without the consent of the Tenderer and without referring the\n     matter to the ‘Tenderer’.\n\n9.  The Bank will deliver the money required by the ‘Employer’ immediately on demand without delay without\n     reference to the ‘Tenderer’ and without the necessity of a previous notice or of judicial or administrative\n    procedures and without it being necessary to prove to the Bank the liability or damages resulting from any\n     defects or shortcomings or debts of the ‘Tenderer’.\n\n10.  Courts at Bengaluru, Karnataka, India shall have exclusive jurisdiction for adjudicating the disputes arising\n     out of the encashment of the Guarantee, and, the said Bank undertakes not to revoke this Guarantee\n     during its currency, except with the previous consent of the ‘Employer’ in writing and agree that any change\n      in the constitution of the Bank shall not discharge its liability hereunder.\n\n11.  This Bank Guarantee will not be discharged due to the change in the constitution of the ‘Tenderer’ or\n    change in the constitution of the bank.\n\n12.  This Deed of guarantee is signed and executed by Shri/Smt ______________ and Shri/Smt ______ who\n     is/are authorized by the Bank for issuing the guarantee on  its behalf and their ID No/s. and Email\n    address/es being________ & ________ and ________ & ________ respectively.\n\n13. The Bank’s liability under this Guarantee shall not exceed the amount of ______ [Indian Rupees/Foreign\n     Currency/ies to be specified in words and figures].\n\n14.  This guarantee shall be valid till _________ [Date to be mentioned].\n\n15. The pendency of any dispute or legal proceedings shall not affect this guarantee in any manner.\n\n16.   It is hereby agreed that the liability of the Bank under this guarantee shall cease on whichever of the\n     following events first occurs.\n        a) Payment by the Bank of the guaranteed sum in full to the ‘Employer’,\n                                                 or\n        b) Receipt of written communication from the ‘Employer’ to the Bank along with the   original Bank\n        Guarantee discharging the Bank of  its  liability covered by this Bank Guarantee and in case of\n          electronic Bank Guarantee (e-BG), after release/discharge of e-BG by the Employer through NeSL\n           portal.\n\n                            SIGNATURE AND SEAL OF THE GUARANTOR\n\n                               DATE: ___________________\n                         NAME OF THE BANK: ______________________________\n                            ADDRESS: _______________________________________\nNote:\n1.  The stamp paper of appropriate value shall be purchased in the name of the bank, who issues the “Bank\n    Guarantee”.\n2.  The above format shall be used for e-Bank Guarantee also.\n3.   For issuance of e-BG, the UIN (PAN No.) of BMRCL is AAACB4881D.\n4.  Tender No. as mentioned in relevant para of ITT pertaining to Tender Security shall be the reference to be\n    mentioned in the e-BG."
>     }

>       "text": "11   Incentives for early completion                 NOT APPLICABLE\n         of whole work\n\n      (GCC Sub- Clause 8.10)\n\n\n  12   Defects Liability Period           Defects Liability Period is 6 months from the date of Taking over\n                                     mentioned in Taking Over Certificates being issued for whole of\n      (GCC Sub-Clause 10)\n                                          the work.\n\n\n  14    Price Variation/ Adjustment                   NOT APPLICABLE\n\n      (GCC Sub-Clause 11.1.2)\n\n\n  15    Mobilization Advance                       NOT APPLICABLE\n\n      (GCC Sub-Clause 11.2.1)\n\n\n  16   Advance  Against  Plant &                  NOT APPLICABLE\n       Machinery (GCC Sub-Clause\n        11.2.2)\n\n\n  17    Special/Acceleration                        NOT APPLICABLE\n       Advance (GCC Sub-Clause\n        11.2.3)\n\n\n  18   Retention Money                 Retention Money in each IPC is limited to 10% of Bill amount.\n\n      (GCC Sub-Clause 11.7)        Maximum limit of Retention Money is 5% of Contract Price (as\n                                           stated in Letter of Acceptance).\n\n\n  19   Professional      Indemnity                  NOT APPLICABLE\n        Insurance amount\n\n      (GCC Sub-Clause 15.1)\n\n\n  20   Period in which  all insurances   Within 06 weeks from the date of commencement of works.\n       must  be   submitted  (GCC\n        Clause- 15.7)"

Insurance.\n\n\n    Professional    15.1       The Contractor  shall effect and maintain  Professional  Indemnity  Insurance,\n    Indemnity                     preferably in the name of BMRCL, for the amount in Indian Rupees stipulated in\n    Insurance                     ‘Contract data’ In respect of any design of the Works to be carried out by or on\n                                     behalf of the Contractor.  This insurance, which shall ensure the Contractor’s\n                                                  liability by reason of professional negligence and errors in the design of the Works,\n                                        shall be valid from the date of commencement of Works, until  5 years  after the\n                                  date  of issue  of Performance  Certificate.\n                                         Alternatively, the Contractor shall renew the insurance before the expiry of the\n                                   Yearly Insurance in such a way that the entire validity period is covered.\n                             The Engineer will not issue Final Payment Certificate until the Contractor has\n                                produced evidence that coverage of the Professional Indemnity Insurance has\n                              been provided for the aforesaid period.\n\n     Injury to third    15.2       The contractor shall be liable for and shall indemnify the Employer (principal)\n     party                          against any liability, loss claim or proceedings whatsoever arising under any\n    personnel and                 statute or at common law in respect of personal injury to or the death of any person\n     Property of                 whosoever arising out of or in the course of or caused by the carrying out the\n     third party/ies                 contract works, unless due to any act or neglect of the Employer (principal).\n\n                             The contractor shall be liable for and shall indemnify the Employer(Principal)\n                                    against any expenses, liability, loss, claim or proceedings in respect of any injury"

>       "text": "or damage whatsoever to any property real or personal in so far as such injury or\n                        damage arises out of or in the course of or by reason of the carrying out of the\n                                contract works, and provided always that the same is due to any negligence,\n                             omission or default of the contractor, his servants or agents of any sub-contractor,\n                                  his servant or agent.\n\nInsurance        15.3         Without prejudice to his  liability to indemnify the Employer (Principal), the\nAgainst  Injury                contractor shall take all types of insurance policies such as Contractors All Risks\nto Persons and             and Workmen’s Compensation (Employers Liability) Policy at his cost in the\nProperty.                  JOINT NAMES OF THE EMPLOYER (PRINCIPAL) AND CONTRACTOR AND\n                     SUB CONTRACTOR, IF ANY and maintain such policies throughout the period\n                                   of contract works.\n\n                          Such insurance as are necessary to cover the liability of the contractor or as the\n                            case may be of such Sub-Contractor in respect of personal injuries or death of\n                         workmen engaged by the contractor or sub-contractor, arising out of or in course\n                                   of or caused by carrying out of work.\n\n                         The contractor at his cost shall maintain in the joint names of the Employer\n                                     (principal) and contractor such insurances as may be required in respect of any\n                             expenses, liability, loss, claim or proceedings which the Employer (principal) may\n                                  incur or sustain by reason of injury or damage to property real or personal arising\n                                out of or in the course or by reason of the carrying out the work, and caused\n                               otherwise than by the negligence, omission or default of the contractor, his\n                               servants or agents or any sub-contractor, his servants or agents.\n\n                         Any such insurance policy or policies or receipts effected shall have to be\n                              submitted in original  with the Employer (principal).\n\nInsurance of     15.4       The contractor at his cost shall in the JOINT NAMES OF THE EMPLOYER\nthe contract                (PRINCIPAL) AND CONTRACTOR:\nworks under a\nContractors All                                   i.    Insure the plant, material and works forming part of the contract works\nRisks                                  against loss or damage by all types of risks as envisaged under the\nInsurance                            standard Contractors All Risks policy.\nPolicy.                                                     ii.    obtain the insurance policy for the full replacement cost at site of the\n                                         contract works entrusted to the contractor, sub-contractor.\n                                                                  iii.    Include in the insurance policy the cost of demolition works if any.\n                                               iv.   Ensure that the policy shall remain in force till the entire contract works\n                                                   is completed and the Taking Over Certificate issued.\n\n                         The Contractors All Risks policy which is an All Risks policy with only named\n                                exclusions normally extends cover for loss or damage caused by fire, storm,\n                               tempest, lightning, flood, aircraft or anything dropped there from, aerial objects,\n                                          riot and civil commotion, defective materials, defective workmanship, etc. and\n                                    shall be extended to cover the following:\n\n                                        a.  earthquake risks\n                                        b.  terrorism\n                                          c.  Third Party Liability including Cross Liability to the extent of at least 10%\n                                            of the contract works.\n                                        d.  Debris removal (of insured property) to the extent of at least Rs. 50 lakhs\n                                        e. Defect Liability for a period of 12 months from the date mentioned in the\n                                        taking over certificate for the whole of the works.\n\n Contractor’s    15.5          All the Contractor's Plant, Equipment & Materials shall be brought and kept at the\n plant &                           Facilities Site at the sole cost, risk and expense of the Contractor. The Employer\n machinery.                     shall not be liable for any loss or damage thereto until the completion of work or\n                            removal of materials, plant & machinery, whichever is later. The Contractor, at its\n                               sole cost, shall maintain adequate, appropriate and prudent insurance with\n                              respect  thereto.   Any  insurance  policy  carried  by  the  Contractor,  any\n                              Subcontractor or any third party on the Contractor's Equipment shall provide for a\n                              waiver of the underwriter's right to subrogation against the Employer, the Lenders,\n                                        affiliates, employees, insurers and underwriters. The Contractor shall obtain"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 136,
>       "text": "adequate insurance to cover all equipment, tools and the Constructional Plants\n                                   rented or leased from third parties.\n\n                             The Contractor shall obtain insurance for all plant & equipment and materials\n                                 whether at Site as aforementioned or whether in transit or in storage awaiting\n                                 shipment  to the  Site, including erection/re-erection and testing at  site and\n                                subsequent operation and until their removal from site.\n\n                             The policy shall be on floater basis which provides usage of the plant & machinery\n                                        at different project sites, including Earthquake, Terrorism, underground works,\n                                           third party liability including cross liability for not less than10% of the total value of\n                                      plant & machinery. The policy should also cover clearance and removal of debris\n                                          for not less than 10% of the claim amount, any other relevant add on cover. Plant\n                              and Machinery brought to the site in semi or completely knocked down condition\n                                              is to be insured under the storage cum erection All Risks policy. Once assembled\n                              and put to use and which are in readiness for operation are to be insured under\n                                    the contractor’s plant & machinery insurance policy. The policy shall be obtained\n                                           in the joint name of the Principal i.e. BMRCL and the contractor.\n\n                             The contractor before commencing the contract shall file with BMRCL the list of\n                                    the Plant & machinery that would be in use at the project site, their make, model,\n                                  year of manufacture, current cost of replacement and the insurance shall be\n                                     availed based on the list so submitted.\n    Workmen       15.6       Workman’s compensation policy with the following endorsement no.140 to protect\n     Compensation             Employer (Principal) interest.\n     (Employers                            “It is hereby understood and agreed that in the event of any workman employed\n       Liability)                  by the within named insured or any dependent of such workman bringing or\n      Insurance.                making a claim under Section 12 of the Workmen’s Compensation Act 1923, and\n                                subsequent amendments of the said Act against …………for personal injury or\n                                  disease sustained whilst at work on any contract covered by the terms and\n                                     conditions of the within policy which the Insured may be carrying out for the\n                         said…………….the Company will indemnify the said……….against such claim\n                                   and/or any costs, charges and expenses in respect thereof Provided always that\n                                    the Company shall not be liable hereunder unless the Company have the sole\n                                 conduct and control of all claims covered by this endorsement.  Nothing in this\n                               endorsement  shall be construed as  affecting the Insured’s  title  to recover\n                            damages under any other Section of the said Act.”\n                             The policy shall be:\n                                        a.  In the joint names of the Employer(Principal), contractor and sub-contractor\n                                        b.  for the declared wages and emoluments as per standard terms of the policy.\n                                          c.  cover all the workmen of the contractor and sub-contractor\n                                        d.  in force till the completion of the work\n                                        e.  include cover for medical expenses incurred on account of an accident or\n                                              injury sustained by the workmen in the course of and during the employment\n                                                           f.   include cover for Liability as per Fatal Accidents Act and Common Law.\n\n    General          15.7      The Contractor shall, within the respective periods stated in the Contract Data\n    requirements                 (calculated from the Commencement Date), submit to the Employer:\n     for insurances\n                                         (a) evidence that the insurances described in this Clause have been effected, with\n                              an Insurance Company operating in India.\n                                         (b) Submit the original of the policies with the Employer (Principal) for the\n                                  insurances described as above.\n\n                         When each premium has been paid, the Contractor shall submit copy of receipts\n                                        to the Employer. The Contractor shall also, when providing such evidence, policies\n                              and receipts to the Employer, notify the Engineer of so doing.\n\n                             The Contractor shall effect all insurances for which he is responsible with insurers\n                              and in terms approved by the employer. Each policy insuring against loss or\n                           damage shall provide for payments required to rectify such loss or damage.\n                              Payments received from insurers shall be used for the rectification of such loss or\n                              damage."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 137,
>       "text": "The contractor (and, if appropriate, the employer) shall comply with the conditions\n                                 stipulated in each of the insurance policies. The contractor shall make no material\n                                   alteration to the terms of any insurance without the prior approval of the employer.\n                                                   If an insurer makes (or purports to make) any such alteration, the contractor shall\n                                    notify the employer immediately.\n\n                                                   If the contractor fails to effect and keep in force any of the insurances required\n                            under the contract, or fails to provide satisfactory evidence, policies and receipts\n                                     in accordance with this sub-clause, the employer may, without prejudice to any\n                               other right or remedy, effect insurance for the coverage relevant to such default,\n                          and pay the premiums due. In such cases the premium paid by the employer plus\n                            nominal administrative charge shall be recoverable from the contractor by the\n                             employer, and may be deducted by the employer from any amounts due, or to be\n                       come due, to the contractor or recover the same as debt due from the contractor.\n                         The contractor shall not dispute the amount of premium paid by the employer or\n                               the overhead charges thereon.\n\n                             Nothing in this clause limits the obligations, liabilities or responsibilities of the\n                                contractor or the employer, under the other terms of the contract or otherwise. Any\n                          amount not insured or not recovered from the insurers shall be borne by the\n                                contractor and/or the Employer accordingly, unless otherwise specified in the\n                              Special Conditions of Contract.\n\n                         The Contractor shall submit to the Engineer, the details of all claims made with\n                               the insurer and claims accepted by the insurer or any other details as required by\n                               the Engineer on monthly basis.\n\n                                      All insurances mentioned  shall be obtained with insurers approved by the\n                           Employer (Principal) and the contractor shall submit with the Employer (Principal)\n                               the policy or policies and the receipts in respect of premium paid.\n\n                           Should the contractor make default in insuring or continuing to insure different\n                            from stated aforesaid the employer(principal) may himself insure against any risk\n                                with respect of which the default shall have occurred and deduct the premium from\n                          any amounts due to or to become due to the contractor. The Deductions shall be\n                           as envisaged in of the General Requirement for insurance given above.\n\n                             Provided always that if the contractor shall independently of his obligations under\n                                     this contract maintain a policy of insurance which covers (inter-alia) the said work,\n                                materials and goods against the aforesaid contingencies to the full value thereof\n                             then the maintenance by the contractor of such policy shall  if the employer’s\n                                   (principal)interest  is endorsed thereon, be a discharge  of  the  contractor’s\n                                 obligation to insure in the joint names of the employer(Principal) and the contractor\n                          and the production by the contractor as and when may reasonably be required by\n                               the Employer(Principal) of a current certificate of insurance from the company or\n                                  firm which shall have issued the said policy shall be a discharge of the contractor’s\n                                 obligation to submit with the Employer(Principal) a policy or policies and the\n                                 receipts in respect of premium paid.\n\n                        Upon settlement of any claim by the insurance companies under the insurances\n                                aforesaid, the contractor shall diligently with due diligence shall restore work\n                          damaged, replace or repair unfixed materials or goods which have been destroyed\n                                or injured, remove or dispose of any debris and proceed with the carrying out and\n                             completion of the work. All amounts received from such insurances shall be paid\n                                  to the contractor by instalments under certificates of the Employer issued at the\n                               “Period of interim Certificate”. The contractor shall not be entitled to payment in\n                              respect of restoration of work damaged, replacement and repair of any unfixed\n                                materials or goods and removal and disposal of debris Employer(Principal) other\n                             than amounts received under said insurance."

### J85cb8851adc8cf4a

[[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part2#Clause 23.0 — Construction machinery|Clause 23.0 — Construction machinery]]

> {
>   "clause_id": "23.0",
>   "fragments": [
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 45,
>       "text": "23.0     Construction machinery\n\n\n          23.1     Construction machineries may include dumpers and dump trucks,  lift trucks and\n                     telescopic handlers piling rigs, vibro hammers, rail welding equipments, mobile\n                    elevating work platforms, cranes, tipper lorries, lorry loaders, skip wagons, 360°\n                    excavators, 180° backhoe loaders, crawler tractors, scrapers, graders, loading\n                    shovels,  trenchers,  side  booms,  pavers,  planers,  chippers,  road  rollers,\n                    locomotives, tankers and bowsers, trailers, hydraulic and mechanical breakers\n                       etc.\n\n\n          23.2     Safe worthiness certificate\n\n\n          23.2.1   Every construction equipment shall be in sound mechanical working condition and\n                       certified by  either competent person under Factories Act  or manufacturers’\n                   warranty in case of brand new equipments or authorized persons / firms approved\n                 by Employer before induction to any site.\n\n\n          23.2.2   Every such  certificate  shall have  the  date  of  purchase, main  overhauling\n                  undertaken in the past, any accident to the equipment, visual examination details,\n                          critical components safety check, list of safety devises and its working condition,\n                   manufacturer’s maintenance checklist, past projects wherein the equipments were\n                 used etc as its minimum content.\n\n\n          23.3     Reverse Horns\n\n\n          23.3.1    All Vehicles shall be fitted with audible reverse alarms and maintained in\n               good working condition. Reversing  shall be done only when there  is\n                 adequate rear view visibility or under the directions of a banksman\n\n\n          23.4     General operating procedures\n\n\n                                 i)     Drivers entering site shall be instructed to follow the safe system of work adopted on\n                                site. These shall be verbal instructions or, preferably, written instructions showing\n                          the relevant site rules, the site layout, delivery areas, speed limits, etc.\n\n                                    ii)   No passengers shall be carried, unless specific seating has been provided  in\n                       accordance with the manufacturers’ recommendations.\n\n                                     iii)   Working on gradients beyond any equipments capability shall not be allowed.\n\n                          iv)   Prevention of dumper and dump truck accidents should be managed by providing\n                       wheel stops at a sufficient distance from the edges of excavations, spoil heaps, pits,\n                             etc.\n\n                      v)   The manufacturer’s recommended bucket size must not be exceeded in excavators.\n\n                          vi)      If excavators operating on a gradient which cannot be avoided, it must be ensured\n                            that the working cycle is slowed down, that the bucket is not extended too far in the\n                          downhill direction, and that travel  is undertaken with extreme caution. A large\n                         excavator must never be permitted to travel in a confined area, or around people,\n                          without a banksman to guide the driver, who should have the excavator attachment\n                         close in to the machine, with the bucket just clear of the ground. On wheeled\n                         excavators, it is essential that the tyres are in good condition and correctly inflated.\n                                           If stabilizing devices are  fitted, they should be employed when the machine  is\n                          excavating."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "text": "vii)  When the front shovel of the 1800 backhoe loaders is being employed, the backhoe\n               attachment shall be in its “travel” position, with the safety locking device in place.\n\n               viii)  When operating the backhoe in poor ground conditions, the stabilisers tend to sink\n                  into the surface of the ground, reducing stability. Therefore frequent checks shall be\n           made for the stability of the machine. The loading shovel should always be lowered\n                 to the ground to stabilise the machine when the backhoe is employed.\n\n             ix)   The netting operation of the skip wagons should be carried out prior to lifting the\n                skip to reduce the risks of working on the rear platform\n\n           x)       If a tractor dozer is employed on clearing scrub or felling trees, it shall be provided\n                with adequate driver protection.\n\n             xi)   When two or more scrapers are working on the same job, a minimum distance of at\n                 least 25m shall be kept between them.\n\n              xii)   Incase of hydraulic breakers, hydraulic rams and hoses shall be in good working\n                condition\n\n\n23.5       All wood working machines shall be fitted with suitable guards and devices such as top\n          guard, riving knife, push stick, guards for drive belts and chains, and emergency stop\n          switch easily accessible by the operator.\n\n\n23.6     Penalty\n\n\n23.6.1      If any of the above clauses are not adhered, penalty shall be imposed as per\n         relevant clause depending upon the gravity of the unsafe act and or condition."
>     }

>     {
>       "pdf_page": 45,
>       "source_line_number": 61,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 45,
>       "source_line_number": 62,
>       "text": "                          iv)   Prevention of dumper and dump truck accidents should be managed by providing"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 45,
>       "source_line_number": 63,
>       "text": "                       wheel stops at a sufficient distance from the edges of excavations, spoil heaps, pits,"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 45,
>       "source_line_number": 64,
>       "text": "                             etc."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 45,
>       "source_line_number": 65,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 45,
>       "source_line_number": 66,
>       "text": "                      v)   The manufacturer’s recommended bucket size must not be exceeded in excavators."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 45,
>       "source_line_number": 67,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 45,
>       "source_line_number": 68,
>       "text": "                          vi)      If excavators operating on a gradient which cannot be avoided, it must be ensured"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 45,
>       "source_line_number": 69,
>       "text": "                            that the working cycle is slowed down, that the bucket is not extended too far in the"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 45,
>       "source_line_number": 70,
>       "text": "                          downhill direction, and that travel  is undertaken with extreme caution. A large"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 45,
>       "source_line_number": 71,
>       "text": "                         excavator must never be permitted to travel in a confined area, or around people,"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 45,
>       "source_line_number": 72,
>       "text": "                          without a banksman to guide the driver, who should have the excavator attachment"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 45,
>       "source_line_number": 73,
>       "text": "                         close in to the machine, with the bucket just clear of the ground. On wheeled"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 45,
>       "source_line_number": 74,
>       "text": "                         excavators, it is essential that the tyres are in good condition and correctly inflated."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 45,
>       "source_line_number": 75,
>       "text": "                                           If stabilizing devices are  fitted, they should be employed when the machine  is"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 45,
>       "source_line_number": 76,
>       "text": "                          excavating."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 45,
>       "source_line_number": 77,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 45,
>       "source_line_number": 78,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 45,
>       "source_line_number": 79,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 45,
>       "source_line_number": 80,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 3,
>       "text": "              vii)  When the front shovel of the 1800 backhoe loaders is being employed, the backhoe"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 4,
>       "text": "               attachment shall be in its “travel” position, with the safety locking device in place."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 5,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 6,
>       "text": "               viii)  When operating the backhoe in poor ground conditions, the stabilisers tend to sink"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 7,
>       "text": "                  into the surface of the ground, reducing stability. Therefore frequent checks shall be"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 8,
>       "text": "           made for the stability of the machine. The loading shovel should always be lowered"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 9,
>       "text": "                 to the ground to stabilise the machine when the backhoe is employed."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 10,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 11,
>       "text": "             ix)   The netting operation of the skip wagons should be carried out prior to lifting the"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 12,
>       "text": "                skip to reduce the risks of working on the rear platform"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 13,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 14,
>       "text": "           x)       If a tractor dozer is employed on clearing scrub or felling trees, it shall be provided"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 15,
>       "text": "                with adequate driver protection."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 16,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 17,
>       "text": "             xi)   When two or more scrapers are working on the same job, a minimum distance of at"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 18,
>       "text": "                 least 25m shall be kept between them."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 19,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 20,
>       "text": "              xii)   Incase of hydraulic breakers, hydraulic rams and hoses shall be in good working"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 21,
>       "text": "                condition"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 22,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 23,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 24,
>       "text": "23.5       All wood working machines shall be fitted with suitable guards and devices such as top"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 25,
>       "text": "          guard, riving knife, push stick, guards for drive belts and chains, and emergency stop"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 26,
>       "text": "          switch easily accessible by the operator."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 27,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 28,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 29,
>       "text": "23.6     Penalty"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 30,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 31,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 32,
>       "text": "23.6.1      If any of the above clauses are not adhered, penalty shall be imposed as per"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 46,
>       "source_line_number": 33,
>       "text": "         relevant clause depending upon the gravity of the unsafe act and or condition."
>     },
>     {

### J906a5c528c244560

[[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part2#Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part II: Safety|Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part II: Safety]]

>   "text": "Safety Health & Environment (SHE) Manual\n241\n                          vi)   Working on electrical equipment\n                             vii)  Work with radioactive isotopes.\n                               viii)  Heavy lifting operations and lifting operations closer to live power line\n\n\n          35.3    The permit-to-work system should be fully documented, laying down:\n\n\n                                 i)   How the system works;\n                                    ii)   The jobs it is to be used for;\n                                     iii)   The responsibilities and training of those involved; and\n                          iv)  How to check its operation;\n\n\n          35.4    A Work Permit authorisation form shall be completed with the maximum duration\n                    period not exceeding 12 hours.\n\n\n          35.5    A copy of each Permit To Work shall be displayed, during its validity, in a conspicuous\n                     location in close proximity to the actual works location to which it applies.\n\n\n          36.0      Traffic Management\n\n\n          36.1    The basic objective of the following guidelines  is to lay down procedures to be\n                  adopted by contractor to ensure the safe and efficient movement of traffic and also to\n                  ensure the safety of workmen at construction sites.\n\n\n          36.2       All construction workers should be provided with high visibility jackets with reflective\n                   tapes as most of viaduct /tunnelling/station and depot works or either above or under\n                      right-of-way. The conspicuity of workmen at  all times shall be increased so as to\n                     protect from speeding vehicular traffic.\n\n\n          36.3    The guiding principles to be adopted for safety in construction zone are to\n\n\n                                 i)    Warn the road user clearly and sufficiently in advance.\n\n                                    ii)    Provide safe and clearly marked lanes for guiding road users.\n\n                                     iii)    Provide safe and clearly marked buffer and work zones\n\n                          iv)   Provide adequate measures that control driver behaviour through construction\n                        zones.\n\n\n          36.4     Legal permission\n\n\n          36.4.1    In  all cases, the contractor shall employ proper precautions. Wherever operations\n                  undertaken are likely to interfere with public traffic, specific traffic management plans\n                      shall be drawn up and implemented by the contractor in consultation with the approval\n                      of local police authorities and/or the concerned metropolitan/civil authorities as the\n                 case may be.\n\n\n          36.4.2   Such  traffic management plans  shall  include  provision  for  traffic  diversion and\n                     selection of alternative routes for transport of equipment.  If necessary, the contractor\n                      shall carry out road widening before commencement of works to accommodate the\n                    extra load\n\n\n          36.5    The primary traffic control devices used in work zones shall include signs, delineators,\n                    barricades, cones, pylons, pavement markings and flashing lights.\n\n\n          36.6    The road construction and maintenance signs which fall into the same three major\n                    categories as do other traffic signs, that are Regulatory Signs, Warning Signs and\n                     Direction (or guidelines) Signs shall only be used. The IRC: 67 (Code of Practice for\n               Road Signs) provide a list of traffic signs. The size, colours and placement of sign\n                      shall confirm to IRC: 67.\n\n\n\n         Bangalore Metro Rail Corporation Ltd.              September 2026     Section Page 53 of 114",

### J573bee61f4ed333d

[[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part3#Clause 42.0 — Medical Facilities|Clause 42.0 — Medical Facilities]]

> {
>   "clause_id": "42.0",
>   "fragments": [
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "text": "42.0     Medical Facilities\n\n\n42.1     Medical Examination\n\n\n42.1.1   The contractor shall arrange a medical examination of all his employees including his\n          sub-contractor employees employed as drivers, operators of  lifting appliances and\n          transport equipment before employing, after illness or injury,  if  it appears that the\n           illness or injury might have affected his fitness and, thereafter, once in every two years\n        up to the age of 40 and once in a year, thereafter.\n\n\n                i)    The Contractor shall maintain the confidential records of medical examination or\n                the physician authorized by the Employer.\n\n                  ii)   No building or other construction worker is charged for the medical examination\n             and the cost of such examination is borne by contractor employing such building\n               worker.\n\n                  iii)   The medical examination shall include: -\n\n\n                a)     Full medical and occupational history.\n\n                b)     Clinical examination with particular reference to\n\n                                    i)    General Physique;\n\n                                       ii)     Vision:- Total visual performance using standard orthorator  like\n                         Titmus  Vision  Tester  should be  estimated and  suitability  for\n                         placement  ascertained  in accordance  with  the  prescribed  job\n                           standards.\n\n                                        iii)    Hearing:  - Persons with normal must be able to hear a forced\n                          whisper at twenty-four feet. Persons using hearing aids must be\n                           able to hear a warning shout under noisy working conditions.\n\n                             iv)    Breathing:- Peak flow rate using standard peak flow meter and the\n                        average peak flow rate determined out of these readings of the test\n                          performed.  The  results  recorded  at  pre-placement  medical\n                          examination could be used as a standard for the same individual at\n                           the same altitude for reference during subsequent examination.\n\n                        v)   Upper Limbs: - Adequate arm function and grip\n\n                             vi)    Spine: - Adequately flexible for the job concerned.\n\n                               vii)   Lower Limbs: - Adequate leg and foot concerned.\n\n                                 viii)   General: - Mental alertness and stability with good eye, hand and\n                              foot coordination.\n\n                 c)   Any other tests which the examining doctor considers necessary\n\n\n42.1.2.     If the contractor fails to get the medical examination conducted as mentioned above,\n          the employer will have the right to get the same conducted by through an agency with\n           intimation to the contractor and deduct the cost and overhead charges."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "text": "42.2     Occupational Health Centre\n\n          42.2.1   The contractor shall ensure at a construction site an occupational health centre,\n                 mobile or static is provided and maintained in good order. Services and facilities\n                as per the scale lay down in Schedule X of BOCWR. A construction medical\n                     officer appointed in an occupational health centre, possess the qualification as\n                     laid down in Schedule XI of BOCWR.\n\n\n          42.3    Ambulance van and room\n\n\n          42.3.1   The contractor  shall ensure  at a construction  site of a building or other\n                  construction work that an ambulance van and room are provided at such\n                  construction site or an arrangement is made with a nearby hospital for providing\n                such ambulance van for transportation of serious cases of accident or sickness\n                   of workers  to  hospital promptly and such ambulance van and room are\n                 maintained in good repair and is equipped with standard facilities specified in\n                Schedule IV and Schedule V of BOCWR.\n\n\n          42.4      First-aid boxes\n\n\n          42.4.1   The contractor shall ensure at a construction site one First-aid box for 100 workers\n                   provided and maintained for providing First-aid to the building workers. Every First-aid\n                 box  is  distinctly marked  “First-aid” and  is equipped with the  articles specified  in\n                 Schedule III of BOCWR.\n\n\n          42.5     HIV/ AIDS prevention and control\n\n\n          42.5.1   The contractor shall adopt the Employer’s Policy on “HIV  / AIDS Prevention and\n                    Control for Workmen Engaged by Contractors” and the copy of the policy is given in\n                Appendix No.: 4.\n\n          42.5.2   The Employer will engage a professional agency for implementing the guidelines laid\n               down in the policy and communicate to the contractor.\n\n          42.5.3   The Contractor shall extend necessary support to the appointed agency by deputing\n                    the workmen to attend the awareness creation programmes.\n\n          42.5.4   The contractor shall also extend necessary organizational support to the appointed\n                 agency  for the  effective implementation  of the Employers’ workplace  policy on\n                 HIV/AIDS for workmen of the Contractors.\n\n          42.5.5   As laid down in the policy the contractor shall identify peer educators (1 for every 100\n                   workers) and refer them for professional training to the Employers’ appointed agency\n                       for the purpose.\n\n          42.5.6   The peer educators on completion of the training shall serve as the focal point\n                    for any information, education and awareness campaign among the workmen\n                 throughout the contract period.\n\n          42.5.7   The peer educators will be paid a monthly honorarium as fixed by the Employer for\n                   rendering his services in addition to his regular duty.\n\n          42.5.8   The total number of peer educators (1 for 100 workers) shall always be maintained by\n                    the contractor.\n\n          42.5.9    In case if these peer educators leave the contractor by creating vacancy, then\n                  the contractor at his own expense train the new replacement peer educator from\n                  the Employers’ appointed agency for the purpose.\n          42.5.10    It is suggested to the contractor that due care should be taken to select the peer\n                   educators from among the group of workmen so that they remain with the contractor\n                   throughout the contract period.\n\n          42.6     Prevention of mosquito breeding"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "text": "42.6.1   Measures shall be taken to prevent mosquito breeding at site. The measures to be\n         taken shall include:\n\n\n                i)    Empty cans, oil drums, packing and other receptacles, which may retain water\n                  shall be deposited at a central collection point and shall be removed from the\n                   site regularly.\n\n                  ii)      Still waters shall be treated at least once every week with oil in order to prevent\n              mosquito breeding.\n\n                  iii)    Contractor’s equipment and other items on the site, which may retain water,\n                  shall be stored, covered or treated in such a manner that water could not be\n                 retained.\n\n             iv)   Water storage tanks shall be provided.\n\n\n42.6.2    Posters in both Hindi, English and local language which draw attention to the dangers\n           of permitting mosquito breeding, shall be displayed prominently on the site.\n\n\n42.6.3   The contractor at periodic interval shall arrange to prevent mosquito breeding\n        by fumigation  / spraying of insecticides. Most  effective insecticides  shall\n         include SOLFAC WP 10 or Baytex, The Ideal Larvicide etc.\n\n\n42.7     Alcohol and drugs\n\n\n42.7.1   The contractor shall ensure at  all times that no employee  is working under the\n          influence of alcohol / drugs which are punishable under Govt. regulations.\n\n\n42.7.2   Smoking  at  public worksites by any employee  is also prohibited as per Govt.\n          regulations."
>     }

>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 36,
>       "text": "             and the cost of such examination is borne by contractor employing such building"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 37,
>       "text": "               worker."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 38,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 39,
>       "text": "                  iii)   The medical examination shall include: -"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 40,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 41,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 42,
>       "text": "                a)     Full medical and occupational history."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 43,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 44,
>       "text": "                b)     Clinical examination with particular reference to"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 45,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 46,
>       "text": "                                    i)    General Physique;"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 47,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 48,
>       "text": "                                       ii)     Vision:- Total visual performance using standard orthorator  like"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 49,
>       "text": "                         Titmus  Vision  Tester  should be  estimated and  suitability  for"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 50,
>       "text": "                         placement  ascertained  in accordance  with  the  prescribed  job"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 51,
>       "text": "                           standards."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 52,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 53,
>       "text": "                                        iii)    Hearing:  - Persons with normal must be able to hear a forced"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 54,
>       "text": "                          whisper at twenty-four feet. Persons using hearing aids must be"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 55,
>       "text": "                           able to hear a warning shout under noisy working conditions."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 56,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 57,
>       "text": "                             iv)    Breathing:- Peak flow rate using standard peak flow meter and the"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 58,
>       "text": "                        average peak flow rate determined out of these readings of the test"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 59,
>       "text": "                          performed.  The  results  recorded  at  pre-placement  medical"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 60,
>       "text": "                          examination could be used as a standard for the same individual at"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 61,
>       "text": "                           the same altitude for reference during subsequent examination."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 62,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 63,
>       "text": "                        v)   Upper Limbs: - Adequate arm function and grip"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 64,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 65,
>       "text": "                             vi)    Spine: - Adequately flexible for the job concerned."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 66,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 67,
>       "text": "                               vii)   Lower Limbs: - Adequate leg and foot concerned."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 68,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 69,
>       "text": "                                 viii)   General: - Mental alertness and stability with good eye, hand and"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 70,
>       "text": "                              foot coordination."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 71,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 72,
>       "text": "                 c)   Any other tests which the examining doctor considers necessary"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 73,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 74,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 75,
>       "text": "42.1.2.     If the contractor fails to get the medical examination conducted as mentioned above,"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 76,
>       "text": "          the employer will have the right to get the same conducted by through an agency with"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 77,
>       "text": "           intimation to the contractor and deduct the cost and overhead charges."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 78,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 79,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 80,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 81,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 64,
>       "source_line_number": 82,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 3,
>       "text": "          42.2     Occupational Health Centre"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 4,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 5,
>       "text": "          42.2.1   The contractor shall ensure at a construction site an occupational health centre,"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 6,
>       "text": "                 mobile or static is provided and maintained in good order. Services and facilities"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 7,
>       "text": "                as per the scale lay down in Schedule X of BOCWR. A construction medical"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 8,
>       "text": "                     officer appointed in an occupational health centre, possess the qualification as"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 9,
>       "text": "                     laid down in Schedule XI of BOCWR."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 10,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 11,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 12,
>       "text": "          42.3    Ambulance van and room"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 13,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 14,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 15,
>       "text": "          42.3.1   The contractor  shall ensure  at a construction  site of a building or other"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 16,
>       "text": "                  construction work that an ambulance van and room are provided at such"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 17,
>       "text": "                  construction site or an arrangement is made with a nearby hospital for providing"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 18,
>       "text": "                such ambulance van for transportation of serious cases of accident or sickness"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 19,
>       "text": "                   of workers  to  hospital promptly and such ambulance van and room are"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 20,
>       "text": "                 maintained in good repair and is equipped with standard facilities specified in"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 21,
>       "text": "                Schedule IV and Schedule V of BOCWR."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 22,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 23,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 24,
>       "text": "          42.4      First-aid boxes"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 25,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 26,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 27,
>       "text": "          42.4.1   The contractor shall ensure at a construction site one First-aid box for 100 workers"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 28,
>       "text": "                   provided and maintained for providing First-aid to the building workers. Every First-aid"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 29,
>       "text": "                 box  is  distinctly marked  “First-aid” and  is equipped with the  articles specified  in"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 30,
>       "text": "                 Schedule III of BOCWR."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 31,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 32,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 33,
>       "text": "          42.5     HIV/ AIDS prevention and control"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 34,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 35,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 36,
>       "text": "          42.5.1   The contractor shall adopt the Employer’s Policy on “HIV  / AIDS Prevention and"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 37,
>       "text": "                    Control for Workmen Engaged by Contractors” and the copy of the policy is given in"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 38,
>       "text": "                Appendix No.: 4."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 39,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 40,
>       "text": "          42.5.2   The Employer will engage a professional agency for implementing the guidelines laid"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 41,
>       "text": "               down in the policy and communicate to the contractor."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 42,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 43,
>       "text": "          42.5.3   The Contractor shall extend necessary support to the appointed agency by deputing"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 44,
>       "text": "                    the workmen to attend the awareness creation programmes."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 45,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 46,
>       "text": "          42.5.4   The contractor shall also extend necessary organizational support to the appointed"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 47,
>       "text": "                 agency  for the  effective implementation  of the Employers’ workplace  policy on"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 48,
>       "text": "                 HIV/AIDS for workmen of the Contractors."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 49,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 50,
>       "text": "          42.5.5   As laid down in the policy the contractor shall identify peer educators (1 for every 100"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 51,
>       "text": "                   workers) and refer them for professional training to the Employers’ appointed agency"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 52,
>       "text": "                       for the purpose."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 53,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 54,
>       "text": "          42.5.6   The peer educators on completion of the training shall serve as the focal point"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 55,
>       "text": "                    for any information, education and awareness campaign among the workmen"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 56,
>       "text": "                 throughout the contract period."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 57,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 58,
>       "text": "          42.5.7   The peer educators will be paid a monthly honorarium as fixed by the Employer for"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 59,
>       "text": "                   rendering his services in addition to his regular duty."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 60,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 61,
>       "text": "          42.5.8   The total number of peer educators (1 for 100 workers) shall always be maintained by"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 62,
>       "text": "                    the contractor."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 63,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 64,
>       "text": "          42.5.9    In case if these peer educators leave the contractor by creating vacancy, then"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 65,
>       "text": "                  the contractor at his own expense train the new replacement peer educator from"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 66,
>       "text": "                  the Employers’ appointed agency for the purpose."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 67,
>       "text": "          42.5.10    It is suggested to the contractor that due care should be taken to select the peer"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 68,
>       "text": "                   educators from among the group of workmen so that they remain with the contractor"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 69,
>       "text": "                   throughout the contract period."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 70,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 71,
>       "text": "          42.6     Prevention of mosquito breeding"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 72,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 73,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 74,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 65,
>       "source_line_number": 75,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 3,
>       "text": "42.6.1   Measures shall be taken to prevent mosquito breeding at site. The measures to be"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 4,
>       "text": "         taken shall include:"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 5,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 6,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 7,
>       "text": "                i)    Empty cans, oil drums, packing and other receptacles, which may retain water"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 8,
>       "text": "                  shall be deposited at a central collection point and shall be removed from the"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 9,
>       "text": "                   site regularly."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 10,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 11,
>       "text": "                  ii)      Still waters shall be treated at least once every week with oil in order to prevent"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 12,
>       "text": "              mosquito breeding."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 13,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 14,
>       "text": "                  iii)    Contractor’s equipment and other items on the site, which may retain water,"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 15,
>       "text": "                  shall be stored, covered or treated in such a manner that water could not be"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 16,
>       "text": "                 retained."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 17,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 18,
>       "text": "             iv)   Water storage tanks shall be provided."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 19,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 20,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 21,
>       "text": "42.6.2    Posters in both Hindi, English and local language which draw attention to the dangers"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 22,
>       "text": "           of permitting mosquito breeding, shall be displayed prominently on the site."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 23,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 24,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 25,
>       "text": "42.6.3   The contractor at periodic interval shall arrange to prevent mosquito breeding"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 26,
>       "text": "        by fumigation  / spraying of insecticides. Most  effective insecticides  shall"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 27,
>       "text": "         include SOLFAC WP 10 or Baytex, The Ideal Larvicide etc."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 28,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 29,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 30,
>       "text": "42.7     Alcohol and drugs"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 31,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 32,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 33,
>       "text": "42.7.1   The contractor shall ensure at  all times that no employee  is working under the"
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 34,
>       "text": "          influence of alcohol / drugs which are punishable under Govt. regulations."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 35,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 36,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 37,
>       "text": "42.7.2   Smoking  at  public worksites by any employee  is also prohibited as per Govt."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 38,
>       "text": "          regulations."
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 39,
>       "text": ""
>     },
>     {
>       "pdf_page": 66,
>       "source_line_number": 40,
>       "text": ""
>     }
>   ],
>   "review_status": "pending_manual_review",
>   "title": "Medical Facilities"

### J68c70ff4ff74226a

[[OKF_bundle/Technical/Vol3/vol3_part3#Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part III: Occupational Health and Welfare|Safety, Health and Environment Manual – Part III: Occupational Health and Welfare]]

> {
